Implement the following plan:

# Use ComputeTranscriptPath in Log Restoration

## Context

`RestoreLogsOnly` (manual_commit_rewind.go:619-740) and `resumeSingleSession` (resume.go:484-572) hardcode Claude-specific paths when restoring session transcripts from checkpoints. This breaks restoration for Gemini sessions (and any future agents).

**The problems:**
1. `RestoreLogsOnly` uses `paths.GetClaudeProjectDir(repoRoot)` — only works for Claude
2. Both `RestoreLogsOnly` and `resumeSingleSession` hardcode `.jsonl` extension (Gemini uses `.json`)
3. `classifySessionsForRestore` has the same hardcoded Claude paths

**The fix:** Use the existing `ComputeTranscriptPath` method (already on the `Agent` interface from our previous work) to compute the correct path for each session during restoration.

**Prerequisite:** Gemini's `ComputeTranscriptPath` requires `startTime` (session start time) to build its filename (`session-<date>-<id>.json`), but `CommittedMetadata` (checkpoint metadata stored in git) only has `CreatedAt` (checkpoint creation time). We need to add `StartedAt` to the committed metadata.

## Changes

### 1. Add `StartedAt` to checkpoint metadata

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/checkpoint.go`

Add to `CommittedMetadata` (after line 335):
```go
StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at,omitempty"`
```

Add to `WriteCommittedOptions` (after `Agent` field, around line 256):
```go
StartedAt time.Time
```

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/checkpoint/committed.go` (line 333-348)

Add to the `CommittedMetadata` struct literal where it's created from opts:
```go
StartedAt: opts.StartedAt,
```

### 2. Populate `StartedAt` during condensation and checkpoint writes

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go` (line 177-195)

Add `StartedAt: state.StartedAt` to `WriteCommittedOptions`:
```go
StartedAt: state.StartedAt,
```

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/auto_commit.go`

Add `StartedAt` to both `WriteCommittedOptions` call sites:
- Line 253: Regular checkpoint write — get `StartedAt` from session state
- Line 642: Task checkpoint write — same

### 3. Update `RestoreLogsOnly` to use `ComputeTranscriptPath`

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` (lines 619-740)

Replace the Claude-specific path computation:
```go
// REMOVE these lines (648-662):
claudeProjectDir, err := paths.GetClaudeProjectDir(repoRoot)
// ...
os.MkdirAll(claudeProjectDir, 0o750)

// REMOVE in the loop (710-711):
modelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)
claudeSessionFile := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+".jsonl")
```

Replace with agent-aware path computation per session:
```go
// In the loop, after getting sessionID:
agentType := content.Metadata.Agent
ag, agErr := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
if agErr != nil {
    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: unknown agent type %q for session %d, skipping\n", agentType, i)
    continue
}

agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
startedAt := content.Metadata.StartedAt
if startedAt.IsZero() {
    startedAt = content.Metadata.CreatedAt // backward compat
}
transcriptPath, pathErr := ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, agentSessionID, startedAt)
if pathErr != nil {
    fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: failed to compute transcript path for session %d: %v\n", i, pathErr)
    continue
}

// Ensure parent directory exists
if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), 0o750); err != nil {
    // ...
}

// Write transcript
if writeErr := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
    // ...
}
```

### 4. Update `classifySessionsForRestore` similarly

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go` (lines 792-825)

Change the function signature to remove `claudeProjectDir` parameter, add `repoRoot`:
```go
func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, store cpkg.Store, ...) []SessionRestoreInfo
```

Inside the loop, replace:
```go
modelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)
localPath := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+".jsonl")
```

With:
```go
agentType := content.Metadata.Agent
ag, agErr := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)
if agErr != nil {
    continue
}
agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
startedAt := content.Metadata.StartedAt
if startedAt.IsZero() {
    startedAt = content.Metadata.CreatedAt
}
localPath, pathErr := ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, agentSessionID, startedAt)
if pathErr != nil {
    continue
}
```

Update the caller in `RestoreLogsOnly` (line 666) to pass `repoRoot` instead of `claudeProjectDir`.

### 5. Update `resumeSingleSession` to use `ComputeTranscriptPath`

**File:** `cmd/entire/cli/resume.go` (line 486)

Replace:
```go
sessionLogPath := filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
```

With computing the path via `ComputeTranscriptPath`. We need the `startTime` — get it from the checkpoint metadata via `checkpoint.LookupSessionLog` or just use `time.Time{}` as zero (Claude ignores startTime, and for Gemini we'd need the actual metadata).

Actually, `resumeSingleSession` already uses `ag.WriteSession()` which takes `SessionRef`. The path just needs to be correct. Since this is the fallback path when `RestoreLogsOnly` fails, and we don't have metadata with `StartedAt` here, the simplest fix is to use `ag.GetSessionDir()` + the agent-appropriate extension. But we'd need to know the extension.

Better approach: use `ComputeTranscriptPath` with zero time as fallback. For Claude, time is ignored. For Gemini, a zero time would produce a usable (if imperfect) path. This is only the fallback path.

```go
transcriptPath, err := ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, agentSessionID, time.Time{})
if err != nil {
    // Fall back to old behavior
    transcriptPath = filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
}
sessionLogPath := transcriptPath
```

### 6. Remove now-unused `paths.GetClaudeProjectDir` references from rewind

After these changes, `paths.GetClaudeProjectDir` is no longer called from `RestoreLogsOnly` or `classifySessionsForRestore`. The function itself stays (used elsewhere), but the rewind code no longer depends on it.

    29→// Checkpoint represents a save point within a session.

    46→
    47→// Type indicates the storage location and lifecycle of a checkpoint.
    48→type Type int
    49→
    50→const (
    51→	// Temporary checkpoints contain full state (code + metadata) and are stored
    52→	// on shadow branches (entire/<commit-hash>). Used for intra-session rewind.
    53→	Temporary Type = iota
    54→
    55→	// Committed checkpoints contain metadata + commit reference and are stored
    56→	// on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch. They are the permanent record.
    57→	Committed
    58→)
    59→
    60→// Store provides low-level primitives for reading and writing checkpoints.
    61→// This is used by strategies to implement their storage approach.
    62→//
    63→// The interface matches the GitStore implementation signatures directly:
    64→// - WriteTemporary takes WriteTemporaryOptions and returns a result with commit hash and skip status
    65→// - ReadTemporary takes baseCommit (not sessionID) since shadow branches are keyed by commit
    66→// - List methods return implementation-specific info types for richer data
    67→type Store interface {
    68→	// WriteTemporary writes a temporary checkpoint (full state) to a shadow branch.
    69→	// Shadow branches are named entire/<base-commit-short-hash>.
    70→	// Returns a result containing the commit hash and whether the checkpoint was skipped.
    71→	// Checkpoints are skipped (deduplicated) when the tree hash matches the previous checkpoint.
    72→	WriteTemporary(ctx context.Context, opts WriteTemporaryOptions) (WriteTemporaryResult, error)
    73→
    74→	// ReadTemporary reads the latest checkpoint from a shadow branch.
    75→	// baseCommit is the commit hash the session is based on.
    76→	// worktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree).
    77→	// Returns nil, nil if the shadow branch doesn't exist.
    78→	ReadTemporary(ctx context.Context, baseCommit, worktreeID string) (*ReadTemporaryResult, error)
    79→
    80→	// ListTemporary lists all shadow branches with their checkpoint info.
    81→	ListTemporary(ctx context.Context) ([]TemporaryInfo, error)
    82→
    83→	// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    84→	// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    85→	WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error
    86→
    87→	// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID.
    88→	// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
    89→	// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
    90→	// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint does not exist.
    91→	ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error)
    92→
    93→	// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
    94→	// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
    95→	// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
    96→	ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error)
    97→
    98→	// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
    99→	// Useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   100→	ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error)
   101→
   102→	// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints.
   103→	ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error)
   104→}

   364→// GetTranscriptStart returns the transcript line offset at which this checkpoint's data begins.
   365→// Returns 0 for new checkpoints (start from beginning). For data written by older CLI versions,
   366→// falls back to the deprecated TranscriptLinesAtStart field.
   367→func (m CommittedMetadata) GetTranscriptStart() int {
   368→	if m.CheckpointTranscriptStart > 0 {
   369→		return m.CheckpointTranscriptStart
   370→	}
   371→	return m.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   372→}

   386→// It contains aggregated statistics from all sessions and a map of session IDs

   392→//	<checkpoint-id[:2]>/<checkpoint-id[2:]>/

   404→type CheckpointSummary struct {
   405→	CLIVersion       string             `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
   406→	CheckpointID     id.CheckpointID    `json:"checkpoint_id"`
   407→	Strategy         string             `json:"strategy"`
   408→	Branch           string             `json:"branch,omitempty"`
   409→	CheckpointsCount int                `json:"checkpoints_count"`
   410→	FilesTouched     []string           `json:"files_touched"`
   411→	Sessions         []SessionFilePaths `json:"sessions"`
   412→	TokenUsage       *agent.TokenUsage  `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
   413→}
   414→
   415→// Summary contains AI-generated summary of a checkpoint.
   416→type Summary struct {
   417→	Intent    string           `json:"intent"`     // What user wanted to accomplish
   418→	Outcome   string           `json:"outcome"`    // What was achieved
   419→	Learnings LearningsSummary `json:"learnings"`  // Categorized learnings
   420→	Friction  []string         `json:"friction"`   // Problems/annoyances encountered
   421→	OpenItems []string         `json:"open_items"` // Tech debt, unfinished work
   422→}
   423→
   424→// LearningsSummary contains learnings grouped by scope.
   425→type LearningsSummary struct {
   426→	Repo     []string       `json:"repo"`     // Codebase-specific patterns/conventions
   427→	Code     []CodeLearning `json:"code"`     // File/module specific findings
   428→	Workflow []string       `json:"workflow"` // General dev practices
   429→}
   430→
   431→// CodeLearning captures a learning tied to a specific code location.
   432→type CodeLearning struct {
   433→	Path    string `json:"path"`               // File path
   434→	Line    int    `json:"line,omitempty"`     // Start line number
   435→	EndLine int    `json:"end_line,omitempty"` // End line for ranges (optional)
   436→	Finding string `json:"finding"`            // What was learned
   437→}
   438→
   439→// InitialAttribution captures line-level attribution metrics at commit time.
   440→// This is a point-in-time snapshot comparing the checkpoint tree (agent work)
   441→// against the committed tree (may include human edits).
   442→//
   443→// Attribution Metrics:
   444→//   - TotalCommitted measures "net additions" (lines added that remain in the commit)
   445→//   - AgentPercentage represents "of the new code added, what percentage came from the agent"
   446→//   - Deletion work is tracked separately in HumanRemoved (not included in percentage)
   447→//
   448→// Deletion-Only Commits:
   449→// For commits with only deletions (no additions), TotalCommitted will be 0 and
   450→// AgentPercentage will be 0. This is by design - the percentage metric is only
   451→// meaningful for commits that add code. Deletion contributions are captured in
   452→// the HumanRemoved field but don't affect the attribution percentage.
   453→type InitialAttribution struct {
   454→	CalculatedAt    time.Time `json:"calculated_at"`
   455→	AgentLines      int       `json:"agent_lines"`      // Lines added by agent (base → shadow diff)
   456→	HumanAdded      int       `json:"human_added"`      // Lines added by human (excluding modifications)
   457→	HumanModified   int       `json:"human_modified"`   // Lines modified by human (estimate: min(added, removed))
   458→	HumanRemoved    int       `json:"human_removed"`    // Lines removed by human (excluding modifications)
   459→	TotalCommitted  int       `json:"total_committed"`  // Net additions in commit (agent + human new lines, not total file size)
   460→	AgentPercentage float64   `json:"agent_percentage"` // agent_lines / total_committed * 100 (0 for deletion-only commits)
   461→}
   462→
   463→// Info provides summary information for listing checkpoints.
   464→// This is the generic checkpoint info type.
   465→type Info struct {
   466→	// ID is the checkpoint identifier
   467→	ID string
   468→
   469→	// SessionID identifies the session
   470→	SessionID string
   471→
   472→	// Type indicates temporary or committed
   473→	Type Type
   474→
   475→	// CreatedAt is when the checkpoint was created
   476→	CreatedAt time.Time
   477→
   478→	// Message is a summary description
   479→	Message string
   480→}
   481→
   482→// WriteTemporaryTaskOptions contains options for writing a task checkpoint.
   483→// Task checkpoints are created when a subagent completes and contain both
   484→// code changes and task-specific metadata.
   485→type WriteTemporaryTaskOptions struct {
   486→	// SessionID is the session identifier
   487→	SessionID string
   488→
   489→	// BaseCommit is the commit hash this session is based on
   490→	BaseCommit string
   491→
   492→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
   493→	// Used to create worktree-specific shadow branch names
   494→	WorktreeID string
   495→
   496→	// ToolUseID is the unique identifier for this Task tool invocation
   497→	ToolUseID string
   498→
   499→	// AgentID is the subagent identifier
   500→	AgentID string
   501→
   502→	// ModifiedFiles are files that have been modified (relative paths)
   503→	ModifiedFiles []string
   504→
   505→	// NewFiles are files that have been created (relative paths)
   506→	NewFiles []string
   507→
   508→	// DeletedFiles are files that have been deleted (relative paths)
   509→	DeletedFiles []string
   510→
   511→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the main session transcript
   512→	TranscriptPath string
   513→
   514→	// SubagentTranscriptPath is the path to the subagent's transcript
   515→	SubagentTranscriptPath string
   516→
   517→	// CheckpointUUID is the UUID for transcript truncation when rewinding
   518→	CheckpointUUID string
   519→
   520→	// CommitMessage is the commit message (already formatted)
   521→	CommitMessage string
   522→
   523→	// AuthorName is the name to use for commits
   524→	AuthorName string
   525→
   526→	// AuthorEmail is the email to use for commits
   527→	AuthorEmail string
   528→
   529→	// IsIncremental indicates this is an incremental checkpoint
   530→	IsIncremental bool
   531→
   532→	// IncrementalSequence is the checkpoint sequence number
   533→	IncrementalSequence int
   534→
   535→	// IncrementalType is the tool that triggered this checkpoint
   536→	IncrementalType string
   537→
   538→	// IncrementalData is the tool_input payload for this checkpoint
   539→	IncrementalData []byte
   540→}

    34→
    35→// errStopIteration is used to stop commit iteration early in GetCheckpointAuthor.
    36→var errStopIteration = errors.New("stop iteration")
    37→
    38→// WriteCommitted writes a committed checkpoint to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
    39→// Checkpoints are stored at sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    40→//
    41→// For task checkpoints (IsTask=true), additional files are written under tasks/<tool-use-id>/:
    42→//   - For incremental checkpoints: checkpoints/NNN-<tool-use-id>.json
    43→//   - For final checkpoints: checkpoint.json and agent-<agent-id>.jsonl
    44→func (s *GitStore) WriteCommitted(ctx context.Context, opts WriteCommittedOptions) error {
    45→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
    46→
    47→	// Validate identifiers to prevent path traversal and malformed data
    48→	if opts.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
    49→		return errors.New("invalid checkpoint options: checkpoint ID is required")
    50→	}
    51→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(opts.SessionID); err != nil {
    52→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	if err := validation.ValidateToolUseID(opts.ToolUseID); err != nil {
    55→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    56→	}
    57→	if err := validation.ValidateAgentID(opts.AgentID); err != nil {
    58→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid checkpoint options: %w", err)
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
    62→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
    63→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
    64→	}
    65→
    66→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
    67→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
    68→	if err != nil {
    69→		return err
    70→	}
    71→
    72→	// Use sharded path: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/
    73→	basePath := opts.CheckpointID.Path() + "/"
    74→
    75→	// Track task metadata path for commit trailer
    76→	var taskMetadataPath string
    77→
    78→	// Handle task checkpoints
    79→	if opts.IsTask && opts.ToolUseID != "" {
    80→		taskMetadataPath, err = s.writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries)
    81→		if err != nil {
    82→			return err
    83→		}
    84→	}
    85→
    86→	// Write standard checkpoint entries (transcript, prompts, context, metadata)
    87→	if err := s.writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts, basePath, entries); err != nil {
    88→		return err
    89→	}
    90→
    91→	// Build and commit
    92→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return err
    95→	}
    96→
    97→	commitMsg := s.buildCommitMessage(opts, taskMetadataPath)
    98→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, opts.AuthorName, opts.AuthorEmail)
    99→	if err != nil {
   100→		return err
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   104→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
   105→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
   106→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
   107→	}
   108→
   109→	return nil
   110→}
   111→
   112→// getSessionsBranchEntries returns the sessions branch reference and flattened tree entries.
   113→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchEntries() (*plumbing.Reference, map[string]object.TreeEntry, error) {
   114→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   115→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
   116→	if err != nil {
   117→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get sessions branch reference: %w", err)
   118→	}
   119→
   120→	parentCommit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   121→	if err != nil {
   122→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   123→	}
   124→
   125→	baseTree, err := parentCommit.Tree()
   126→	if err != nil {
   127→		return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   128→	}
   129→
   130→	entries := make(map[string]object.TreeEntry)
   131→	if err := FlattenTree(s.repo, baseTree, "", entries); err != nil {
   132→		return nil, nil, err
   133→	}
   134→
   135→	return ref, entries, nil
   136→}
   137→
   138→// writeTaskCheckpointEntries writes task-specific checkpoint entries and returns the task metadata path.
   139→func (s *GitStore) writeTaskCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   140→	taskPath := basePath + "tasks/" + opts.ToolUseID + "/"
   141→
   142→	if opts.IsIncremental {
   143→		return s.writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   144→	}
   145→	return s.writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts, taskPath, entries)
   146→}
   147→
   148→// writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint writes an incremental checkpoint file during task execution.
   149→func (s *GitStore) writeIncrementalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   150→	incData, err := redact.JSONLBytes(opts.IncrementalData)
   151→	if err != nil {
   152→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to redact incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   153→	}
   154→	checkpoint := incrementalCheckpointData{
   155→		Type:      opts.IncrementalType,
   156→		ToolUseID: opts.ToolUseID,
   157→		Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
   158→		Data:      incData,
   159→	}
   160→	cpData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal incremental checkpoint: %w", err)
   163→	}
   164→	cpBlobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, cpData)
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create incremental checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	cpFilename := fmt.Sprintf("%03d-%s.json", opts.IncrementalSequence, opts.ToolUseID)
   170→	cpPath := taskPath + "checkpoints/" + cpFilename
   171→	entries[cpPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   172→		Name: cpPath,
   173→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   174→		Hash: cpBlobHash,
   175→	}
   176→	return cpPath, nil
   177→}
   178→
   179→// writeFinalTaskCheckpoint writes the final checkpoint.json and subagent transcript.
   180→func (s *GitStore) writeFinalTaskCheckpoint(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (string, error) {
   181→	checkpoint := taskCheckpointData{
   182→		SessionID:      opts.SessionID,
   183→		ToolUseID:      opts.ToolUseID,
   184→		CheckpointUUID: opts.CheckpointUUID,
   185→		AgentID:        opts.AgentID,
   186→	}
   187→	checkpointData, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(checkpoint, "", "  ")
   188→	if err != nil {
   189→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal task checkpoint: %w", err)
   190→	}
   191→	blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, checkpointData)
   192→	if err != nil {
   193→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to create task checkpoint blob: %w", err)
   194→	}
   195→
   196→	checkpointFile := taskPath + "checkpoint.json"
   197→	entries[checkpointFile] = object.TreeEntry{
   198→		Name: checkpointFile,
   199→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   200→		Hash: blobHash,
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	// Write subagent transcript if available
   204→	if opts.SubagentTranscriptPath != "" && opts.AgentID != "" {
   205→		agentContent, readErr := os.ReadFile(opts.SubagentTranscriptPath)
   206→		if readErr == nil {
   207→			agentContent, readErr = redact.JSONLBytes(agentContent)
   208→		}
   209→		if readErr == nil {
   210→			agentBlobHash, agentBlobErr := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, agentContent)
   211→			if agentBlobErr == nil {
   212→				agentPath := taskPath + "agent-" + opts.AgentID + ".jsonl"
   213→				entries[agentPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   214→					Name: agentPath,
   215→					Mode: filemode.Regular,
   216→					Hash: agentBlobHash,
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// Return task path without trailing slash
   223→	return taskPath[:len(taskPath)-1], nil
   224→}
   225→
   226→// writeStandardCheckpointEntries writes session files to numbered subdirectories and
   227→// maintains a CheckpointSummary at the root level with aggregated statistics.
   228→//
   229→// Structure:
   230→//
   231→//	basePath/
   232→//	├── metadata.json         # CheckpointSummary (aggregated stats)
   233→//	├── 1/                    # First session
   234→//	│   ├── metadata.json     # CommittedMetadata (session-specific, includes initial_attribution)
   235→//	│   ├── full.jsonl
   236→//	│   ├── prompt.txt
   237→//	│   ├── context.md
   238→//	│   └── content_hash.txt
   239→//	├── 2/                    # Second session
   240→//	└── ...
   241→func (s *GitStore) writeStandardCheckpointEntries(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   242→	// Read existing summary to get current session count
   243→	var existingSummary *CheckpointSummary
   244→	metadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   245→	if entry, exists := entries[metadataPath]; exists {
   246→		existing, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   247→		if err == nil {
   248→			existingSummary = existing
   249→		}
   250→	}
   251→
   252→	// Determine session index: reuse existing slot if session ID matches, otherwise append
   253→	sessionIndex := s.findSessionIndex(basePath, existingSummary, entries, opts.SessionID)
   254→
   255→	// Write session files to numbered subdirectory
   256→	sessionPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/", basePath, sessionIndex)
   257→	sessionFilePaths, err := s.writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts, sessionPath, entries)
   258→	if err != nil {
   259→		return err
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	// Copy additional metadata files from directory if specified (to session subdirectory)
   263→	if opts.MetadataDir != "" {
   264→		if err := s.copyMetadataDir(opts.MetadataDir, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   265→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to copy metadata directory: %w", err)
   266→		}
   267→	}
   268→
   269→	// Build the sessions array
   270→	var sessions []SessionFilePaths
   271→	if existingSummary != nil {
   272→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, max(len(existingSummary.Sessions), sessionIndex+1))
   273→		copy(sessions, existingSummary.Sessions)
   274→	} else {
   275→		sessions = make([]SessionFilePaths, 1)
   276→	}
   277→	sessions[sessionIndex] = sessionFilePaths
   278→
   279→	// Update root metadata.json with CheckpointSummary
   280→	return s.writeCheckpointSummary(opts, basePath, entries, sessions)
   281→}
   282→
   283→// writeSessionToSubdirectory writes a single session's files to a numbered subdirectory.
   284→// Returns the absolute file paths from the git tree root for the sessions map.
   285→func (s *GitStore) writeSessionToSubdirectory(opts WriteCommittedOptions, sessionPath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (SessionFilePaths, error) {
   286→	filePaths := SessionFilePaths{}
   287→
   288→	// Clear any existing entries at this path so stale files from a previous
   289→	// write (e.g. prompt.txt, context.md) don't persist on overwrite.
   290→	for key := range entries {
   291→		if strings.HasPrefix(key, sessionPath) {
   292→			delete(entries, key)
   293→		}
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Write transcript
   297→	if err := s.writeTranscript(opts, sessionPath, entries); err != nil {
   298→		return filePaths, err
   299→	}
   300→	filePaths.Transcript = "/" + sessionPath + paths.TranscriptFileName
   301→	filePaths.ContentHash = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContentHashFileName
   302→
   303→	// Write prompts
   304→	if len(opts.Prompts) > 0 {
   305→		promptContent := redact.String(strings.Join(opts.Prompts, "\n\n---\n\n"))
   306→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(promptContent))
   307→		if err != nil {
   308→			return filePaths, err
   309→		}
   310→		entries[sessionPath+paths.PromptFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   311→			Name: sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName,
   312→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   313→			Hash: blobHash,
   314→		}
   315→		filePaths.Prompt = "/" + sessionPath + paths.PromptFileName
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Write context
   319→	if len(opts.Context) > 0 {
   320→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, redact.Bytes(opts.Context))
   321→		if err != nil {
   322→			return filePaths, err
   323→		}
   324→		entries[sessionPath+paths.ContextFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   325→			Name: sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName,
   326→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   327→			Hash: blobHash,
   328→		}
   329→		filePaths.Context = "/" + sessionPath + paths.ContextFileName
   330→	}
   331→
   332→	// Write session-level metadata.json (CommittedMetadata with all fields including initial_attribution)
   333→	sessionMetadata := CommittedMetadata{
   334→		CheckpointID:                opts.CheckpointID,
   335→		SessionID:                   opts.SessionID,
   336→		Strategy:                    opts.Strategy,
   337→		CreatedAt:                   time.Now().UTC(),
   338→		Branch:                      opts.Branch,
   339→		CheckpointsCount:            opts.CheckpointsCount,
   340→		FilesTouched:                opts.FilesTouched,
   341→		Agent:                       opts.Agent,
   342→		IsTask:                      opts.IsTask,
   343→		ToolUseID:                   opts.ToolUseID,
   344→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: opts.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   345→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   346→		TranscriptLinesAtStart:      opts.CheckpointTranscriptStart, // Deprecated: kept for backward compat
   347→		TokenUsage:                  opts.TokenUsage,
   348→		InitialAttribution:          opts.InitialAttribution,
   349→		Summary:                     opts.Summary,
   350→		CLIVersion:                  buildinfo.Version,
   351→	}
   352→
   353→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(sessionMetadata, "", "  ")
   354→	if err != nil {
   355→		return filePaths, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session metadata: %w", err)
   356→	}
   357→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   358→	if err != nil {
   359→		return filePaths, err
   360→	}
   361→	entries[sessionPath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   362→		Name: sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   363→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   364→		Hash: metadataHash,
   365→	}
   366→	filePaths.Metadata = "/" + sessionPath + paths.MetadataFileName
   367→
   368→	return filePaths, nil
   369→}
   370→
   371→// writeCheckpointSummary writes the root-level CheckpointSummary with aggregated statistics.
   372→// sessions is the complete sessions array (already built by the caller).
   373→func (s *GitStore) writeCheckpointSummary(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessions []SessionFilePaths) error {
   374→	checkpointsCount, filesTouched, tokenUsage, err :=
   375→		s.reaggregateFromEntries(basePath, len(sessions), entries)
   376→	if err != nil {
   377→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to aggregate session stats: %w", err)
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	summary := CheckpointSummary{
   381→		CheckpointID:     opts.CheckpointID,
   382→		CLIVersion:       buildinfo.Version,
   383→		Strategy:         opts.Strategy,
   384→		Branch:           opts.Branch,
   385→		CheckpointsCount: checkpointsCount,
   386→		FilesTouched:     filesTouched,
   387→		Sessions:         sessions,
   388→		TokenUsage:       tokenUsage,
   389→	}
   390→
   391→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(summary, "", "  ")
   392→	if err != nil {
   393→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal checkpoint summary: %w", err)
   394→	}
   395→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   396→	if err != nil {
   397→		return err
   398→	}
   399→	entries[basePath+paths.MetadataFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   400→		Name: basePath + paths.MetadataFileName,
   401→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   402→		Hash: metadataHash,
   403→	}
   404→	return nil
   405→}
   406→
   407→// findSessionIndex returns the index of an existing session with the given ID,
   408→// or the next available index if not found. This prevents duplicate session entries.
   409→func (s *GitStore) findSessionIndex(basePath string, existingSummary *CheckpointSummary, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry, sessionID string) int {
   410→	if existingSummary == nil {
   411→		return 0
   412→	}
   413→	for i := range len(existingSummary.Sessions) {
   414→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   415→		if entry, exists := entries[path]; exists {
   416→			meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   417→			if err != nil {
   418→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read session metadata during dedup check",
   419→					slog.Int("session_index", i),
   420→					slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   421→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   422→				)
   423→				continue
   424→			}
   425→			if meta.SessionID == sessionID {
   426→				return i
   427→			}
   428→		}
   429→	}
   430→	return len(existingSummary.Sessions)
   431→}
   432→
   433→// reaggregateFromEntries reads all session metadata from the entries map and
   434→// reaggregates CheckpointsCount, FilesTouched, and TokenUsage.
   435→func (s *GitStore) reaggregateFromEntries(basePath string, sessionCount int, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) (int, []string, *agent.TokenUsage, error) {
   436→	var totalCount int
   437→	var allFiles []string
   438→	var totalTokens *agent.TokenUsage
   439→
   440→	for i := range sessionCount {
   441→		path := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, i, paths.MetadataFileName)
   442→		entry, exists := entries[path]
   443→		if !exists {
   444→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d metadata not found at %s", i, path)
   445→		}
   446→		meta, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   447→		if err != nil {
   448→			return 0, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session %d metadata: %w", i, err)
   449→		}
   450→		totalCount += meta.CheckpointsCount
   451→		allFiles = mergeFilesTouched(allFiles, meta.FilesTouched)
   452→		totalTokens = aggregateTokenUsage(totalTokens, meta.TokenUsage)
   453→	}
   454→
   455→	return totalCount, allFiles, totalTokens, nil
   456→}
   457→
   458→// readJSONFromBlob reads JSON from a blob hash and decodes it to the given type.
   459→func readJSONFromBlob[T any](repo *git.Repository, hash plumbing.Hash) (*T, error) {
   460→	blob, err := repo.BlobObject(hash)
   461→	if err != nil {
   462→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob: %w", err)
   463→	}
   464→
   465→	reader, err := blob.Reader()
   466→	if err != nil {
   467→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get blob reader: %w", err)
   468→	}
   469→	defer reader.Close()
   470→
   471→	var result T
   472→	if err := json.NewDecoder(reader).Decode(&result); err != nil {
   473→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode: %w", err)
   474→	}
   475→
   476→	return &result, nil
   477→}
   478→
   479→// readSummaryFromBlob reads CheckpointSummary from a blob hash.
   480→func (s *GitStore) readSummaryFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   481→	return readJSONFromBlob[CheckpointSummary](s.repo, hash)
   482→}
   483→
   484→// aggregateTokenUsage sums two TokenUsage structs.
   485→// Returns nil if both inputs are nil.
   486→func aggregateTokenUsage(a, b *agent.TokenUsage) *agent.TokenUsage {
   487→	if a == nil && b == nil {
   488→		return nil
   489→	}
   490→	result := &agent.TokenUsage{}
   491→	if a != nil {
   492→		result.InputTokens = a.InputTokens
   493→		result.CacheCreationTokens = a.CacheCreationTokens
   494→		result.CacheReadTokens = a.CacheReadTokens
   495→		result.OutputTokens = a.OutputTokens
   496→		result.APICallCount = a.APICallCount
   497→	}
   498→	if b != nil {
   499→		result.InputTokens += b.InputTokens
   500→		result.CacheCreationTokens += b.CacheCreationTokens
   501→		result.CacheReadTokens += b.CacheReadTokens
   502→		result.OutputTokens += b.OutputTokens
   503→		result.APICallCount += b.APICallCount
   504→	}
   505→	return result
   506→}
   507→
   508→// writeTranscript writes the transcript file from in-memory content or file path.
   509→// If the transcript exceeds MaxChunkSize, it's split into multiple chunk files.
   510→func (s *GitStore) writeTranscript(opts WriteCommittedOptions, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
   511→	transcript := opts.Transcript
   512→	if len(transcript) == 0 && opts.TranscriptPath != "" {
   513→		var readErr error
   514→		transcript, readErr = os.ReadFile(opts.TranscriptPath)
   515→		if readErr != nil {
   516→			// Non-fatal: transcript may not exist yet
   517→			transcript = nil
   518→		}
   519→	}
   520→	if len(transcript) == 0 {
   521→		return nil
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Redact secrets before chunking so content hash reflects redacted content
   525→	transcript, err := redact.JSONLBytes(transcript)
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to redact transcript secrets: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→
   530→	// Chunk the transcript if it's too large
   531→	chunks, err := agent.ChunkTranscript(transcript, opts.Agent)
   532→	if err != nil {
   533→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to chunk transcript: %w", err)
   534→	}
   535→
   536→	// Write chunk files
   537→	for i, chunk := range chunks {
   538→		chunkPath := basePath + agent.ChunkFileName(paths.TranscriptFileName, i)
   539→		blobHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, chunk)
   540→		if err != nil {
   541→			return err
   542→		}
   543→		entries[chunkPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   544→			Name: chunkPath,
   545→			Mode: filemode.Regular,
   546→			Hash: blobHash,
   547→		}
   548→	}
   549→
   550→	// Content hash for deduplication (hash of full transcript)
   551→	contentHash := fmt.Sprintf("sha256:%x", sha256.Sum256(transcript))
   552→	hashBlob, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, []byte(contentHash))
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		return err
   555→	}
   556→	entries[basePath+paths.ContentHashFileName] = object.TreeEntry{
   557→		Name: basePath + paths.ContentHashFileName,
   558→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   559→		Hash: hashBlob,
   560→	}
   561→	return nil
   562→}
   563→
   564→// mergeFilesTouched combines two file lists, removing duplicates.
   565→func mergeFilesTouched(existing, additional []string) []string {
   566→	seen := make(map[string]bool)
   567→	var result []string
   568→
   569→	for _, f := range existing {
   570→		if !seen[f] {
   571→			seen[f] = true
   572→			result = append(result, f)
   573→		}
   574→	}
   575→	for _, f := range additional {
   576→		if !seen[f] {
   577→			seen[f] = true
   578→			result = append(result, f)
   579→		}
   580→	}
   581→
   582→	sort.Strings(result)
   583→	return result
   584→}
   585→
   586→// readMetadataFromBlob reads CommittedMetadata from a blob hash.
   587→func (s *GitStore) readMetadataFromBlob(hash plumbing.Hash) (*CommittedMetadata, error) {
   588→	return readJSONFromBlob[CommittedMetadata](s.repo, hash)
   589→}
   590→
   591→// buildCommitMessage constructs the commit message with proper trailers.
   592→// The commit subject is always "Checkpoint: <id>" for consistency.
   593→// If CommitSubject is provided (e.g., for task checkpoints), it's included in the body.
   594→func (s *GitStore) buildCommitMessage(opts WriteCommittedOptions, taskMetadataPath string) string {
   595→	var commitMsg strings.Builder
   596→
   597→	// Subject line is always the checkpoint ID for consistent formatting
   598→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("Checkpoint: %s\n\n", opts.CheckpointID))
   599→
   600→	// Include custom description in body if provided (e.g., task checkpoint details)
   601→	if opts.CommitSubject != "" {
   602→		commitMsg.WriteString(opts.CommitSubject + "\n\n")
   603→	}
   604→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.SessionTrailerKey, opts.SessionID))
   605→	commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.StrategyTrailerKey, opts.Strategy))
   606→	if opts.Agent != "" {
   607→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.AgentTrailerKey, opts.Agent))
   608→	}
   609→	if opts.EphemeralBranch != "" {
   610→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.EphemeralBranchTrailerKey, opts.EphemeralBranch))
   611→	}
   612→	if taskMetadataPath != "" {
   613→		commitMsg.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("%s: %s\n", trailers.MetadataTaskTrailerKey, taskMetadataPath))
   614→	}
   615→
   616→	return commitMsg.String()
   617→}
   618→
   619→// incrementalCheckpointData represents an incremental checkpoint during subagent execution.
   620→// This mirrors strategy.SubagentCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   621→type incrementalCheckpointData struct {
   622→	Type      string          `json:"type"`
   623→	ToolUseID string          `json:"tool_use_id"`
   624→	Timestamp time.Time       `json:"timestamp"`
   625→	Data      json.RawMessage `json:"data"`
   626→}
   627→
   628→// taskCheckpointData represents a final task checkpoint.
   629→// This mirrors strategy.TaskCheckpoint but avoids import cycles.
   630→type taskCheckpointData struct {
   631→	SessionID      string `json:"session_id"`
   632→	ToolUseID      string `json:"tool_use_id"`
   633→	CheckpointUUID string `json:"checkpoint_uuid"`
   634→	AgentID        string `json:"agent_id,omitempty"`
   635→}
   636→
   637→// ReadCommitted reads a committed checkpoint's summary by ID from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   638→// Returns only the CheckpointSummary (paths + aggregated stats), not actual content.
   639→// Use ReadSessionContent to read actual transcript/prompts/context.
   640→// Returns nil, nil if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   641→//
   642→// The storage format uses numbered subdirectories for each session (0-based):
   643→//
   644→//	<checkpoint-id>/
   645→//	├── metadata.json      # CheckpointSummary with sessions map
   646→//	├── 0/                 # First session
   647→//	│   ├── metadata.json  # Session-specific metadata
   648→//	│   └── full.jsonl     # Transcript
   649→//	├── 1/                 # Second session
   650→//	└── ...
   651→func (s *GitStore) ReadCommitted(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*CheckpointSummary, error) {
   652→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   653→
   654→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   655→	if err != nil {
   656→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // No sessions branch means no checkpoint exists
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   660→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   661→	if err != nil {
   662→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // Checkpoint directory not found
   663→	}
   664→
   665→	// Read root metadata.json as CheckpointSummary
   666→	metadataFile, err := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName)
   667→	if err != nil {
   668→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil,nilerr // metadata.json not found
   669→	}
   670→
   671→	content, err := metadataFile.Contents()
   672→	if err != nil {
   673→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read metadata.json: %w", err)
   674→	}
   675→
   676→	var summary CheckpointSummary
   677→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err != nil {
   678→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse metadata.json: %w", err)
   679→	}
   680→
   681→	return &summary, nil
   682→}
   683→
   684→// ReadSessionContent reads the actual content for a specific session within a checkpoint.
   685→// sessionIndex is 0-based (0 for first session, 1 for second, etc.).
   686→// Returns the session's metadata, transcript, prompts, and context.
   687→// Returns an error if the checkpoint or session doesn't exist.
   688→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionIndex int) (*SessionContent, error) {
   689→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   690→
   691→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   692→	if err != nil {
   693→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   694→	}
   695→
   696→	checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   697→	checkpointTree, err := tree.Tree(checkpointPath)
   698→	if err != nil {
   699→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   700→	}
   701→
   702→	// Get the session subdirectory
   703→	sessionDir := strconv.Itoa(sessionIndex)
   704→	sessionTree, err := checkpointTree.Tree(sessionDir)
   705→	if err != nil {
   706→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %d not found: %w", sessionIndex, err)
   707→	}
   708→
   709→	result := &SessionContent{}
   710→
   711→	// Read session-specific metadata
   712→	var agentType agent.AgentType
   713→	if metadataFile, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   714→		if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   715→			if jsonErr := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &result.Metadata); jsonErr == nil {
   716→				agentType = result.Metadata.Agent
   717→			}
   718→		}
   719→	}
   720→
   721→	// Read transcript
   722→	if transcript, transcriptErr := readTranscriptFromTree(sessionTree, agentType); transcriptErr == nil && transcript != nil {
   723→		result.Transcript = transcript
   724→	}
   725→
   726→	// Read prompts
   727→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.PromptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   728→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   729→			result.Prompts = content
   730→		}
   731→	}
   732→
   733→	// Read context
   734→	if file, fileErr := sessionTree.File(paths.ContextFileName); fileErr == nil {
   735→		if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   736→			result.Context = content
   737→		}
   738→	}
   739→
   740→	return result, nil
   741→}
   742→
   743→// ReadLatestSessionContent is a convenience method that reads the latest session's content.
   744→// This is equivalent to ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, len(summary.Sessions)-1).
   745→func (s *GitStore) ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (*SessionContent, error) {
   746→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   747→	if err != nil {
   748→		return nil, err
   749→	}
   750→	if summary == nil {
   751→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   752→	}
   753→	if len(summary.Sessions) == 0 {
   754→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint has no sessions: %s", checkpointID)
   755→	}
   756→
   757→	latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   758→	return s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, latestIndex)
   759→}
   760→
   761→// ReadSessionContentByID reads a session's content by its session ID.
   762→// This is useful when you have the session ID but don't know its index within the checkpoint.
   763→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   764→// Returns an error if no session with the given ID exists in the checkpoint.
   765→func (s *GitStore) ReadSessionContentByID(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionID string) (*SessionContent, error) {
   766→	summary, err := s.ReadCommitted(ctx, checkpointID)
   767→	if err != nil {
   768→		return nil, err
   769→	}
   770→	if summary == nil {
   771→		return nil, ErrCheckpointNotFound
   772→	}
   773→
   774→	// Iterate through sessions to find the one with matching session ID
   775→	for i := range len(summary.Sessions) {
   776→		content, readErr := s.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   777→		if readErr != nil {
   778→			continue
   779→		}
   780→		if content != nil && content.Metadata.SessionID == sessionID {
   781→			return content, nil
   782→		}
   783→	}
   784→
   785→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("session %q not found in checkpoint %s", sessionID, checkpointID)
   786→}
   787→
   788→// ListCommitted lists all committed checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   789→// Scans sharded paths: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/ directories containing metadata.json.
   790→//
   791→
   792→func (s *GitStore) ListCommitted(ctx context.Context) ([]CommittedInfo, error) {
   793→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   794→
   795→	tree, err := s.getSessionsBranchTree()
   796→	if err != nil {
   797→		return []CommittedInfo{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No sessions branch means empty list
   798→	}
   799→
   800→	var checkpoints []CommittedInfo
   801→
   802→	// Scan sharded structure: <2-char-prefix>/<remaining-id>/metadata.json
   803→	for _, bucketEntry := range tree.Entries {
   804→		if bucketEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   805→			continue
   806→		}
   807→		// Bucket should be 2 hex chars
   808→		if len(bucketEntry.Name) != 2 {
   809→			continue
   810→		}
   811→
   812→		bucketTree, treeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(bucketEntry.Hash)
   813→		if treeErr != nil {
   814→			continue
   815→		}
   816→
   817→		// Each entry in the bucket is the remaining part of the checkpoint ID
   818→		for _, checkpointEntry := range bucketTree.Entries {
   819→			if checkpointEntry.Mode != filemode.Dir {
   820→				continue
   821→			}
   822→
   823→			checkpointTree, cpTreeErr := s.repo.TreeObject(checkpointEntry.Hash)
   824→			if cpTreeErr != nil {
   825→				continue
   826→			}
   827→
   828→			// Reconstruct checkpoint ID: <bucket><remaining>
   829→			checkpointIDStr := bucketEntry.Name + checkpointEntry.Name
   830→			checkpointID, cpIDErr := id.NewCheckpointID(checkpointIDStr)
   831→			if cpIDErr != nil {
   832→				// Skip invalid checkpoint IDs (shouldn't happen with our own data)
   833→				continue
   834→			}
   835→
   836→			info := CommittedInfo{
   837→				CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   838→			}
   839→
   840→			// Get details from root metadata file (CheckpointSummary format)
   841→			if metadataFile, fileErr := checkpointTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); fileErr == nil {
   842→				if content, contentErr := metadataFile.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   843→					var summary CheckpointSummary
   844→					if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &summary); err == nil {
   845→						info.CheckpointsCount = summary.CheckpointsCount
   846→						info.FilesTouched = summary.FilesTouched
   847→						info.SessionCount = len(summary.Sessions)
   848→
   849→						// Read session metadata from latest session to get Agent, SessionID, CreatedAt
   850→						if len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   851→							latestIndex := len(summary.Sessions) - 1
   852→							latestDir := strconv.Itoa(latestIndex)
   853→							if sessionTree, treeErr := checkpointTree.Tree(latestDir); treeErr == nil {
   854→								if sessionMetadataFile, smErr := sessionTree.File(paths.MetadataFileName); smErr == nil {
   855→									if sessionContent, scErr := sessionMetadataFile.Contents(); scErr == nil {
   856→										var sessionMetadata CommittedMetadata
   857→										if json.Unmarshal([]byte(sessionContent), &sessionMetadata) == nil {
   858→											info.Agent = sessionMetadata.Agent
   859→											info.SessionID = sessionMetadata.SessionID
   860→											info.CreatedAt = sessionMetadata.CreatedAt
   861→										}
   862→									}
   863→								}
   864→							}
   865→						}
   866→					}
   867→				}
   868→			}
   869→
   870→			checkpoints = append(checkpoints, info)
   871→		}
   872→	}
   873→
   874→	// Sort by time (most recent first)
   875→	sort.Slice(checkpoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   876→		return checkpoints[i].CreatedAt.After(checkpoints[j].CreatedAt)
   877→	})
   878→
   879→	return checkpoints, nil
   880→}
   881→
   882→// GetTranscript retrieves the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
   883→// Returns the latest session's transcript.
   884→func (s *GitStore) GetTranscript(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
   885→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID)
   886→	if err != nil {
   887→		return nil, err
   888→	}
   889→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   890→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   891→	}
   892→	return content.Transcript, nil
   893→}
   894→
   895→// GetSessionLog retrieves the session transcript and session ID for a checkpoint.
   896→// This is the primary method for looking up session logs by checkpoint ID.
   897→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   898→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   899→func (s *GitStore) GetSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error) {
   900→	content, err := s.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cpID)
   901→	if err != nil {
   902→		if errors.Is(err, ErrCheckpointNotFound) {
   903→			return nil, "", ErrCheckpointNotFound
   904→		}
   905→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   906→	}
   907→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   908→		return nil, "", ErrNoTranscript
   909→	}
   910→	return content.Transcript, content.Metadata.SessionID, nil
   911→}
   912→
   913→// LookupSessionLog is a convenience function that opens the repository and retrieves
   914→// a session log by checkpoint ID. This is the primary entry point for callers that
   915→// don't already have a GitStore instance.
   916→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   917→// Returns ErrNoTranscript if the checkpoint exists but has no transcript.
   918→func LookupSessionLog(cpID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, string, error) {
   919→	repo, err := git.PlainOpenWithOptions(".", &git.PlainOpenOptions{DetectDotGit: true})
   920→	if err != nil {
   921→		return nil, "", fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   922→	}
   923→	store := NewGitStore(repo)
   924→	return store.GetSessionLog(cpID)
   925→}
   926→
   927→// UpdateSummary updates the summary field in the latest session's metadata.
   928→// Returns ErrCheckpointNotFound if the checkpoint doesn't exist.
   929→func (s *GitStore) UpdateSummary(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *Summary) error {
   930→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   931→
   932→	// Ensure sessions branch exists
   933→	if err := s.ensureSessionsBranch(); err != nil {
   934→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure sessions branch: %w", err)
   935→	}
   936→
   937→	// Get current branch tip and flatten tree
   938→	ref, entries, err := s.getSessionsBranchEntries()
   939→	if err != nil {
   940→		return err
   941→	}
   942→
   943→	// Read root CheckpointSummary to find the latest session
   944→	basePath := checkpointID.Path() + "/"
   945→	rootMetadataPath := basePath + paths.MetadataFileName
   946→	entry, exists := entries[rootMetadataPath]
   947→	if !exists {
   948→		return ErrCheckpointNotFound
   949→	}
   950→
   951→	checkpointSummary, err := s.readSummaryFromBlob(entry.Hash)
   952→	if err != nil {
   953→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint summary: %w", err)
   954→	}
   955→
   956→	// Find the latest session's metadata path (0-based indexing)
   957→	latestIndex := len(checkpointSummary.Sessions) - 1
   958→	sessionMetadataPath := fmt.Sprintf("%s%d/%s", basePath, latestIndex, paths.MetadataFileName)
   959→	sessionEntry, exists := entries[sessionMetadataPath]
   960→	if !exists {
   961→		return fmt.Errorf("session metadata not found at %s", sessionMetadataPath)
   962→	}
   963→
   964→	// Read and update session metadata
   965→	existingMetadata, err := s.readMetadataFromBlob(sessionEntry.Hash)
   966→	if err != nil {
   967→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read session metadata: %w", err)
   968→	}
   969→
   970→	// Update the summary
   971→	existingMetadata.Summary = summary
   972→
   973→	// Write updated session metadata
   974→	metadataJSON, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(existingMetadata, "", "  ")
   975→	if err != nil {
   976→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal metadata: %w", err)
   977→	}
   978→	metadataHash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(s.repo, metadataJSON)
   979→	if err != nil {
   980→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create metadata blob: %w", err)
   981→	}
   982→	entries[sessionMetadataPath] = object.TreeEntry{
   983→		Name: sessionMetadataPath,
   984→		Mode: filemode.Regular,
   985→		Hash: metadataHash,
   986→	}
   987→
   988→	// Build and commit
   989→	newTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, entries)
   990→	if err != nil {
   991→		return err
   992→	}
   993→
   994→	authorName, authorEmail := getGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
   995→	commitMsg := fmt.Sprintf("Update summary for checkpoint %s (session: %s)", checkpointID, existingMetadata.SessionID)
   996→	newCommitHash, err := s.createCommit(newTreeHash, ref.Hash(), commitMsg, authorName, authorEmail)
   997→	if err != nil {
   998→		return err
   999→	}
  1000→
  1001→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1002→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, newCommitHash)
  1003→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1004→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1005→	}
  1006→
  1007→	return nil
  1008→}
  1009→
  1010→// ensureSessionsBranch ensures the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists.
  1011→func (s *GitStore) ensureSessionsBranch() error {
  1012→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1013→	_, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1014→	if err == nil {
  1015→		return nil // Branch exists
  1016→	}
  1017→
  1018→	// Create orphan branch with empty tree
  1019→	emptyTreeHash, err := BuildTreeFromEntries(s.repo, make(map[string]object.TreeEntry))
  1020→	if err != nil {
  1021→		return err
  1022→	}
  1023→
  1024→	authorName, authorEmail := getGitAuthorFromRepo(s.repo)
  1025→	commitHash, err := s.createCommit(emptyTreeHash, plumbing.ZeroHash, "Initialize sessions branch", authorName, authorEmail)
  1026→	if err != nil {
  1027→		return err
  1028→	}
  1029→
  1030→	newRef := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commitHash)
  1031→	if err := s.repo.Storer.SetReference(newRef); err != nil {
  1032→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to set branch reference: %w", err)
  1033→	}
  1034→	return nil
  1035→}
  1036→
  1037→// getSessionsBranchTree returns the tree object for the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
  1038→// Falls back to origin/entire/checkpoints/v1 if the local branch doesn't exist.
  1039→func (s *GitStore) getSessionsBranchTree() (*object.Tree, error) {
  1040→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1041→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1042→	if err != nil {
  1043→		// Local branch doesn't exist, try remote-tracking branch
  1044→		remoteRefName := plumbing.NewRemoteReferenceName("origin", paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1045→		ref, err = s.repo.Reference(remoteRefName, true)
  1046→		if err != nil {
  1047→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("sessions branch not found: %w", err)
  1048→		}
  1049→	}
  1050→
  1051→	commit, err := s.repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
  1052→	if err != nil {
  1053→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
  1054→	}
  1055→
  1056→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
  1057→	if err != nil {
  1058→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
  1059→	}
  1060→
  1061→	return tree, nil
  1062→}
  1063→
  1064→// CreateBlobFromContent creates a blob object from in-memory content.
  1065→// Exported for use by strategy package (session_test.go)
  1066→func CreateBlobFromContent(repo *git.Repository, content []byte) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
  1067→	obj := repo.Storer.NewEncodedObject()
  1068→	obj.SetType(plumbing.BlobObject)
  1069→	obj.SetSize(int64(len(content)))
  1070→
  1071→	writer, err := obj.Writer()
  1072→	if err != nil {
  1073→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get object writer: %w", err)
  1074→	}
  1075→
  1076→	_, err = writer.Write(content)
  1077→	if err != nil {
  1078→		_ = writer.Close()
  1079→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write blob content: %w", err)
  1080→	}
  1081→	if err := writer.Close(); err != nil {
  1082→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to close blob writer: %w", err)
  1083→	}
  1084→
  1085→	hash, err := repo.Storer.SetEncodedObject(obj)
  1086→	if err != nil {
  1087→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to store blob object: %w", err)
  1088→	}
  1089→	return hash, nil
  1090→}
  1091→
  1092→// copyMetadataDir copies all files from a directory to the checkpoint path.
  1093→// Used to include additional metadata files like task checkpoints, subagent transcripts, etc.
  1094→func (s *GitStore) copyMetadataDir(metadataDir, basePath string, entries map[string]object.TreeEntry) error {
  1095→	err := filepath.Walk(metadataDir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
  1096→		if err != nil {
  1097→			return err
  1098→		}
  1099→		if info.IsDir() {
  1100→			return nil
  1101→		}
  1102→
  1103→		// Skip symlinks to prevent reading files outside the metadata directory.
  1104→		// A symlink could point to sensitive files (e.g., /etc/passwd) which would
  1105→		// then be captured in the checkpoint and stored in git history.
  1106→		if info.Mode()&os.ModeSymlink != 0 {
  1107→			return nil
  1108→		}
  1109→
  1110→		// Get relative path within metadata dir
  1111→		relPath, err := filepath.Rel(metadataDir, path)
  1112→		if err != nil {
  1113→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to get relative path for %s: %w", path, err)
  1114→		}
  1115→
  1116→		// Prevent path traversal via symlinks pointing outside the metadata dir
  1117→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, "..") {
  1118→			return fmt.Errorf("path traversal detected: %s", relPath)
  1119→		}
  1120→
  1121→		// Create blob from file with secrets redaction
  1122→		blobHash, mode, err := createRedactedBlobFromFile(s.repo, path, relPath)
  1123→		if err != nil {
  1124→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob for %s: %w", path, err)
  1125→		}
  1126→
  1127→		// Store at checkpoint path
  1128→		fullPath := basePath + relPath
  1129→		entries[fullPath] = object.TreeEntry{
  1130→			Name: fullPath,
  1131→			Mode: mode,
  1132→			Hash: blobHash,
  1133→		}
  1134→
  1135→		return nil
  1136→	})
  1137→	if err != nil {
  1138→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to walk metadata directory: %w", err)
  1139→	}
  1140→	return nil
  1141→}
  1142→
  1143→// createRedactedBlobFromFile reads a file, applies secrets redaction, and creates a git blob.
  1144→// JSONL files get JSONL-aware redaction; all other files get plain string redaction.
  1145→func createRedactedBlobFromFile(repo *git.Repository, filePath, treePath string) (plumbing.Hash, filemode.FileMode, error) {
  1146→	info, err := os.Stat(filePath)
  1147→	if err != nil {
  1148→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to stat file: %w", err)
  1149→	}
  1150→
  1151→	mode := filemode.Regular
  1152→	if info.Mode()&0o111 != 0 {
  1153→		mode = filemode.Executable
  1154→	}
  1155→
  1156→	content, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // filePath comes from walking the metadata directory
  1157→	if err != nil {
  1158→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to read file: %w", err)
  1159→	}
  1160→
  1161→	// Skip redaction for binary files — they can't contain text secrets and
  1162→	// running string replacement on them would corrupt the data.
  1163→	isBin, binErr := binary.IsBinary(bytes.NewReader(content))
  1164→	if binErr != nil || isBin {
  1165→		hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1166→		if err != nil {
  1167→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1168→		}
  1169→		return hash, mode, nil
  1170→	}
  1171→
  1172→	if strings.HasSuffix(treePath, ".jsonl") {
  1173→		content, err = redact.JSONLBytes(content)
  1174→		if err != nil {
  1175→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to redact secrets: %w", err)
  1176→		}
  1177→	} else {
  1178→		content = redact.Bytes(content)
  1179→	}
  1180→
  1181→	hash, err := CreateBlobFromContent(repo, content)
  1182→	if err != nil {
  1183→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create blob: %w", err)
  1184→	}
  1185→	return hash, mode, nil
  1186→}
  1187→
  1188→// getGitAuthorFromRepo retrieves the git user.name and user.email from the repository config.
  1189→func getGitAuthorFromRepo(repo *git.Repository) (name, email string) {
  1190→	// Get repository config (includes local settings)
  1191→	cfg, err := repo.Config()
  1192→	if err == nil {
  1193→		name = cfg.User.Name
  1194→		email = cfg.User.Email
  1195→	}
  1196→
  1197→	// Provide sensible defaults if git user is not configured
  1198→	if name == "" {
  1199→		name = "Unknown"
  1200→	}
  1201→	if email == "" {
  1202→		email = "unknown@local"
  1203→	}
  1204→
  1205→	return name, email
  1206→}
  1207→
  1208→// readTranscriptFromTree reads a transcript from a git tree, handling both chunked and non-chunked formats.
  1209→// It checks for chunk files first (.001, .002, etc.), then falls back to the base file.
  1210→// The agentType is used for reassembling chunks in the correct format.
  1211→func readTranscriptFromTree(tree *object.Tree, agentType agent.AgentType) ([]byte, error) {
  1212→	// Collect all transcript-related files
  1213→	var chunkFiles []string
  1214→	var hasBaseFile bool
  1215→
  1216→	for _, entry := range tree.Entries {
  1217→		if entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileName || entry.Name == paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy {
  1218→			hasBaseFile = true
  1219→		}
  1220→		// Check for chunk files (full.jsonl.001, full.jsonl.002, etc.)
  1221→		if strings.HasPrefix(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName+".") {
  1222→			idx := agent.ParseChunkIndex(entry.Name, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1223→			if idx > 0 {
  1224→				chunkFiles = append(chunkFiles, entry.Name)
  1225→			}
  1226→		}
  1227→	}
  1228→
  1229→	// If we have chunk files, read and reassemble them
  1230→	if len(chunkFiles) > 0 {
  1231→		// Sort chunk files by index
  1232→		chunkFiles = agent.SortChunkFiles(chunkFiles, paths.TranscriptFileName)
  1233→
  1234→		// Check if base file should be included as chunk 0.
  1235→		// NOTE: This assumes the chunking convention where the unsuffixed file
  1236→		// (full.jsonl) is chunk 0, and numbered files (.001, .002) are chunks 1+.
  1237→		if hasBaseFile {
  1238→			chunkFiles = append([]string{paths.TranscriptFileName}, chunkFiles...)
  1239→		}
  1240→
  1241→		var chunks [][]byte
  1242→		for _, chunkFile := range chunkFiles {
  1243→			file, err := tree.File(chunkFile)
  1244→			if err != nil {
  1245→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk file from tree",
  1246→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1247→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1248→				)
  1249→				continue
  1250→			}
  1251→			content, err := file.Contents()
  1252→			if err != nil {
  1253→				logging.Warn(context.Background(), "failed to read transcript chunk contents",
  1254→					slog.String("chunk_file", chunkFile),
  1255→					slog.String("error", err.Error()),
  1256→				)
  1257→				continue
  1258→			}
  1259→			chunks = append(chunks, []byte(content))
  1260→		}
  1261→
  1262→		if len(chunks) > 0 {
  1263→			result, err := agent.ReassembleTranscript(chunks, agentType)
  1264→			if err != nil {
  1265→				return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to reassemble transcript: %w", err)
  1266→			}
  1267→			return result, nil
  1268→		}
  1269→	}
  1270→
  1271→	// Fall back to reading base file (non-chunked or backwards compatibility)
  1272→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileName); err == nil {
  1273→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1274→			return []byte(content), nil
  1275→		}
  1276→	}
  1277→
  1278→	// Try legacy filename
  1279→	if file, err := tree.File(paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); err == nil {
  1280→		if content, err := file.Contents(); err == nil {
  1281→			return []byte(content), nil
  1282→		}
  1283→	}
  1284→
  1285→	return nil, nil
  1286→}
  1287→
  1288→// Author contains author information for a checkpoint.
  1289→type Author struct {
  1290→	Name  string
  1291→	Email string
  1292→}
  1293→
  1294→// GetCheckpointAuthor retrieves the author of a checkpoint from the entire/checkpoints/v1 commit history.
  1295→// Returns the author of the commit that introduced this checkpoint's metadata.json file.
  1296→// Returns empty Author if the checkpoint is not found or the sessions branch doesn't exist.
  1297→func (s *GitStore) GetCheckpointAuthor(ctx context.Context, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (Author, error) {
  1298→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
  1299→
  1300→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
  1301→	ref, err := s.repo.Reference(refName, true)
  1302→	if err != nil {
  1303→		return Author{}, nil
  1304→	}
  1305→
  1306→	// Path to the checkpoint's metadata file
  1307→	metadataPath := checkpointID.Path() + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName
  1308→
  1309→	// Walk commit history looking for the commit that introduced this file
  1310→	iter, err := s.repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
  1311→		From:  ref.Hash(),
  1312→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
  1313→	})
  1314→	if err != nil {
  1315→		return Author{}, nil
  1316→	}
  1317→	defer iter.Close()
  1318→
  1319→	var author Author
  1320→	var foundCommit *object.Commit
  1321→
  1322→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
  1323→		tree, treeErr := c.Tree()
  1324→		if treeErr != nil {
  1325→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip commits we can't read, continue searching
  1326→		}
  1327→
  1328→		_, fileErr := tree.File(metadataPath)
  1329→		if fileErr != nil {
  1330→			// File doesn't exist in this commit - we've gone past the creation point
  1331→			if foundCommit != nil {
  1332→				return errStopIteration
  1333→			}
  1334→			return nil
  1335→		}
  1336→
  1337→		// File exists - track it (oldest one with file is the creator)
  1338→		foundCommit = c
  1339→		author = Author{
  1340→			Name:  c.Author.Name,
  1341→			Email: c.Author.Email,
  1342→		}
  1343→		return nil
  1344→	})
  1345→
  1346→	// Ignore errStopIteration - it's just for early exit
  1347→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStopIteration) {
  1348→		return Author{}, nil
  1349→	}
  1350→
  1351→	return author, nil
  1352→}
  1353→
