    26→// GetRewindPoints returns available rewind points.
    27→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListTemporaryCheckpoints for reading from shadow branches.
    28→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
    29→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
    30→	if err != nil {
    31→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	// Get checkpoint store
    35→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    36→	if err != nil {
    37→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    38→	}
    39→
    40→	// Get current HEAD to find matching shadow branch
    41→	head, err := repo.Head()
    42→	if err != nil {
    43→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	// Find sessions for current HEAD
    47→	sessions, err := s.findSessionsForCommit(head.Hash().String())
    48→	if err != nil {
    49→		// Log error but continue to check for logs-only points
    50→		sessions = nil
    51→	}
    52→
    53→	var allPoints []RewindPoint
    54→
    55→	// Collect checkpoint points from active sessions using checkpoint.GitStore
    56→	// Cache session prompts by session ID to avoid re-reading the same prompt file
    57→	sessionPrompts := make(map[string]string)
    58→
    59→	for _, state := range sessions {
    60→		checkpoints, err := store.ListTemporaryCheckpoints(context.Background(), state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID, state.SessionID, limit)
    61→		if err != nil {
    62→			continue // Error reading checkpoints, skip this session
    63→		}
    64→
    65→		for _, cp := range checkpoints {
    66→			// Get session prompt (cached by session ID)
    67→			sessionPrompt, ok := sessionPrompts[cp.SessionID]
    68→			if !ok {
    69→				sessionPrompt = readSessionPrompt(repo, cp.CommitHash, cp.MetadataDir)
    70→				sessionPrompts[cp.SessionID] = sessionPrompt
    71→			}
    72→
    73→			allPoints = append(allPoints, RewindPoint{
    74→				ID:               cp.CommitHash.String(),
    75→				Message:          cp.Message,
    76→				MetadataDir:      cp.MetadataDir,
    77→				Date:             cp.Timestamp,
    78→				IsTaskCheckpoint: cp.IsTaskCheckpoint,
    79→				ToolUseID:        cp.ToolUseID,
    80→				SessionID:        cp.SessionID,
    81→				SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
    82→				Agent:            state.AgentType,
    83→			})
    84→		}
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Sort by date, most recent first
    88→	sort.Slice(allPoints, func(i, j int) bool {
    89→		return allPoints[i].Date.After(allPoints[j].Date)
    90→	})
    91→
    92→	if len(allPoints) > limit {
    93→		allPoints = allPoints[:limit]
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Also include logs-only points from commit history
    97→	logsOnlyPoints, err := s.GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints(limit)
    98→	if err == nil && len(logsOnlyPoints) > 0 {
    99→		// Build set of existing point IDs for deduplication
   100→		existingIDs := make(map[string]bool)
   101→		for _, p := range allPoints {
   102→			existingIDs[p.ID] = true
   103→		}
   104→
   105→		// Add logs-only points that aren't already in the list
   106→		for _, p := range logsOnlyPoints {
   107→			if !existingIDs[p.ID] {
   108→				allPoints = append(allPoints, p)
   109→			}
   110→		}
   111→
   112→		// Re-sort by date
   113→		sort.Slice(allPoints, func(i, j int) bool {
   114→			return allPoints[i].Date.After(allPoints[j].Date)
   115→		})
   116→
   117→		// Re-trim to limit
   118→		if len(allPoints) > limit {
   119→			allPoints = allPoints[:limit]
   120→		}
   121→	}
   122→
   123→	return allPoints, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints finds commits in the current branch's history that have
   127→// condensed session logs on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch. These are commits that
   128→// were created with session data but the shadow branch has been condensed.
   129→//
   130→// The function works by:
   131→// 1. Getting all checkpoints from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   132→// 2. Building a map of checkpoint ID -> checkpoint info
   133→// 3. Scanning the current branch history for commits with Entire-Checkpoint trailers
   134→// 4. Matching by checkpoint ID (stable across amend/rebase)
   135→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) GetLogsOnlyRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
   136→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   137→	if err != nil {
   138→		return nil, err
   139→	}
   140→
   141→	// Get all checkpoints from entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   142→	checkpoints, err := s.listCheckpoints()
   143→	if err != nil {
   144→		// No checkpoints yet is fine
   145→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no checkpoints exist
   146→	}
   147→
   148→	if len(checkpoints) == 0 {
   149→		return nil, nil
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	// Build map of checkpoint ID -> checkpoint info
   153→	// Checkpoint ID is the stable link from Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   154→	checkpointInfoMap := make(map[id.CheckpointID]CheckpointInfo)
   155→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   156→		if !cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   157→			checkpointInfoMap[cp.CheckpointID] = cp
   158→		}
   159→	}
   160→
   161→	// Get metadata branch tree for reading session prompts (best-effort, ignore errors)
   162→	metadataTree, _ := GetMetadataBranchTree(repo) //nolint:errcheck // Best-effort for session prompts
   163→
   164→	head, err := repo.Head()
   165→	if err != nil {
   166→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Use LogOptions with Order=LogOrderCommitterTime to traverse all parents of merge commits.
   170→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   171→		From:  head.Hash(),
   172→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   173→	})
   174→	if err != nil {
   175→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   176→	}
   177→
   178→	var points []RewindPoint
   179→	count := 0
   180→
   181→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   182→		if count >= logsOnlyScanLimit {
   183→			return errStop
   184→		}
   185→		count++
   186→
   187→		// Extract checkpoint ID from Entire-Checkpoint trailer (ParseCheckpoint validates format)
   188→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   189→		if !found {
   190→			return nil
   191→		}
   192→		// Check if this checkpoint ID has metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1
   193→		cpInfo, found := checkpointInfoMap[cpID]
   194→		if !found {
   195→			return nil
   196→		}
   197→
   198→		// Create logs-only rewind point
   199→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   200→
   201→		// Read session prompts from metadata tree
   202→		var sessionPrompt string
   203→		var sessionPrompts []string
   204→		if metadataTree != nil {
   205→			checkpointPath := paths.CheckpointPath(cpInfo.CheckpointID) //nolint:staticcheck // already present in codebase
   206→			// For multi-session checkpoints, read all prompts
   207→			if cpInfo.SessionCount > 1 && len(cpInfo.SessionIDs) > 1 {
   208→				sessionPrompts = ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath, cpInfo.SessionCount, cpInfo.SessionIDs)
   209→				// Use the last (most recent) prompt as the main session prompt
   210→				if len(sessionPrompts) > 0 {
   211→					sessionPrompt = sessionPrompts[len(sessionPrompts)-1]
   212→				}
   213→			} else {
   214→				sessionPrompt = ReadSessionPromptFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   215→				if sessionPrompt != "" {
   216→					sessionPrompts = []string{sessionPrompt}
   217→				}
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→
   221→		points = append(points, RewindPoint{
   222→			ID:             c.Hash.String(),
   223→			Message:        message,
   224→			Date:           c.Author.When,
   225→			IsLogsOnly:     true,
   226→			CheckpointID:   cpInfo.CheckpointID,
   227→			Agent:          cpInfo.Agent,
   228→			SessionID:      cpInfo.SessionID,
   229→			SessionPrompt:  sessionPrompt,
   230→			SessionCount:   cpInfo.SessionCount,
   231→			SessionIDs:     cpInfo.SessionIDs,
   232→			SessionPrompts: sessionPrompts,
   233→		})
   234→
   235→		return nil
   236→	})
   237→
   238→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   239→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("error iterating commits: %w", err)
   240→	}
   241→
   242→	if len(points) > limit {
   243→		points = points[:limit]
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	return points, nil
   247→}
   248→
   249→// Rewind restores the working directory to a checkpoint.
   250→//
   251→//nolint:maintidx // Complex rewind flow spans multiple recovery modes.
   252→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
   253→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   254→	if err != nil {
   255→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   256→	}
   257→
   258→	// Get the checkpoint commit
   259→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   260→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   261→	if err != nil {
   262→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   263→	}
   264→
   265→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   266→	if err != nil {
   267→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   268→	}
   269→
   270→	// Reset the shadow branch to the rewound checkpoint
   271→	// This ensures the next checkpoint will only include prompts from this point forward
   272→	if err := s.resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint(repo, commit); err != nil {
   273→		// Log warning but don't fail - file restoration is the primary operation
   274→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Warning: failed to reset shadow branch: %v\n", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	// Load session state to get untracked files that existed at session start
   278→	sessionID, hasSessionTrailer := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   279→	var preservedUntrackedFiles map[string]bool
   280→	if hasSessionTrailer {
   281→		state, stateErr := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   282→		if stateErr == nil && state != nil && len(state.UntrackedFilesAtStart) > 0 {
   283→			preservedUntrackedFiles = make(map[string]bool)
   284→			for _, f := range state.UntrackedFilesAtStart {
   285→				preservedUntrackedFiles[f] = true
   286→			}
   287→		}
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Build set of files in the checkpoint tree (excluding metadata)
   291→	checkpointFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   292→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   293→		if !strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   294→			checkpointFiles[f.Name] = true
   295→		}
   296→		return nil
   297→	})
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoint files: %w", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// Get HEAD tree to identify tracked files
   303→	head, err := repo.Head()
   304→	if err != nil {
   305→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   306→	}
   307→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   310→	}
   311→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD tree: %w", err)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	// Build set of files tracked in HEAD
   317→	trackedFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   318→	//nolint:errcheck // Error is not critical for rewind
   319→	_ = headTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   320→		trackedFiles[f.Name] = true
   321→		return nil
   322→	})
   323→
   324→	// Get repository root to walk from there
   325→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   326→	if err != nil {
   327→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to current directory
   328→	}
   329→
   330→	// Find and delete untracked files that aren't in the checkpoint
   331→	// These are likely files created by Claude in later checkpoints
   332→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
   333→		if walkErr != nil {
   334→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
   335→		}
   336→
   337→		// Get path relative to repo root
   338→		relPath, relErr := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
   339→		if relErr != nil {
   340→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Skip directories and special paths
   344→		if info.IsDir() {
   345→			if relPath == gitDir || relPath == claudeDir || relPath == entireDir || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
   346→				return filepath.SkipDir
   347→			}
   348→			return nil
   349→		}
   350→
   351→		// Skip if path is a special directory
   352→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") || strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
   353→			return nil
   354→		}
   355→
   356→		// If file is in checkpoint, it will be restored
   357→		if checkpointFiles[relPath] {
   358→			return nil
   359→		}
   360→
   361→		// If file is tracked in HEAD, don't delete (user's committed work)
   362→		if trackedFiles[relPath] {
   363→			return nil
   364→		}
   365→
   366→		// If file existed at session start, preserve it (untracked user files)
   367→		if preservedUntrackedFiles[relPath] {
   368→			return nil
   369→		}
   370→
   371→		// File is untracked and not in checkpoint - delete it (use absolute path)
   372→		if removeErr := os.Remove(path); removeErr == nil {
   373→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Deleted: %s\n", relPath)
   374→		}
   375→
   376→		return nil
   377→	})
   378→	if err != nil {
   379→		// Non-fatal - continue with restoration
   380→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: error walking directory: %v\n", err)
   381→	}
   382→
   383→	// Restore files from checkpoint
   384→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   385→		// Skip metadata directories - these are for checkpoint storage, not working dir
   386→		if strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   387→			return nil
   388→		}
   389→
   390→		contents, err := f.Contents()
   391→		if err != nil {
   392→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to read file %s: %w", f.Name, err)
   393→		}
   394→
   395→		// Ensure directory exists
   396→		dir := filepath.Dir(f.Name)
   397→		if dir != "." {
   398→			//nolint:gosec // G301: Need 0o755 for user directories during rewind
   399→			if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o755); err != nil {
   400→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to create directory %s: %w", dir, err)
   401→			}
   402→		}
   403→
   404→		// Write file with appropriate permissions
   405→		var perm os.FileMode = 0o644
   406→		if f.Mode == filemode.Executable {
   407→			perm = 0o755
   408→		}
   409→		if err := os.WriteFile(f.Name, []byte(contents), perm); err != nil {
   410→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write file %s: %w", f.Name, err)
   411→		}
   412→
   413→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Restored: %s\n", f.Name)
   414→		return nil
   415→	})
   416→	if err != nil {
   417→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate tree files: %w", err)
   418→	}
   419→
   420→	fmt.Println()
   421→	if len(point.ID) >= 7 {
   422→		fmt.Printf("Restored files from shadow commit %s\n", point.ID[:7])
   423→	} else {
   424→		fmt.Printf("Restored files from shadow commit %s\n", point.ID)
   425→	}
   426→	fmt.Println()
   427→
   428→	return nil
   429→}
   430→
   431→// resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint resets the shadow branch HEAD to the given checkpoint.
   432→// This ensures that when the user commits after rewinding, the next checkpoint will only
   433→// include prompts from the rewound point, not prompts from later checkpoints.
   434→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) resetShadowBranchToCheckpoint(repo *git.Repository, commit *object.Commit) error {
   435→	// Extract session ID from the checkpoint commit's Entire-Session trailer
   436→	sessionID, found := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   437→	if !found {
   438→		return errors.New("checkpoint has no Entire-Session trailer")
   439→	}
   440→
   441→	// Load session state to get the shadow branch name
   442→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   443→	if err != nil {
   444→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   445→	}
   446→	if state == nil {
   447→		return fmt.Errorf("session %s not found", sessionID)
   448→	}
   449→
   450→	// Reset the shadow branch to the checkpoint commit
   451→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   452→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   453→
   454→	// Update the reference to point to the checkpoint commit
   455→	ref := plumbing.NewHashReference(refName, commit.Hash)
   456→	if err := repo.Storer.SetReference(ref); err != nil {
   457→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to update shadow branch: %w", err)
   458→	}
   459→
   460→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[entire] Reset shadow branch %s to checkpoint %s\n", shadowBranchName, commit.Hash.String()[:7])
   461→	return nil
   462→}
   463→
   464→// CanRewind checks if rewinding is possible.
   465→// For manual-commit strategy, rewind restores files from a checkpoint - uncommitted changes are expected
   466→// and will be replaced by the checkpoint contents. Returns true with a warning message showing
   467→// what changes will be reverted.
   468→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   469→	return checkCanRewindWithWarning()
   470→}
   471→
   472→// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   473→// This allows showing warnings about untracked files that will be deleted.
   474→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(point RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   475→	// Logs-only points don't modify the working directory
   476→	if point.IsLogsOnly {
   477→		return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   478→	}
   479→
   480→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   481→	if err != nil {
   482→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   483→	}
   484→
   485→	// Get the checkpoint commit
   486→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   487→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   488→	if err != nil {
   489→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit: %w", err)
   490→	}
   491→
   492→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   493→	if err != nil {
   494→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get tree: %w", err)
   495→	}
   496→
   497→	// Load session state to get untracked files that existed at session start
   498→	sessionID, hasSessionTrailer := trailers.ParseSession(commit.Message)
   499→	var preservedUntrackedFiles map[string]bool
   500→	if hasSessionTrailer {
   501→		state, stateErr := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   502→		if stateErr == nil && state != nil && len(state.UntrackedFilesAtStart) > 0 {
   503→			preservedUntrackedFiles = make(map[string]bool)
   504→			for _, f := range state.UntrackedFilesAtStart {
   505→				preservedUntrackedFiles[f] = true
   506→			}
   507→		}
   508→	}
   509→
   510→	// Build set of files in the checkpoint tree (excluding metadata)
   511→	checkpointFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   512→	var filesToRestore []string
   513→	err = tree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   514→		if !strings.HasPrefix(f.Name, entireDir) {
   515→			checkpointFiles[f.Name] = true
   516→			filesToRestore = append(filesToRestore, f.Name)
   517→		}
   518→		return nil
   519→	})
   520→	if err != nil {
   521→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list checkpoint files: %w", err)
   522→	}
   523→
   524→	// Get HEAD tree to identify tracked files
   525→	head, err := repo.Head()
   526→	if err != nil {
   527→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   528→	}
   529→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   530→	if err != nil {
   531→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   532→	}
   533→	headTree, err := headCommit.Tree()
   534→	if err != nil {
   535→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD tree: %w", err)
   536→	}
   537→
   538→	// Build set of files tracked in HEAD
   539→	trackedFiles := make(map[string]bool)
   540→	//nolint:errcheck // Error is not critical for preview
   541→	_ = headTree.Files().ForEach(func(f *object.File) error {
   542→		trackedFiles[f.Name] = true
   543→		return nil
   544→	})
   545→
   546→	// Get repository root to walk from there
   547→	repoRoot, err := GetWorktreePath()
   548→	if err != nil {
   549→		repoRoot = "."
   550→	}
   551→
   552→	// Find untracked files that would be deleted
   553→	var filesToDelete []string
   554→	err = filepath.Walk(repoRoot, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, walkErr error) error {
   555→		if walkErr != nil {
   556→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip filesystem errors during walk
   557→		}
   558→
   559→		relPath, relErr := filepath.Rel(repoRoot, path)
   560→		if relErr != nil {
   561→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Skip paths we can't make relative
   562→		}
   563→
   564→		// Skip directories and special paths
   565→		if info.IsDir() {
   566→			if relPath == gitDir || relPath == claudeDir || relPath == entireDir ||
   567→				strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") ||
   568→				strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") ||
   569→				strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
   570→				return filepath.SkipDir
   571→			}
   572→			return nil
   573→		}
   574→
   575→		// Skip special directories
   576→		if strings.HasPrefix(relPath, gitDir+"/") ||
   577→			strings.HasPrefix(relPath, claudeDir+"/") ||
   578→			strings.HasPrefix(relPath, entireDir+"/") {
   579→			return nil
   580→		}
   581→
   582→		// If file is in checkpoint, it will be restored (not deleted)
   583→		if checkpointFiles[relPath] {
   584→			return nil
   585→		}
   586→
   587→		// If file is tracked in HEAD, don't delete (user's committed work)
   588→		if trackedFiles[relPath] {
   589→			return nil
   590→		}
   591→
   592→		// If file existed at session start, preserve it (untracked user files)
   593→		if preservedUntrackedFiles[relPath] {
   594→			return nil
   595→		}
   596→
   597→		// File is untracked and not in checkpoint - will be deleted
   598→		filesToDelete = append(filesToDelete, relPath)
   599→		return nil
   600→	})
   601→	if err != nil {
   602→		// Non-fatal, return what we have
   603→		return &RewindPreview{ //nolint:nilerr // Partial result is still useful
   604→			FilesToRestore: filesToRestore,
   605→			FilesToDelete:  filesToDelete,
   606→		}, nil
   607→	}
   608→
   609→	// Sort for consistent output
   610→	sort.Strings(filesToRestore)
   611→	sort.Strings(filesToDelete)
   612→
   613→	return &RewindPreview{
   614→		FilesToRestore: filesToRestore,
   615→		FilesToDelete:  filesToDelete,
   616→	}, nil
   617→}
   618→
   619→// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   620→// This fetches the transcript from entire/checkpoints/v1 and writes it to Claude's project directory.
   621→// Does not modify the working directory.
   622→// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.
   623→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   624→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error {
   625→	if !point.IsLogsOnly {
   626→		return errors.New("not a logs-only rewind point")
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	if point.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   630→		return errors.New("missing checkpoint ID")
   631→	}
   632→
   633→	// Get checkpoint store
   634→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   635→	if err != nil {
   636→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Read checkpoint summary to get session count
   640→	summary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)
   641→	if err != nil {
   642→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   643→	}
   644→	if summary == nil {
   645→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", point.CheckpointID)
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	// Get repo root for Claude project path lookup
   649→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   650→	if err != nil {
   651→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	claudeProjectDir, err := paths.GetClaudeProjectDir(repoRoot)
   655→	if err != nil {
   656→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get Claude project directory: %w", err)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	// Ensure project directory exists
   660→	if err := os.MkdirAll(claudeProjectDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   661→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create Claude project directory: %w", err)
   662→	}
   663→
   664→	// Check for newer local logs if not forcing
   665→	if !force {
   666→		sessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), claudeProjectDir, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)
   667→		hasConflicts := false
   668→		for _, sess := range sessions {
   669→			if sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   670→				hasConflicts = true
   671→				break
   672→			}
   673→		}
   674→		if hasConflicts {
   675→			shouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)
   676→			if promptErr != nil {
   677→				return promptErr
   678→			}
   679→			if !shouldOverwrite {
   680→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resume cancelled. Local session logs preserved.\n")
   681→				return nil
   682→			}
   683→		}
   684→	}
   685→
   686→	// Count sessions to restore
   687→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   688→	if totalSessions > 1 {
   689→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\n", totalSessions)
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	// Restore all sessions (oldest to newest, using 0-based indexing)
   693→	for i := range totalSessions {
   694→		content, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID, i)
   695→		if readErr != nil {
   696→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: failed to read session %d: %v\n", i, readErr)
   697→			continue
   698→		}
   699→		if content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   700→			continue
   701→		}
   702→
   703→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   704→		if sessionID == "" {
   705→			// Fallback: can't identify session without ID
   706→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: session %d has no session ID, skipping\n", i)
   707→			continue
   708→		}
   709→
   710→		modelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)
   711→		claudeSessionFile := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+".jsonl")
   712→
   713→		// Get first prompt for display
   714→		promptPreview := ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts)
   715→
   716→		if totalSessions > 1 {
   717→			isLatest := i == totalSessions-1
   718→			if promptPreview != "" {
   719→				if isLatest {
   720→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d (latest): %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   721→				} else {
   722→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d: %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   723→				}
   724→			}
   725→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Writing to: %s\n", claudeSessionFile)
   726→		} else {
   727→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing transcript to: %s\n", claudeSessionFile)
   728→		}
   729→
   730→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(claudeSessionFile, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   731→			if totalSessions > 1 {
   732→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Warning: failed to write transcript: %v\n", writeErr)
   733→				continue
   734→			}
   735→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", writeErr)
   736→		}
   737→	}
   738→
   739→	return nil
   740→}
   741→
   742→// readSessionPrompt reads the first prompt from the session's prompt.txt file stored in git.
   743→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
   744→func readSessionPrompt(repo *git.Repository, commitHash plumbing.Hash, metadataDir string) string {
   745→	// Get the commit and its tree
   746→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(commitHash)
   747→	if err != nil {
   748→		return ""
   749→	}
   750→
   751→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   752→	if err != nil {
   753→		return ""
   754→	}
   755→
   756→	// Look for prompt.txt in the metadata directory
   757→	promptPath := metadataDir + "/" + paths.PromptFileName
   758→	promptEntry, err := tree.File(promptPath)
   759→	if err != nil {
   760→		return ""
   761→	}
   762→
   763→	content, err := promptEntry.Contents()
   764→	if err != nil {
   765→		return ""
   766→	}
   767→
   768→	return ExtractFirstPrompt(content)
   769→}
   770→
   771→// SessionRestoreStatus represents the status of a session being restored.
   772→type SessionRestoreStatus int
   773→
   774→const (
   775→	StatusNew             SessionRestoreStatus = iota // Local file doesn't exist
   776→	StatusUnchanged                                   // Local and checkpoint are the same
   777→	StatusCheckpointNewer                             // Checkpoint has newer entries
   778→	StatusLocalNewer                                  // Local has newer entries (conflict)
   779→)
   780→
   781→// SessionRestoreInfo contains information about a session being restored.
   782→type SessionRestoreInfo struct {
   783→	SessionID      string
   784→	Prompt         string               // First prompt preview for display
   785→	Status         SessionRestoreStatus // Status of this session
   786→	LocalTime      time.Time
   787→	CheckpointTime time.Time
   788→}
   789→
   790→// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info
   791→// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.
   792→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, claudeProjectDir string, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {
   793→	var sessions []SessionRestoreInfo
   794→
   795→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   796→	// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)
   797→	for i := range totalSessions {
   798→		content, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   799→		if err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   800→			continue
   801→		}
   802→
   803→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   804→		if sessionID == "" {
   805→			continue
   806→		}
   807→
   808→		modelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)
   809→		localPath := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+".jsonl")
   810→
   811→		localTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)
   812→		checkpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)
   813→		status := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)
   814→
   815→		sessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{
   816→			SessionID:      sessionID,
   817→			Prompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),
   818→			Status:         status,
   819→			LocalTime:      localTime,
   820→			CheckpointTime: checkpointTime,
   821→		})
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	return sessions
   825→}
   826→
   827→// ClassifyTimestamps determines the restore status based on local and checkpoint timestamps.
   828→func ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime time.Time) SessionRestoreStatus {
   829→	// Local file doesn't exist (no timestamp found)
   830→	if localTime.IsZero() {
   831→		return StatusNew
   832→	}
   833→
   834→	// Can't determine checkpoint time - treat as new/safe
   835→	if checkpointTime.IsZero() {
   836→		return StatusNew
   837→	}
   838→
   839→	// Compare timestamps
   840→	if localTime.After(checkpointTime) {
   841→		return StatusLocalNewer
   842→	}
   843→	if checkpointTime.After(localTime) {
   844→		return StatusCheckpointNewer
   845→	}
   846→	return StatusUnchanged
   847→}
   848→
   849→// StatusToText returns a human-readable status string.
   850→func StatusToText(status SessionRestoreStatus) string {
   851→	switch status {
   852→	case StatusNew:
   853→		return "(new)"
   854→	case StatusUnchanged:
   855→		return "(unchanged)"
   856→	case StatusCheckpointNewer:
   857→		return "(checkpoint is newer)"
   858→	case StatusLocalNewer:
   859→		return "(local is newer)" // shouldn't appear in non-conflict list
   860→	default:
   861→		return ""
   862→	}
   863→}
   864→
   865→// PromptOverwriteNewerLogs asks the user for confirmation to overwrite local
   866→// session logs that have newer timestamps than the checkpoint versions.
   867→func PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions []SessionRestoreInfo) (bool, error) {
   868→	// Separate conflicting and non-conflicting sessions
   869→	var conflicting, nonConflicting []SessionRestoreInfo
   870→	for _, s := range sessions {
   871→		if s.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   872→			conflicting = append(conflicting, s)
   873→		} else {
   874→			nonConflicting = append(nonConflicting, s)
   875→		}
   876→	}
   877→
   878→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nWarning: Local session log(s) have newer entries than the checkpoint:\n")
   879→	for _, info := range conflicting {
   880→		// Show prompt if available, otherwise fall back to session ID
   881→		if info.Prompt != "" {
   882→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  \"%s\"\n", info.Prompt)
   883→		} else {
   884→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session: %s\n", info.SessionID)
   885→		}
   886→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Local last entry:      %s\n", info.LocalTime.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   887→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Checkpoint last entry: %s\n", info.CheckpointTime.Local().Format("2006-01-02 15:04:05"))
   888→	}
   889→
   890→	// Show non-conflicting sessions with their status
   891→	if len(nonConflicting) > 0 {
   892→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nThese other session(s) will also be restored:\n")
   893→		for _, info := range nonConflicting {
   894→			statusText := StatusToText(info.Status)
   895→			if info.Prompt != "" {
   896→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  \"%s\" %s\n", info.Prompt, statusText)
   897→			} else {
   898→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session: %s %s\n", info.SessionID, statusText)
   899→			}
   900→		}
   901→	}
   902→
   903→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nOverwriting will lose the newer local entries.\n\n")
   904→
   905→	var confirmed bool
   906→	form := huh.NewForm(
   907→		huh.NewGroup(
   908→			huh.NewConfirm().
   909→				Title("Overwrite local session logs with checkpoint versions?").
   910→				Value(&confirmed),
   911→		),
   912→	)
   913→	if isAccessibleMode() {
   914→		form = form.WithAccessible(true)
   915→	}
   916→
   917→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   918→		if errors.Is(err, huh.ErrUserAborted) {
   919→			return false, nil
   920→		}
   921→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get confirmation: %w", err)
   922→	}
   923→
   924→	return confirmed, nil
   925→}
   926→

    29→
    30→// listCheckpoints returns all checkpoints from the metadata branch.
    31→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ListCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    32→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) listCheckpoints() ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    33→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    34→	if err != nil {
    35→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    36→	}
    37→
    38→	committed, err := store.ListCommitted(context.Background())
    39→	if err != nil {
    40→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to list committed checkpoints: %w", err)
    41→	}
    42→
    43→	// Convert from checkpoint.CommittedInfo to strategy.CheckpointInfo
    44→	result := make([]CheckpointInfo, 0, len(committed))
    45→	for _, c := range committed {
    46→		result = append(result, CheckpointInfo{
    47→			CheckpointID:     c.CheckpointID,
    48→			SessionID:        c.SessionID,
    49→			CreatedAt:        c.CreatedAt,
    50→			CheckpointsCount: c.CheckpointsCount,
    51→			FilesTouched:     c.FilesTouched,
    52→			Agent:            c.Agent,
    53→			IsTask:           c.IsTask,
    54→			ToolUseID:        c.ToolUseID,
    55→			SessionCount:     c.SessionCount,
    56→			SessionIDs:       c.SessionIDs,
    57→		})
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	return result, nil
    61→}
    62→
    63→// getCheckpointsForSession returns all checkpoints for a session ID.
    64→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointsForSession(sessionID string) ([]CheckpointInfo, error) {
    65→	all, err := s.listCheckpoints()
    66→	if err != nil {
    67→		return nil, err
    68→	}
    69→
    70→	var result []CheckpointInfo
    71→	for _, cp := range all {
    72→		if cp.SessionID == sessionID || strings.HasPrefix(cp.SessionID, sessionID) {
    73→			result = append(result, cp)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	if len(result) == 0 {
    78→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no checkpoints for session: %s", sessionID)
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	return result, nil
    82→}
    83→
    84→// getCheckpointLog returns the transcript for a specific checkpoint ID.
    85→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.ReadCommitted() for reading from entire/checkpoints/v1.
    86→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) getCheckpointLog(checkpointID id.CheckpointID) ([]byte, error) {
    87→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
    88→	if err != nil {
    89→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
    90→	}
    91→
    92→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), checkpointID)
    93→	if err != nil {
    94→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
    95→	}
    96→	if content == nil {
    97→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", checkpointID)
    98→	}
    99→	if len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   100→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("no transcript found for checkpoint: %s", checkpointID)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	return content.Transcript, nil
   104→}
   105→
   106→// CondenseSession condenses a session's shadow branch to permanent storage.
   107→// checkpointID is the 12-hex-char value from the Entire-Checkpoint trailer.
   108→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpoint_id[:2]>/<checkpoint_id[2:]>/
   109→// Uses checkpoint.GitStore.WriteCommitted for the git operations.
   110→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSession(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, state *SessionState) (*CondenseResult, error) {
   111→	// Get shadow branch
   112→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   113→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   114→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   115→	if err != nil {
   116→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("shadow branch not found: %w", err)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	// Extract session data from the shadow branch (with live transcript fallback).
   120→	// Use tracked files from session state instead of collecting all files from tree.
   121→	// Pass agent type to handle different transcript formats (JSONL for Claude, JSON for Gemini).
   122→	// Pass live transcript path so condensation reads the current file rather than a
   123→	// potentially stale shadow branch copy (SaveChanges may have been skipped if the
   124→	// last turn had no code changes).
   125→	// Pass CheckpointTranscriptStart for accurate token calculation (line offset for Claude, message index for Gemini).
   126→	sessionData, err := s.extractSessionData(repo, ref.Hash(), state.SessionID, state.FilesTouched, state.AgentType, state.TranscriptPath, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   127→	if err != nil {
   128→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to extract session data: %w", err)
   129→	}
   130→
   131→	// Get checkpoint store
   132→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   133→	if err != nil {
   134→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Get author info
   138→	authorName, authorEmail := GetGitAuthorFromRepo(repo)
   139→	attribution := calculateSessionAttributions(repo, ref, sessionData, state)
   140→	// Get current branch name
   141→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   142→
   143→	// Generate summary if enabled
   144→	var summary *cpkg.Summary
   145→	if settings.IsSummarizeEnabled() && len(sessionData.Transcript) > 0 {
   146→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   147→		summarizeCtx := logging.WithComponent(logCtx, "summarize")
   148→
   149→		// Scope transcript to this checkpoint's portion.
   150→		// SliceFromLine only works for JSONL (Claude Code) transcripts where
   151→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart is a line offset. For Gemini JSON transcripts,
   152→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart is a message index and the transcript is a single
   153→		// JSON blob, so line-based slicing would produce malformed content.
   154→		// The summarizer (ParseFromBytes) only supports JSONL, so skip scoping for Gemini.
   155→		var scopedTranscript []byte
   156→		if state.AgentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   157→			scopedTranscript = sessionData.Transcript
   158→		} else {
   159→			scopedTranscript = transcript.SliceFromLine(sessionData.Transcript, state.CheckpointTranscriptStart)
   160→		}
   161→		if len(scopedTranscript) > 0 {
   162→			var err error
   163→			summary, err = summarize.GenerateFromTranscript(summarizeCtx, scopedTranscript, sessionData.FilesTouched, nil)
   164→			if err != nil {
   165→				logging.Warn(summarizeCtx, "summary generation failed",
   166→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID),
   167→					slog.String("error", err.Error()))
   168→				// Continue without summary - non-blocking
   169→			} else {
   170→				logging.Info(summarizeCtx, "summary generated",
   171→					slog.String("session_id", state.SessionID))
   172→			}
   173→		}
   174→	}
   175→
   176→	// Write checkpoint metadata using the checkpoint store
   177→	if err := store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), cpkg.WriteCommittedOptions{
   178→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   179→		SessionID:                   state.SessionID,
   180→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameManualCommit,
   181→		Branch:                      branchName,
   182→		Transcript:                  sessionData.Transcript,
   183→		Prompts:                     sessionData.Prompts,
   184→		Context:                     sessionData.Context,
   185→		FilesTouched:                sessionData.FilesTouched,
   186→		CheckpointsCount:            state.StepCount,
   187→		EphemeralBranch:             shadowBranchName,
   188→		AuthorName:                  authorName,
   189→		AuthorEmail:                 authorEmail,
   190→		Agent:                       state.AgentType,
   191→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: state.TranscriptIdentifierAtStart,
   192→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   state.CheckpointTranscriptStart,
   193→		TokenUsage:                  sessionData.TokenUsage,
   194→		InitialAttribution:          attribution,
   195→		Summary:                     summary,
   196→	}); err != nil {
   197→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to write checkpoint metadata: %w", err)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	return &CondenseResult{
   201→		CheckpointID:         checkpointID,
   202→		SessionID:            state.SessionID,
   203→		CheckpointsCount:     state.StepCount,
   204→		FilesTouched:         sessionData.FilesTouched,
   205→		TotalTranscriptLines: sessionData.FullTranscriptLines,
   206→	}, nil
   207→}
   208→
   209→func calculateSessionAttributions(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef *plumbing.Reference, sessionData *ExtractedSessionData, state *SessionState) *cpkg.InitialAttribution {
   210→	// Calculate initial attribution using accumulated prompt attribution data.
   211→	// This uses user edits captured at each prompt start (before agent works),
   212→	// plus any user edits after the final checkpoint (shadow → head).
   213→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "attribution")
   214→	var attribution *cpkg.InitialAttribution
   215→	headRef, headErr := repo.Head()
   216→	if headErr != nil {
   217→		logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD",
   218→			slog.String("error", headErr.Error()))
   219→	} else {
   220→		headCommit, commitErr := repo.CommitObject(headRef.Hash())
   221→		if commitErr != nil {
   222→			logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD commit",
   223→				slog.String("error", commitErr.Error()))
   224→		} else {
   225→			headTree, treeErr := headCommit.Tree()
   226→			if treeErr != nil {
   227→				logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get HEAD tree",
   228→					slog.String("error", treeErr.Error()))
   229→			} else {
   230→				// Get shadow branch tree (checkpoint tree - what the agent wrote)
   231→				shadowCommit, shadowErr := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef.Hash())
   232→				if shadowErr != nil {
   233→					logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow commit",
   234→						slog.String("error", shadowErr.Error()),
   235→						slog.String("shadow_ref", shadowRef.Hash().String()))
   236→				} else {
   237→					shadowTree, shadowTreeErr := shadowCommit.Tree()
   238→					if shadowTreeErr != nil {
   239→						logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution skipped: failed to get shadow tree",
   240→							slog.String("error", shadowTreeErr.Error()))
   241→					} else {
   242→						// Get base tree (state before session started)
   243→						var baseTree *object.Tree
   244→						attrBase := state.AttributionBaseCommit
   245→						if attrBase == "" {
   246→							attrBase = state.BaseCommit // backward compat
   247→						}
   248→						if baseCommit, baseErr := repo.CommitObject(plumbing.NewHash(attrBase)); baseErr == nil {
   249→							if tree, baseTErr := baseCommit.Tree(); baseTErr == nil {
   250→								baseTree = tree
   251→							} else {
   252→								logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base tree unavailable",
   253→									slog.String("error", baseTErr.Error()))
   254→							}
   255→						} else {
   256→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "attribution: base commit unavailable",
   257→								slog.String("error", baseErr.Error()),
   258→								slog.String("attribution_base", attrBase))
   259→						}
   260→
   261→						// Log accumulated prompt attributions for debugging
   262→						var totalUserAdded, totalUserRemoved int
   263→						for i, pa := range state.PromptAttributions {
   264→							totalUserAdded += pa.UserLinesAdded
   265→							totalUserRemoved += pa.UserLinesRemoved
   266→							logging.Debug(logCtx, "prompt attribution data",
   267→								slog.Int("checkpoint", pa.CheckpointNumber),
   268→								slog.Int("user_added", pa.UserLinesAdded),
   269→								slog.Int("user_removed", pa.UserLinesRemoved),
   270→								slog.Int("agent_added", pa.AgentLinesAdded),
   271→								slog.Int("agent_removed", pa.AgentLinesRemoved),
   272→								slog.Int("index", i))
   273→						}
   274→
   275→						attribution = CalculateAttributionWithAccumulated(
   276→							baseTree,
   277→							shadowTree,
   278→							headTree,
   279→							sessionData.FilesTouched,
   280→							state.PromptAttributions,
   281→						)
   282→
   283→						if attribution != nil {
   284→							logging.Info(logCtx, "attribution calculated",
   285→								slog.Int("agent_lines", attribution.AgentLines),
   286→								slog.Int("human_added", attribution.HumanAdded),
   287→								slog.Int("human_modified", attribution.HumanModified),
   288→								slog.Int("human_removed", attribution.HumanRemoved),
   289→								slog.Int("total_committed", attribution.TotalCommitted),
   290→								slog.Float64("agent_percentage", attribution.AgentPercentage),
   291→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_added", totalUserAdded),
   292→								slog.Int("accumulated_user_removed", totalUserRemoved),
   293→								slog.Int("files_touched", len(sessionData.FilesTouched)))
   294→						}
   295→					}
   296→				}
   297→			}
   298→		}
   299→	}
   300→	return attribution
   301→}
   302→
   303→// extractSessionData extracts session data from the shadow branch.
   304→// filesTouched is the list of files tracked during the session (from SessionState.FilesTouched).
   305→// agentType identifies the agent (e.g., "Gemini CLI", "Claude Code") to determine transcript format.
   306→// liveTranscriptPath, when non-empty and readable, is preferred over the shadow branch copy.
   307→// This handles the case where SaveChanges was skipped (no code changes) but the transcript
   308→// continued growing — the shadow branch copy would be stale.
   309→// checkpointTranscriptStart is the line offset (Claude) or message index (Gemini) where the current checkpoint began.
   310→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) extractSessionData(repo *git.Repository, shadowRef plumbing.Hash, sessionID string, filesTouched []string, agentType agent.AgentType, liveTranscriptPath string, checkpointTranscriptStart int) (*ExtractedSessionData, error) {
   311→	commit, err := repo.CommitObject(shadowRef)
   312→	if err != nil {
   313→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit object: %w", err)
   314→	}
   315→
   316→	tree, err := commit.Tree()
   317→	if err != nil {
   318→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit tree: %w", err)
   319→	}
   320→
   321→	data := &ExtractedSessionData{}
   322→	// sessionID is already an "entire session ID" (with date prefix)
   323→	metadataDir := paths.SessionMetadataDirFromSessionID(sessionID)
   324→
   325→	// Extract transcript — prefer the live file when available, fall back to shadow branch.
   326→	// The shadow branch copy may be stale if the last turn ended without code changes
   327→	// (SaveChanges is only called when there are file modifications).
   328→	var fullTranscript string
   329→	if liveTranscriptPath != "" {
   330→		if liveData, readErr := os.ReadFile(liveTranscriptPath); readErr == nil && len(liveData) > 0 { //nolint:gosec // path from session state
   331→			fullTranscript = string(liveData)
   332→		}
   333→	}
   334→	if fullTranscript == "" {
   335→		// Fall back to shadow branch copy
   336→		if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName); fileErr == nil {
   337→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   338→				fullTranscript = content
   339→			}
   340→		} else if file, fileErr := tree.File(metadataDir + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileNameLegacy); fileErr == nil {
   341→			if content, contentErr := file.Contents(); contentErr == nil {
   342→				fullTranscript = content
   343→			}
   344→		}
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	// Process transcript based on agent type
   348→	if fullTranscript != "" {
   349→		data.Transcript = []byte(fullTranscript)
   350→		data.FullTranscriptLines = countTranscriptItems(agentType, fullTranscript)
   351→		data.Prompts = extractUserPrompts(agentType, fullTranscript)
   352→		data.Context = generateContextFromPrompts(data.Prompts)
   353→	}
   354→
   355→	// Use tracked files from session state (not all files in tree)
   356→	data.FilesTouched = filesTouched
   357→
   358→	// Calculate token usage from the extracted transcript portion
   359→	if len(data.Transcript) > 0 {
   360→		data.TokenUsage = calculateTokenUsage(agentType, data.Transcript, checkpointTranscriptStart)
   361→	}
   362→
   363→	return data, nil
   364→}
   365→
   366→// countTranscriptItems counts lines (JSONL) or messages (JSON) in a transcript.
   367→// For Claude Code and JSONL-based agents, this counts lines.
   368→// For Gemini CLI and JSON-based agents, this counts messages.
   369→// Returns 0 if the content is empty or malformed.
   370→func countTranscriptItems(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) int {
   371→	if content == "" {
   372→		return 0
   373→	}
   374→
   375→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   376→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   377→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript([]byte(content))
   378→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   379→			return len(transcript.Messages)
   380→		}
   381→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return 0
   382→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   383→			return 0
   384→		}
   385→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   389→	allLines := strings.Split(content, "\n")
   390→	// Trim trailing empty lines (from final \n in JSONL)
   391→	for len(allLines) > 0 && strings.TrimSpace(allLines[len(allLines)-1]) == "" {
   392→		allLines = allLines[:len(allLines)-1]
   393→	}
   394→	return len(allLines)
   395→}
   396→
   397→// extractUserPrompts extracts all user prompts from transcript content.
   398→// Returns prompts with IDE context tags stripped (e.g., <ide_opened_file>).
   399→func extractUserPrompts(agentType agent.AgentType, content string) []string {
   400→	if content == "" {
   401→		return nil
   402→	}
   403→
   404→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   405→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   406→		prompts, err := geminicli.ExtractAllUserPrompts([]byte(content))
   407→		if err == nil && len(prompts) > 0 {
   408→			// Strip IDE context tags for consistency with Claude Code handling
   409→			cleaned := make([]string, 0, len(prompts))
   410→			for _, prompt := range prompts {
   411→				if stripped := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(prompt); stripped != "" {
   412→					cleaned = append(cleaned, stripped)
   413→				}
   414→			}
   415→			return cleaned
   416→		}
   417→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return nil
   418→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   419→			return nil
   420→		}
   421→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   422→	}
   423→
   424→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   425→	return extractUserPromptsFromLines(strings.Split(content, "\n"))
   426→}
   427→
   428→// calculateTokenUsage calculates token usage from raw transcript data.
   429→// startOffset is the line number (Claude Code) or message index (Gemini CLI)
   430→// where the current checkpoint began, allowing calculation for only the portion
   431→// of the transcript since the last checkpoint.
   432→func calculateTokenUsage(agentType agent.AgentType, data []byte, startOffset int) *agent.TokenUsage {
   433→	if len(data) == 0 {
   434→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   435→	}
   436→
   437→	// Try Gemini format first if agentType is Gemini, or as fallback if Unknown
   438→	if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini || agentType == agent.AgentTypeUnknown {
   439→		// Attempt to parse as Gemini JSON
   440→		transcript, err := geminicli.ParseTranscript(data)
   441→		if err == nil && transcript != nil && len(transcript.Messages) > 0 {
   442→			return geminicli.CalculateTokenUsage(data, startOffset)
   443→		}
   444→		// If agentType is explicitly Gemini but parsing failed, return empty usage
   445→		if agentType == agent.AgentTypeGemini {
   446→			return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   447→		}
   448→		// Otherwise fall through to JSONL parsing for Unknown type
   449→	}
   450→
   451→	// Claude Code and other JSONL-based agents
   452→	lines, err := claudecode.ParseTranscript(data)
   453→	if err != nil || len(lines) == 0 {
   454→		return &agent.TokenUsage{}
   455→	}
   456→	// Slice transcript lines to only include checkpoint portion
   457→	if startOffset > 0 && startOffset < len(lines) {
   458→		lines = lines[startOffset:]
   459→	}
   460→	return claudecode.CalculateTokenUsage(lines)
   461→}
   462→
   463→// extractUserPromptsFromLines extracts user prompts from JSONL transcript lines.
   464→// IDE-injected context tags (like <ide_opened_file>) are stripped from the results.
   465→func extractUserPromptsFromLines(lines []string) []string {
   466→	var prompts []string
   467→	for _, line := range lines {
   468→		line = strings.TrimSpace(line)
   469→		if line == "" {
   470→			continue
   471→		}
   472→
   473→		var entry map[string]interface{}
   474→		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(line), &entry); err != nil {
   475→			continue
   476→		}
   477→
   478→		// Check for user message (supports both "human" and "user" types)
   479→		msgType, ok := entry["type"].(string)
   480→		if !ok || (msgType != "human" && msgType != "user") {
   481→			continue
   482→		}
   483→
   484→		// Extract message content
   485→		message, ok := entry["message"].(map[string]interface{})
   486→		if !ok {
   487→			continue
   488→		}
   489→
   490→		// Handle string content
   491→		if content, ok := message["content"].(string); ok && content != "" {
   492→			cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(content)
   493→			if cleaned != "" {
   494→				prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   495→			}
   496→			continue
   497→		}
   498→
   499→		// Handle array content (e.g., multiple text blocks from VSCode)
   500→		if arr, ok := message["content"].([]interface{}); ok {
   501→			var texts []string
   502→			for _, item := range arr {
   503→				if m, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {
   504→					if m["type"] == "text" {
   505→						if text, ok := m["text"].(string); ok {
   506→							texts = append(texts, text)
   507→						}
   508→					}
   509→				}
   510→			}
   511→			if len(texts) > 0 {
   512→				cleaned := textutil.StripIDEContextTags(strings.Join(texts, "\n\n"))
   513→				if cleaned != "" {
   514→					prompts = append(prompts, cleaned)
   515→				}
   516→			}
   517→		}
   518→	}
   519→	return prompts
   520→}
   521→
   522→// generateContextFromPrompts generates context.md content from a list of prompts.
   523→func generateContextFromPrompts(prompts []string) []byte {
   524→	if len(prompts) == 0 {
   525→		return nil
   526→	}
   527→
   528→	var buf strings.Builder
   529→	buf.WriteString("# Session Context\n\n")
   530→	buf.WriteString("## User Prompts\n\n")
   531→
   532→	for i, prompt := range prompts {
   533→		// Truncate very long prompts for readability
   534→		displayPrompt := prompt
   535→		if len(displayPrompt) > 500 {
   536→			displayPrompt = displayPrompt[:500] + "..."
   537→		}
   538→		buf.WriteString(fmt.Sprintf("### Prompt %d\n\n", i+1))
   539→		buf.WriteString(displayPrompt)
   540→		buf.WriteString("\n\n")
   541→	}
   542→
   543→	return []byte(buf.String())
   544→}
   545→
   546→// CondenseSessionByID force-condenses a session by its ID and cleans up.
   547→// This is used by "entire doctor" to salvage stuck sessions.
   548→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) CondenseSessionByID(sessionID string) error {
   549→	ctx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "condense-by-id")
   550→
   551→	// Load session state
   552→	state, err := s.loadSessionState(sessionID)
   553→	if err != nil {
   554→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to load session state: %w", err)
   555→	}
   556→	if state == nil {
   557→		return fmt.Errorf("session not found: %s", sessionID)
   558→	}
   559→
   560→	// Open repository
   561→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   562→	if err != nil {
   563→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   564→	}
   565→
   566→	// Generate a checkpoint ID
   567→	checkpointID, err := id.Generate()
   568→	if err != nil {
   569→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   570→	}
   571→
   572→	// Check if shadow branch exists (required for condensation)
   573→	shadowBranchName := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   574→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(shadowBranchName)
   575→	_, refErr := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   576→	hasShadowBranch := refErr == nil
   577→
   578→	if !hasShadowBranch {
   579→		// No shadow branch means no checkpoint data to condense.
   580→		// Just clean up the state file.
   581→		logging.Info(ctx, "no shadow branch for session, clearing state only",
   582→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   583→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   584→		)
   585→		if err := s.clearSessionState(sessionID); err != nil {
   586→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to clear session state: %w", err)
   587→		}
   588→		return nil
   589→	}
   590→
   591→	// Condense the session
   592→	result, err := s.CondenseSession(repo, checkpointID, state)
   593→	if err != nil {
   594→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to condense session: %w", err)
   595→	}
   596→
   597→	logging.Info(ctx, "session condensed by ID",
   598→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   599→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", result.CheckpointID.String()),
   600→		slog.Int("checkpoints_condensed", result.CheckpointsCount),
   601→	)
   602→
   603→	// Update session state: reset step count and transition to idle
   604→	state.StepCount = 0
   605→	state.CheckpointTranscriptStart = result.TotalTranscriptLines
   606→	state.Phase = session.PhaseIdle
   607→	state.LastCheckpointID = checkpointID
   608→	state.PendingCheckpointID = "" // Clear after condensation (amend handler uses LastCheckpointID)
   609→	state.AttributionBaseCommit = state.BaseCommit
   610→	state.PromptAttributions = nil
   611→	state.PendingPromptAttribution = nil
   612→
   613→	if err := s.saveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   614→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   615→	}
   616→
   617→	// Clean up shadow branch if no other sessions need it
   618→	if err := s.cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(repo, shadowBranchName, sessionID); err != nil {
   619→		logging.Warn(ctx, "failed to clean up shadow branch",
   620→			slog.String("shadow_branch", shadowBranchName),
   621→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   622→		)
   623→		// Non-fatal: condensation succeeded, shadow branch cleanup is best-effort
   624→	}
   625→
   626→	return nil
   627→}
   628→
   629→// cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused deletes a shadow branch if no other active sessions reference it.
   630→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) cleanupShadowBranchIfUnused(_ *git.Repository, shadowBranchName, excludeSessionID string) error {
   631→	// List all session states to check if any other session uses this shadow branch
   632→	allStates, err := s.listAllSessionStates()
   633→	if err != nil {
   634→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to list session states: %w", err)
   635→	}
   636→
   637→	for _, state := range allStates {
   638→		if state.SessionID == excludeSessionID {
   639→			continue
   640→		}
   641→		otherShadow := getShadowBranchNameForCommit(state.BaseCommit, state.WorktreeID)
   642→		if otherShadow == shadowBranchName && state.StepCount > 0 {
   643→			// Another session still needs this shadow branch
   644→			return nil
   645→		}
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	// No other sessions need it, delete the shadow branch via CLI
   649→	// (go-git v5's RemoveReference doesn't persist with packed refs/worktrees)
   650→	if err := DeleteBranchCLI(shadowBranchName); err != nil {
   651→		// Branch already gone is not an error
   652→		if errors.Is(err, ErrBranchNotFound) {
   653→			return nil
   654→		}
   655→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove shadow branch: %w", err)
   656→	}
   657→	return nil
   658→}
