    26→func newResumeCmd() *cobra.Command {
    27→	var force bool
    28→
    29→	cmd := &cobra.Command{
    30→		Use:   "resume <branch>",
    31→		Short: "Switch to a branch and resume its session",
    32→		Long: `Switch to a local branch and resume the agent session from its last commit.
    33→
    34→This command:
    35→1. Checks out the specified branch
    36→2. Finds the session ID from commits unique to this branch (not on main)
    37→3. Restores the session log if it doesn't exist locally
    38→4. Shows the command to resume the session
    39→
    40→If the branch doesn't exist locally but exists on origin, you'll be prompted
    41→to fetch it.
    42→
    43→If newer commits without checkpoints exist on the branch (e.g., after merging main
    44→or cherry-picking from elsewhere), this operation will reset your Git status to the
    45→most recent commit with a checkpoint.  You'll be prompted to confirm resuming in this case.`,
    46→		Args: cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    47→		RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    48→			if checkDisabledGuard(cmd.OutOrStdout()) {
    49→				return nil
    50→			}
    51→			return runResume(args[0], force)
    52→		},
    53→	}
    54→
    55→	cmd.Flags().BoolVarP(&force, "force", "f", false, "Resume from older checkpoint without confirmation")
    56→
    57→	return cmd
    58→}
    59→
    60→func runResume(branchName string, force bool) error {
    61→	// Check if we're already on this branch
    62→	currentBranch, err := GetCurrentBranch()
    63→	if err == nil && currentBranch == branchName {
    64→		// Already on the branch, skip checkout
    65→		return resumeFromCurrentBranch(branchName, force)
    66→	}
    67→
    68→	// Check if branch exists locally
    69→	exists, err := BranchExistsLocally(branchName)
    70→	if err != nil {
    71→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check branch: %w", err)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	if !exists {
    75→		// Branch doesn't exist locally, check if it exists on remote
    76→		remoteExists, err := BranchExistsOnRemote(branchName)
    77→		if err != nil {
    78→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check remote branch: %w", err)
    79→		}
    80→
    81→		if !remoteExists {
    82→			return fmt.Errorf("branch '%s' not found locally or on origin", branchName)
    83→		}
    84→
    85→		// Ask user if they want to fetch from remote
    86→		shouldFetch, err := promptFetchFromRemote(branchName)
    87→		if err != nil {
    88→			return err
    89→		}
    90→		if !shouldFetch {
    91→			return nil
    92→		}
    93→
    94→		// Fetch and checkout the remote branch
    95→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Fetching branch '%s' from origin...\n", branchName)
    96→		if err := FetchAndCheckoutRemoteBranch(branchName); err != nil {
    97→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Failed to checkout branch: %v\n", err)
    98→			return NewSilentError(errors.New("failed to checkout branch"))
    99→		}
   100→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Switched to branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   101→	} else {
   102→		// Branch exists locally, check for uncommitted changes before checkout
   103→		hasChanges, err := HasUncommittedChanges()
   104→		if err != nil {
   105→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to check for uncommitted changes: %w", err)
   106→		}
   107→		if hasChanges {
   108→			return errors.New("you have uncommitted changes. Please commit or stash them first")
   109→		}
   110→
   111→		// Checkout the branch
   112→		if err := CheckoutBranch(branchName); err != nil {
   113→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Failed to checkout branch: %v\n", err)
   114→			return NewSilentError(errors.New("failed to checkout branch"))
   115→		}
   116→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Switched to branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   117→	}
   118→
   119→	return resumeFromCurrentBranch(branchName, force)
   120→}
   121→
   122→func resumeFromCurrentBranch(branchName string, force bool) error {
   123→	repo, err := openRepository()
   124→	if err != nil {
   125→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   126→	}
   127→
   128→	// Find a commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer, looking at branch-only commits
   129→	result, err := findBranchCheckpoint(repo, branchName)
   130→	if err != nil {
   131→		return err
   132→	}
   133→	if result.checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   134→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No Entire checkpoint found on branch '%s'\n", branchName)
   135→		return nil
   136→	}
   137→
   138→	// If there are newer commits without checkpoints, ask for confirmation.
   139→	// Merge commits (e.g., from merging main) don't count as "work" and are skipped silently.
   140→	if result.newerCommitsExist && !force {
   141→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Found checkpoint in an older commit.\n")
   142→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "There are %d newer commit(s) on this branch without checkpoints.\n", result.newerCommitCount)
   143→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Checkpoint from: %s %s\n\n", result.commitHash[:7], firstLine(result.commitMessage))
   144→
   145→		shouldResume, err := promptResumeFromOlderCheckpoint()
   146→		if err != nil {
   147→			return err
   148→		}
   149→		if !shouldResume {
   150→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resume cancelled.\n")
   151→			return nil
   152→		}
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	checkpointID := result.checkpointID
   156→
   157→	// Get metadata branch tree for lookups
   158→	metadataTree, err := strategy.GetMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   159→	if err != nil {
   160→		// No local metadata branch, check if remote has it
   161→		return checkRemoteMetadata(repo, checkpointID)
   162→	}
   163→
   164→	// Look up metadata from sharded path
   165→	metadata, err := strategy.ReadCheckpointMetadata(metadataTree, checkpointID.Path())
   166→	if err != nil {
   167→		// Checkpoint exists in commit but no local metadata - check remote
   168→		return checkRemoteMetadata(repo, checkpointID)
   169→	}
   170→
   171→	return resumeSession(metadata.SessionID, checkpointID, force)
   172→}
   173→
   174→// branchCheckpointResult contains the result of searching for a checkpoint on a branch.
   175→type branchCheckpointResult struct {
   176→	checkpointID      id.CheckpointID
   177→	commitHash        string
   178→	commitMessage     string
   179→	newerCommitsExist bool // true if there are branch-only commits (not merge commits) without checkpoints
   180→	newerCommitCount  int  // count of branch-only commits without checkpoints
   181→}
   182→
   183→// findBranchCheckpoint finds the most recent commit with an Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   184→// among commits that are unique to this branch (not reachable from the default branch).
   185→// This handles the case where main has been merged into the feature branch.
   186→func findBranchCheckpoint(repo *git.Repository, branchName string) (*branchCheckpointResult, error) {
   187→	result := &branchCheckpointResult{}
   188→
   189→	// Get HEAD commit
   190→	head, err := repo.Head()
   191→	if err != nil {
   192→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   193→	}
   194→
   195→	headCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(head.Hash())
   196→	if err != nil {
   197→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD commit: %w", err)
   198→	}
   199→
   200→	// First, check if HEAD itself has a checkpoint (most common case)
   201→	if cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(headCommit.Message); found {
   202→		result.checkpointID = cpID
   203→		result.commitHash = head.Hash().String()
   204→		result.commitMessage = headCommit.Message
   205→		result.newerCommitsExist = false
   206→		return result, nil
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// HEAD doesn't have a checkpoint - find branch-only commits
   210→	// Get the default branch name
   211→	defaultBranch := getDefaultBranchFromRemote(repo)
   212→	if defaultBranch == "" {
   213→		// Fallback: try common names
   214→		for _, name := range []string{"main", "master"} {
   215→			if _, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(name), true); err == nil {
   216→				defaultBranch = name
   217→				break
   218→			}
   219→		}
   220→	}
   221→
   222→	// If we can't find a default branch, or we're on it, just walk all commits
   223→	if defaultBranch == "" || defaultBranch == branchName {
   224→		return findCheckpointInHistory(headCommit, nil), nil
   225→	}
   226→
   227→	// Get the default branch reference
   228→	defaultRef, err := repo.Reference(plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(defaultBranch), true)
   229→	if err != nil {
   230→		// Default branch doesn't exist locally, fall back to walking all commits
   231→		return findCheckpointInHistory(headCommit, nil), nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional fallback
   232→	}
   233→
   234→	defaultCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(defaultRef.Hash())
   235→	if err != nil {
   236→		// Can't get default commit, fall back to walking all commits
   237→		return findCheckpointInHistory(headCommit, nil), nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional fallback
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	// Find merge base
   241→	mergeBase, err := headCommit.MergeBase(defaultCommit)
   242→	if err != nil || len(mergeBase) == 0 {
   243→		// No common ancestor, fall back to walking all commits
   244→		return findCheckpointInHistory(headCommit, nil), nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional fallback
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Walk from HEAD to merge base, looking for checkpoint
   248→	return findCheckpointInHistory(headCommit, &mergeBase[0].Hash), nil
   249→}
   250→
   251→// findCheckpointInHistory walks commit history from start looking for a checkpoint trailer.
   252→// If stopAt is provided, stops when reaching that commit (exclusive).
   253→// Returns the first checkpoint found and info about commits between HEAD and the checkpoint.
   254→// It distinguishes between merge commits (bringing in other branches) and regular commits
   255→// (actual branch work) to avoid false warnings after merging main.
   256→func findCheckpointInHistory(start *object.Commit, stopAt *plumbing.Hash) *branchCheckpointResult {
   257→	result := &branchCheckpointResult{}
   258→	branchWorkCommits := 0 // Regular commits without checkpoints (actual work)
   259→	const maxCommits = 100 // Limit search depth
   260→	totalChecked := 0
   261→
   262→	current := start
   263→	for current != nil && totalChecked < maxCommits {
   264→		// Stop if we've reached the boundary
   265→		if stopAt != nil && current.Hash == *stopAt {
   266→			break
   267→		}
   268→
   269→		// Check for checkpoint trailer
   270→		if cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(current.Message); found {
   271→			result.checkpointID = cpID
   272→			result.commitHash = current.Hash.String()
   273→			result.commitMessage = current.Message
   274→			// Only warn about branch work commits, not merge commits
   275→			result.newerCommitsExist = branchWorkCommits > 0
   276→			result.newerCommitCount = branchWorkCommits
   277→			return result
   278→		}
   279→
   280→		// Only count regular commits (not merge commits) as "branch work"
   281→		if current.NumParents() <= 1 {
   282→			branchWorkCommits++
   283→		}
   284→
   285→		totalChecked++
   286→
   287→		// Move to parent (first parent for merge commits - follows the main line)
   288→		if current.NumParents() == 0 {
   289→			break
   290→		}
   291→		parent, err := current.Parent(0)
   292→		if err != nil {
   293→			// Can't get parent, treat as end of history
   294→			break
   295→		}
   296→		current = parent
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	// No checkpoint found
   300→	return result
   301→}
   302→
   303→// promptResumeFromOlderCheckpoint asks the user if they want to resume from an older checkpoint.
   304→func promptResumeFromOlderCheckpoint() (bool, error) {
   305→	var confirmed bool
   306→
   307→	form := NewAccessibleForm(
   308→		huh.NewGroup(
   309→			huh.NewConfirm().
   310→				Title("Resume from this older checkpoint?").
   311→				Value(&confirmed),
   312→		),
   313→	)
   314→
   315→	if err := form.Run(); err != nil {
   316→		if errors.Is(err, huh.ErrUserAborted) {
   317→			return false, nil
   318→		}
   319→		return false, fmt.Errorf("failed to get confirmation: %w", err)
   320→	}
   321→
   322→	return confirmed, nil
   323→}
   324→
   325→// checkRemoteMetadata checks if checkpoint metadata exists on origin/entire/checkpoints/v1
   326→// and automatically fetches it if available.
   327→func checkRemoteMetadata(repo *git.Repository, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) error {
   328→	// Try to get remote metadata branch tree
   329→	remoteTree, err := strategy.GetRemoteMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   330→	if err != nil {
   331→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Checkpoint '%s' found in commit but session metadata not available\n", checkpointID)
   332→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "The entire/checkpoints/v1 branch may not exist locally or on the remote.\n")
   333→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Informational message, not a fatal error
   334→	}
   335→
   336→	// Check if the checkpoint exists on the remote
   337→	metadata, err := strategy.ReadCheckpointMetadata(remoteTree, checkpointID.Path())
   338→	if err != nil {
   339→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Checkpoint '%s' found in commit but session metadata not available\n", checkpointID)
   340→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // Informational message, not a fatal error
   341→	}
   342→
   343→	// Metadata exists on remote but not locally - fetch it automatically
   344→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Fetching session metadata from origin...\n")
   345→	if err := FetchMetadataBranch(); err != nil {
   346→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Failed to fetch metadata: %v\n", err)
   347→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "You can try manually: git fetch origin entire/checkpoints/v1:entire/checkpoints/v1\n")
   348→		return NewSilentError(errors.New("failed to fetch metadata"))
   349→	}
   350→
   351→	// Now resume the session with the fetched metadata
   352→	return resumeSession(metadata.SessionID, checkpointID, false)
   353→}
   354→
   355→// resumeSession restores and displays the resume command for a specific session.
   356→// For multi-session checkpoints, restores ALL sessions and shows commands for each.
   357→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   358→func resumeSession(sessionID string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, force bool) error {
   359→	// Get the current agent (auto-detect or use default)
   360→	ag, err := agent.Detect()
   361→	if err != nil {
   362→		ag = agent.Default()
   363→		if ag == nil {
   364→			return fmt.Errorf("no agent available: %w", err)
   365→		}
   366→	}
   367→
   368→	// Initialize logging context with agent
   369→	ctx := logging.WithAgent(logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "resume"), ag.Name())
   370→
   371→	logging.Debug(ctx, "resume session started",
   372→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   373→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   374→	)
   375→
   376→	// Get repo root for session directory lookup
   377→	// Use repo root instead of CWD because Claude stores sessions per-repo,
   378→	// and running from a subdirectory would look up the wrong session directory
   379→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   380→	if err != nil {
   381→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   382→	}
   383→
   384→	sessionDir, err := ag.GetSessionDir(repoRoot)
   385→	if err != nil {
   386→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to determine session directory: %w", err)
   387→	}
   388→
   389→	// Create directory if it doesn't exist
   390→	if err := os.MkdirAll(sessionDir, 0o700); err != nil {
   391→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session directory: %w", err)
   392→	}
   393→
   394→	// Get strategy and restore sessions using full checkpoint data
   395→	strat := GetStrategy()
   396→
   397→	// Use RestoreLogsOnly via LogsOnlyRestorer interface for multi-session support
   398→	if restorer, ok := strat.(strategy.LogsOnlyRestorer); ok {
   399→		// Create a logs-only rewind point to trigger full multi-session restore
   400→		point := strategy.RewindPoint{
   401→			IsLogsOnly:   true,
   402→			CheckpointID: checkpointID,
   403→		}
   404→
   405→		if err := restorer.RestoreLogsOnly(point, force); err != nil {
   406→			// Fall back to single-session restore
   407→			return resumeSingleSession(ctx, ag, sessionID, checkpointID, sessionDir, repoRoot, force)
   408→		}
   409→
   410→		// Get checkpoint metadata to show all sessions
   411→		repo, err := openRepository()
   412→		if err != nil {
   413→			// Just show the primary session - graceful fallback
   414→			agentSID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   415→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session: %s\n", sessionID)
   416→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   417→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSID))
   418→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Graceful fallback to single session
   419→		}
   420→
   421→		metadataTree, err := strategy.GetMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   422→		if err != nil {
   423→			agentSID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   424→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session: %s\n", sessionID)
   425→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   426→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSID))
   427→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Graceful fallback to single session
   428→		}
   429→
   430→		metadata, err := strategy.ReadCheckpointMetadata(metadataTree, checkpointID.Path())
   431→		if err != nil || metadata.SessionCount <= 1 {
   432→			// Single session or can't read metadata - show standard single session output
   433→			agentSID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   434→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session: %s\n", sessionID)
   435→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   436→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSID))
   437→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Graceful fallback to single session
   438→		}
   439→
   440→		// Multi-session: show all resume commands with prompts
   441→		checkpointPath := checkpointID.Path()
   442→		sessionPrompts := strategy.ReadAllSessionPromptsFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath, metadata.SessionCount, metadata.SessionIDs)
   443→
   444→		logging.Debug(ctx, "resume session completed (multi-session)",
   445→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   446→			slog.Int("session_count", metadata.SessionCount),
   447→		)
   448→
   449→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nRestored %d sessions. To continue, run:\n", metadata.SessionCount)
   450→		for i, sid := range metadata.SessionIDs {
   451→			agentSID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sid)
   452→			cmd := ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSID)
   453→
   454→			var prompt string
   455→			if i < len(sessionPrompts) {
   456→				prompt = sessionPrompts[i]
   457→			}
   458→
   459→			if i == len(metadata.SessionIDs)-1 {
   460→				if prompt != "" {
   461→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s  # %s (most recent)\n", cmd, prompt)
   462→				} else {
   463→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s  # (most recent)\n", cmd)
   464→				}
   465→			} else {
   466→				if prompt != "" {
   467→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s  # %s\n", cmd, prompt)
   468→				} else {
   469→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", cmd)
   470→				}
   471→			}
   472→		}
   473→
   474→		return nil
   475→	}
   476→
   477→	// Strategy doesn't support LogsOnlyRestorer, fall back to single session
   478→	return resumeSingleSession(ctx, ag, sessionID, checkpointID, sessionDir, repoRoot, force)
   479→}
   480→
   481→// resumeSingleSession restores a single session (fallback when multi-session restore fails).
   482→// Always overwrites existing session logs to ensure consistency with checkpoint state.
   483→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local log has newer timestamps.
   484→func resumeSingleSession(ctx context.Context, ag agent.Agent, sessionID string, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, sessionDir, repoRoot string, force bool) error {
   485→	agentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)
   486→	sessionLogPath := filepath.Join(sessionDir, agentSessionID+".jsonl")
   487→
   488→	if checkpointID.IsEmpty() {
   489→		logging.Debug(ctx, "resume session: empty checkpoint ID",
   490→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   491→		)
   492→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session '%s' found in commit trailer but session log not available\n", sessionID)
   493→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   494→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSessionID))
   495→		return nil
   496→	}
   497→
   498→	logContent, _, err := checkpoint.LookupSessionLog(checkpointID)
   499→	if err != nil {
   500→		if errors.Is(err, checkpoint.ErrCheckpointNotFound) || errors.Is(err, checkpoint.ErrNoTranscript) {
   501→			logging.Debug(ctx, "resume session completed (no metadata)",
   502→				slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   503→				slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   504→			)
   505→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session '%s' found in commit trailer but session log not available\n", sessionID)
   506→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   507→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSessionID))
   508→			return nil
   509→		}
   510→		logging.Error(ctx, "resume session failed",
   511→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   512→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   513→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   514→		)
   515→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session log: %w", err)
   516→	}
   517→
   518→	// Check if local file has newer timestamps than checkpoint
   519→	if !force {
   520→		localTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(sessionLogPath)
   521→		checkpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(logContent)
   522→		status := strategy.ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)
   523→
   524→		if status == strategy.StatusLocalNewer {
   525→			sessions := []strategy.SessionRestoreInfo{{
   526→				SessionID:      sessionID,
   527→				Status:         status,
   528→				LocalTime:      localTime,
   529→				CheckpointTime: checkpointTime,
   530→			}}
   531→			shouldOverwrite, promptErr := strategy.PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)
   532→			if promptErr != nil {
   533→				return fmt.Errorf("failed to get confirmation: %w", promptErr)
   534→			}
   535→			if !shouldOverwrite {
   536→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resume cancelled. Local session log preserved.\n")
   537→				return nil
   538→			}
   539→		}
   540→	}
   541→
   542→	// Create an AgentSession with the native data
   543→	agentSession := &agent.AgentSession{
   544→		SessionID:  agentSessionID,
   545→		AgentName:  ag.Name(),
   546→		RepoPath:   repoRoot,
   547→		SessionRef: sessionLogPath,
   548→		NativeData: logContent,
   549→	}
   550→
   551→	// Write the session using the agent's WriteSession method
   552→	if err := ag.WriteSession(agentSession); err != nil {
   553→		logging.Error(ctx, "resume session failed during write",
   554→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   555→			slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   556→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   557→		)
   558→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session: %w", err)
   559→	}
   560→
   561→	logging.Debug(ctx, "resume session completed",
   562→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),
   563→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   564→	)
   565→
   566→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session restored to: %s\n", sessionLogPath)
   567→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session: %s\n", sessionID)
   568→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")
   569→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSessionID))
   570→
   571→	return nil
   572→}
   573→
   574→func promptFetchFromRemote(branchName string) (bool, error) {

   578→		huh.NewGroup(
   579→			huh.NewConfirm().
   580→				Title(fmt.Sprintf("Branch '%s' not found locally. Fetch from origin?", branchName)).
   581→				Value(&confirmed),

   595→// firstLine returns the first line of a string
   596→func firstLine(s string) string {
   597→	for i, c := range s {
   598→		if c == '\n' {
   599→			return s[:i]
   600→		}
   601→	}
   602→	return s
   603→}

    27→
    28→// isNotFoundError checks if an error represents a "not found" condition in go-git.
    29→// This includes entry not found, file not found, directory not found, and object not found.
    30→func isNotFoundError(err error) bool {
    31→	return errors.Is(err, object.ErrEntryNotFound) ||
    32→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrFileNotFound) ||
    33→		errors.Is(err, object.ErrDirectoryNotFound) ||
    34→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrObjectNotFound) ||
    35→		errors.Is(err, plumbing.ErrReferenceNotFound)
    36→}
    37→
    38→// commitOrHead attempts to create a commit. If the commit would be empty (files already
    39→// committed), it returns HEAD hash instead. This handles the case where files were
    40→// modified during a session but already committed by the user before the hook runs.
    41→func commitOrHead(repo *git.Repository, worktree *git.Worktree, msg string, author *object.Signature) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
    42→	commitHash, err := worktree.Commit(msg, &git.CommitOptions{Author: author})
    43→	if errors.Is(err, git.ErrEmptyCommit) {
    44→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No changes to commit (files already committed)\n")
    45→		head, err := repo.Head()
    46→		if err != nil {
    47→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
    48→		}
    49→		return head.Hash(), nil
    50→	}
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit: %w", err)
    53→	}
    54→	return commitHash, nil
    55→}
    56→
    57→// AutoCommitStrategy implements the auto-commit strategy:
    58→// - Code changes are committed to the active branch (like commit strategy)
    59→// - Session logs are committed to a shadow branch (like manual-commit strategy)
    60→// - Code commits can reference the shadow branch via trailers
    61→type AutoCommitStrategy struct {
    62→	// checkpointStore manages checkpoint data on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
    63→	checkpointStore *checkpoint.GitStore
    64→	// checkpointStoreOnce ensures thread-safe lazy initialization
    65→	checkpointStoreOnce sync.Once
    66→	// checkpointStoreErr captures any error during initialization
    67→	checkpointStoreErr error
    68→}
    69→
    70→// getCheckpointStore returns the checkpoint store, initializing it lazily if needed.
    71→// Thread-safe via sync.Once.
    72→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) getCheckpointStore() (*checkpoint.GitStore, error) {
    73→	s.checkpointStoreOnce.Do(func() {
    74→		repo, err := OpenRepository()
    75→		if err != nil {
    76→			s.checkpointStoreErr = fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
    77→			return
    78→		}
    79→		s.checkpointStore = checkpoint.NewGitStore(repo)
    80→	})
    81→	return s.checkpointStore, s.checkpointStoreErr
    82→}
    83→
    84→// NewAutoCommitStrategy creates a new AutoCommitStrategy instance
    85→//
    86→
    87→func NewAutoCommitStrategy() Strategy { //nolint:ireturn // already present in codebase
    88→	return &AutoCommitStrategy{}
    89→}
    90→
    91→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Name() string {
    92→	return StrategyNameAutoCommit
    93→}
    94→
    95→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Description() string {
    96→	return "Auto-commits code to active branch with metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1"
    97→}
    98→
    99→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ValidateRepository() error {
   100→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   101→	if err != nil {
   102→		return fmt.Errorf("not a git repository: %w", err)
   103→	}
   104→
   105→	_, err = repo.Worktree()
   106→	if err != nil {
   107→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to access worktree: %w", err)
   108→	}
   109→
   110→	return nil
   111→}
   112→
   113→// PrePush is called by the git pre-push hook before pushing to a remote.
   114→// It pushes the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch alongside the user's push.
   115→// Configuration options (stored in .entire/settings.json under strategy_options.push_sessions):
   116→//   - "auto": always push automatically
   117→//   - "prompt" (default): ask user with option to enable auto
   118→//   - "false"/"off"/"no": never push
   119→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PrePush(remote string) error {
   120→	return pushSessionsBranchCommon(remote, paths.MetadataBranchName)
   121→}
   122→
   123→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveChanges(ctx SaveContext) error {
   124→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   125→	if err != nil {
   126→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   127→	}
   128→
   129→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this commit
   130→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   131→	if err != nil {
   132→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   133→	}
   134→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   135→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   136→	// If metadata commit fails after this, the code commit exists but GetRewindPoints
   137→	// already handles missing metadata gracefully (skips commits without metadata).
   138→	codeResult, err := s.commitCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   139→	if err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit code to active branch: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// If no code commit was created (no changes), skip metadata creation
   144→	// This prevents orphaned metadata commits that don't correspond to any code commit
   145→	if !codeResult.Created {
   146→		logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   147→		logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint skipped (no changes)",
   148→			slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   149→			slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   150→		)
   151→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Skipped checkpoint (no changes since last commit)\n")
   152→		return nil
   153→	}
   154→
   155→	// Step 2: Commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using sharded path
   156→	// Path is <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/ for direct lookup
   157→	_, err = s.commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   158→	if err != nil {
   159→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   160→	}
   161→
   162→	// Log checkpoint creation
   163→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   164→	logging.Info(logCtx, "checkpoint saved",
   165→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   166→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "session"),
   167→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   168→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   169→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   170→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   171→	)
   172→
   173→	return nil
   174→}
   175→
   176→// commitCodeResult contains the result of committing code to the active branch.
   177→type commitCodeResult struct {
   178→	CommitHash plumbing.Hash
   179→	Created    bool // True if a new commit was created, false if skipped (no changes)
   180→}
   181→
   182→// commitCodeToActive commits code changes to the active branch.
   183→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   184→// Returns the result containing commit hash and whether a commit was created.
   185→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx SaveContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (commitCodeResult, error) {
   186→	// Check if there are any code changes to commit
   187→	if len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.NewFiles) == 0 && len(ctx.DeletedFiles) == 0 {
   188→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit to active branch\n")
   189→		// Return current HEAD hash but mark as not created
   190→		head, err := repo.Head()
   191→		if err != nil {
   192→			return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   193→		}
   194→		return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: head.Hash(), Created: false}, nil
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   198→	if err != nil {
   199→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   200→	}
   201→
   202→	// Get HEAD hash before commit to detect if commitOrHead actually creates a new commit
   203→	// (commitOrHead returns HEAD hash without error when git.ErrEmptyCommit occurs)
   204→	headBefore, err := repo.Head()
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return commitCodeResult{}, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→
   209→	// Stage code changes
   210→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForSession)
   211→
   212→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   213→	commitMsg := ctx.CommitMessage + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   214→
   215→	author := &object.Signature{
   216→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   217→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   218→		When:  time.Now(),
   219→	}
   220→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   221→	if err != nil {
   222→		return commitCodeResult{}, err
   223→	}
   224→
   225→	// Check if a new commit was actually created by comparing with HEAD before
   226→	created := commitHash != headBefore.Hash()
   227→	if created {
   228→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed code changes to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   229→	}
   230→	return commitCodeResult{CommitHash: commitHash, Created: created}, nil
   231→}
   232→
   233→// commitMetadataToMetadataBranch commits session metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   234→// Metadata is stored at sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/
   235→// This allows direct lookup from the checkpoint ID trailer on the code commit.
   236→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   237→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx SaveContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   238→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
   244→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
   245→
   246→	// Get current branch name
   247→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   248→
   249→	// Combine all file changes into FilesTouched (same as manual-commit)
   250→	filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   251→
   252→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   253→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   254→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   255→		SessionID:                   sessionID,
   256→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameAutoCommit, // Use new strategy name
   257→		Branch:                      branchName,
   258→		MetadataDir:                 ctx.MetadataDirAbs, // Copy all files from metadata dir
   259→		AuthorName:                  ctx.AuthorName,
   260→		AuthorEmail:                 ctx.AuthorEmail,
   261→		Agent:                       ctx.AgentType,
   262→		TranscriptIdentifierAtStart: ctx.StepTranscriptIdentifier,
   263→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   ctx.StepTranscriptStart,
   264→		TokenUsage:                  ctx.TokenUsage,
   265→		CheckpointsCount:            1,            // Each auto-commit checkpoint = 1
   266→		FilesTouched:                filesTouched, // Track modified files (same as manual-commit)
   267→	})
   268→	if err != nil {
   269→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write committed checkpoint: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed session metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   273→	return plumbing.ZeroHash, nil // Commit hash not needed by callers
   274→}
   275→
   276→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetRewindPoints(limit int) ([]RewindPoint, error) {
   277→	// For auto-commit strategy, rewind points are found by looking for Entire-Checkpoint trailers
   278→	// in the current branch's commit history. The checkpoint ID provides direct lookup
   279→	// to metadata on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   280→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   281→	if err != nil {
   282→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   283→	}
   284→
   285→	head, err := repo.Head()
   286→	if err != nil {
   287→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   288→	}
   289→
   290→	// Get metadata branch tree for lookups
   291→	metadataTree, err := GetMetadataBranchTree(repo)
   292→	if err != nil {
   293→		// No metadata branch yet is fine
   294→		return []RewindPoint{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // Expected when no metadata exists
   295→	}
   296→
   297→	// Get the main branch commit hash to determine branch-only commits
   298→	mainBranchHash := GetMainBranchHash(repo)
   299→
   300→	// Walk current branch history looking for commits with checkpoint trailers
   301→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{
   302→		From:  head.Hash(),
   303→		Order: git.LogOrderCommitterTime,
   304→	})
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var points []RewindPoint
   310→	count := 0
   311→
   312→	err = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error {
   313→		if count >= logsOnlyScanLimit || len(points) >= limit {
   314→			return errStop
   315→		}
   316→		count++
   317→
   318→		// Check for Entire-Checkpoint trailer
   319→		cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message)
   320→		if !found {
   321→			return nil
   322→		}
   323→
   324→		// Look up metadata from sharded path
   325→		checkpointPath := cpID.Path()
   326→		metadata, err := ReadCheckpointMetadata(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   327→		if err != nil {
   328→			// Checkpoint exists in commit but no metadata found - skip this commit
   329→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Intentional: skip commits without metadata
   330→		}
   331→
   332→		message := strings.Split(c.Message, "\n")[0]
   333→
   334→		// Determine if this is a full rewind or logs-only
   335→		// Full rewind is allowed if commit is only on this branch (not reachable from main)
   336→		isLogsOnly := false
   337→		if mainBranchHash != plumbing.ZeroHash {
   338→			if IsAncestorOf(repo, c.Hash, mainBranchHash) {
   339→				isLogsOnly = true
   340→			}
   341→		}
   342→
   343→		// Build metadata path - for task checkpoints, include the task path
   344→		metadataDir := checkpointPath
   345→		if metadata.IsTask && metadata.ToolUseID != "" {
   346→			metadataDir = checkpointPath + "/tasks/" + metadata.ToolUseID
   347→		}
   348→
   349→		// Read session prompt from metadata tree
   350→		sessionPrompt := ReadSessionPromptFromTree(metadataTree, checkpointPath)
   351→
   352→		points = append(points, RewindPoint{
   353→			ID:               c.Hash.String(),
   354→			Message:          message,
   355→			MetadataDir:      metadataDir,
   356→			Date:             c.Author.When,
   357→			IsLogsOnly:       isLogsOnly,
   358→			CheckpointID:     cpID,
   359→			IsTaskCheckpoint: metadata.IsTask,
   360→			ToolUseID:        metadata.ToolUseID,
   361→			Agent:            metadata.Agent,
   362→			SessionID:        metadata.SessionID,
   363→			SessionPrompt:    sessionPrompt,
   364→		})
   365→
   366→		return nil
   367→	})
   368→
   369→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   370→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate commits: %w", err)
   371→	}
   372→
   373→	return points, nil
   374→}
   375→
   376→// findTaskMetadataPathForCommit looks up the task metadata path for a task checkpoint commit
   377→// by searching the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch commit history for the checkpoint directory.
   378→// Returns ("", nil) if metadata is not found - this is expected for commits without metadata.
   379→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findTaskMetadataPathForCommit(repo *git.Repository, commitSHA, toolUseID string) (string, error) {
   380→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   381→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   382→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   383→	if err != nil {
   384→		if isNotFoundError(err) {
   385→			return "", nil // No metadata branch yet
   386→		}
   387→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch: %w", err)
   388→	}
   389→
   390→	// Search commit history for a commit referencing this code commit SHA and tool use ID
   391→	shortSHA := commitSHA
   392→	if len(shortSHA) > 7 {
   393→		shortSHA = shortSHA[:7]
   394→	}
   395→
   396→	iter, err := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: ref.Hash()})
   397→	if err != nil {
   398→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get commit log: %w", err)
   399→	}
   400→
   401→	var foundTaskPath string
   402→	err = iter.ForEach(func(commit *object.Commit) error {
   403→		// Check if commit message contains "Commit: <sha>" and the tool use ID
   404→		if strings.Contains(commit.Message, "Commit: "+shortSHA) &&
   405→			strings.Contains(commit.Message, toolUseID) {
   406→			// Parse task metadata trailer
   407→			if taskPath, found := trailers.ParseTaskMetadata(commit.Message); found {
   408→				foundTaskPath = taskPath
   409→				return errStop // Found it
   410→			}
   411→		}
   412→		return nil
   413→	})
   414→	if err != nil && !errors.Is(err, errStop) {
   415→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to iterate commits: %w", err)
   416→	}
   417→
   418→	return foundTaskPath, nil
   419→}
   420→
   421→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) Rewind(point RewindPoint) error {
   422→	commitHash := plumbing.NewHash(point.ID)
   423→	shortID, err := HardResetWithProtection(commitHash)
   424→	if err != nil {
   425→		return err
   426→	}
   427→
   428→	fmt.Println()
   429→	fmt.Printf("Reset to commit %s\n", shortID)
   430→	fmt.Println()
   431→
   432→	return nil
   433→}
   434→
   435→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) CanRewind() (bool, string, error) {
   436→	return checkCanRewind()
   437→}
   438→
   439→// PreviewRewind returns what will happen if rewinding to the given point.
   440→// For auto-commit strategy, this returns nil since git reset doesn't delete untracked files.
   441→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) PreviewRewind(_ RewindPoint) (*RewindPreview, error) {
   442→	// Auto-commit uses git reset --hard which doesn't affect untracked files
   443→	// Return empty preview to indicate no untracked files will be deleted
   444→	return &RewindPreview{}, nil
   445→}
   446→
   447→// EnsureSetup ensures the strategy's required setup is in place.
   448→// For auto-commit strategy:
   449→// - Ensure .entire/.gitignore has all required entries
   450→// - Create orphan entire/checkpoints/v1 branch if it doesn't exist
   451→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) EnsureSetup() error {
   452→	if err := EnsureEntireGitignore(); err != nil {
   453→		return err
   454→	}
   455→
   456→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   457→	if err != nil {
   458→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   459→	}
   460→
   461→	// Ensure the entire/checkpoints/v1 orphan branch exists
   462→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   463→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   464→	}
   465→
   466→	return nil
   467→}
   468→
   469→// GetSessionInfo returns session information for linking commits.
   470→// For auto-commit strategy, we don't track active sessions - metadata is stored on
   471→// entire/checkpoints/v1 branch when SaveChanges is called. Active branch commits
   472→// are kept clean (no trailers), so this returns ErrNoSession.
   473→// Use ListSessions() or GetSession() to retrieve session info from the metadata branch.
   474→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionInfo() (*SessionInfo, error) {
   475→	// Dual strategy doesn't track active sessions like shadow does.
   476→	// Session metadata is stored on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch and can be
   477→	// retrieved via ListSessions() or GetSession().
   478→	return nil, ErrNoSession
   479→}
   480→
   481→// SaveTaskCheckpoint creates a checkpoint commit for a completed task.
   482→// For auto-commit strategy:
   483→// 1. Commit code changes to active branch (no trailers - clean history)
   484→// 2. Commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch with checkpoint format
   485→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) SaveTaskCheckpoint(ctx TaskCheckpointContext) error {
   486→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   487→	if err != nil {
   488→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   489→	}
   490→
   491→	// Ensure entire/checkpoints/v1 branch exists
   492→	if err := EnsureMetadataBranch(repo); err != nil {
   493→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to ensure metadata branch: %w", err)
   494→	}
   495→
   496→	// Generate checkpoint ID for this task checkpoint
   497→	cpID, err := id.Generate()
   498→	if err != nil {
   499→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to generate checkpoint ID: %w", err)
   500→	}
   501→
   502→	// Step 1: Commit code changes to active branch with checkpoint ID trailer
   503→	// We do code first to avoid orphaned metadata if this step fails.
   504→	_, err = s.commitTaskCodeToActive(repo, ctx, cpID)
   505→	if err != nil {
   506→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit task code to active branch: %w", err)
   507→	}
   508→
   509→	// Step 2: Commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch at sharded path
   510→	_, err = s.commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo, ctx, cpID)
   511→	if err != nil {
   512→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to commit task metadata to entire/checkpoints/v1 branch: %w", err)
   513→	}
   514→
   515→	// Log task checkpoint creation
   516→	logCtx := logging.WithComponent(context.Background(), "checkpoint")
   517→	attrs := []any{
   518→		slog.String("strategy", "auto-commit"),
   519→		slog.String("checkpoint_type", "task"),
   520→		slog.String("checkpoint_id", cpID.String()),
   521→		slog.String("checkpoint_uuid", ctx.CheckpointUUID),
   522→		slog.String("tool_use_id", ctx.ToolUseID),
   523→		slog.String("subagent_type", ctx.SubagentType),
   524→		slog.Int("modified_files", len(ctx.ModifiedFiles)),
   525→		slog.Int("new_files", len(ctx.NewFiles)),
   526→		slog.Int("deleted_files", len(ctx.DeletedFiles)),
   527→	}
   528→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   529→		attrs = append(attrs,
   530→			slog.Bool("is_incremental", true),
   531→			slog.String("incremental_type", ctx.IncrementalType),
   532→			slog.Int("incremental_sequence", ctx.IncrementalSequence),
   533→		)
   534→	}
   535→	logging.Info(logCtx, "task checkpoint saved", attrs...)
   536→
   537→	return nil
   538→}
   539→
   540→// commitTaskCodeToActive commits task code changes to the active branch.
   541→// Adds an Entire-Checkpoint trailer for metadata lookup that survives amend/rebase.
   542→// Skips commit creation if there are no file changes.
   543→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskCodeToActive(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskCheckpointContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   544→	hasFileChanges := len(ctx.ModifiedFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.NewFiles) > 0 || len(ctx.DeletedFiles) > 0
   545→
   546→	// If no file changes, skip code commit
   547→	if !hasFileChanges {
   548→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "No code changes to commit for task checkpoint\n")
   549→		// Return current HEAD hash so metadata can still be stored
   550→		head, err := repo.Head()
   551→		if err != nil {
   552→			return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   553→		}
   554→		return head.Hash(), nil
   555→	}
   556→
   557→	worktree, err := repo.Worktree()
   558→	if err != nil {
   559→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get worktree: %w", err)
   560→	}
   561→
   562→	// Stage code changes
   563→	StageFiles(worktree, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles, StageForTask)
   564→
   565→	// Build commit message with checkpoint trailer
   566→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   567→	if len(shortToolUseID) > 12 {
   568→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:12]
   569→	}
   570→
   571→	var subject string
   572→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   573→		subject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   574→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   575→			ctx.SubagentType,
   576→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   577→			ctx.TodoContent,
   578→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   579→			shortToolUseID,
   580→		)
   581→	} else {
   582→		subject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   583→	}
   584→
   585→	// Add checkpoint ID trailer to commit message
   586→	commitMsg := subject + "\n\n" + trailers.CheckpointTrailerKey + ": " + checkpointID.String()
   587→
   588→	author := &object.Signature{
   589→		Name:  ctx.AuthorName,
   590→		Email: ctx.AuthorEmail,
   591→		When:  time.Now(),
   592→	}
   593→
   594→	commitHash, err := commitOrHead(repo, worktree, commitMsg, author)
   595→	if err != nil {
   596→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, err
   597→	}
   598→
   599→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   600→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed incremental checkpoint #%d to active branch (%s)\n", ctx.IncrementalSequence, commitHash.String()[:7])
   601→	} else {
   602→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task checkpoint to active branch (%s)\n", commitHash.String()[:7])
   603→	}
   604→	return commitHash, nil
   605→}
   606→
   607→// commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch commits task metadata to the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   608→// Uses sharded path: <checkpointID[:2]>/<checkpointID[2:]>/tasks/<tool-use-id>/
   609→// Returns the metadata commit hash.
   610→// When IsIncremental is true, only writes the incremental checkpoint file, skipping transcripts.
   611→// Uses checkpoint.WriteCommitted for git operations.
   612→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) commitTaskMetadataToMetadataBranch(repo *git.Repository, ctx TaskCheckpointContext, checkpointID id.CheckpointID) (plumbing.Hash, error) {
   613→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   614→	if err != nil {
   615→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   616→	}
   617→
   618→	// Format commit subject line for better git log readability
   619→	shortToolUseID := ctx.ToolUseID
   620→	if len(shortToolUseID) > 12 {
   621→		shortToolUseID = shortToolUseID[:12]
   622→	}
   623→
   624→	var messageSubject string
   625→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   626→		messageSubject = FormatIncrementalSubject(
   627→			ctx.IncrementalType,
   628→			ctx.SubagentType,
   629→			ctx.TaskDescription,
   630→			ctx.TodoContent,
   631→			ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   632→			shortToolUseID,
   633→		)
   634→	} else {
   635→		messageSubject = FormatSubagentEndMessage(ctx.SubagentType, ctx.TaskDescription, shortToolUseID)
   636→	}
   637→
   638→	// Get current branch name
   639→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   640→
   641→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   642→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   643→		CheckpointID:           checkpointID,
   644→		SessionID:              ctx.SessionID,
   645→		Strategy:               StrategyNameAutoCommit,
   646→		Branch:                 branchName,
   647→		IsTask:                 true,
   648→		ToolUseID:              ctx.ToolUseID,
   649→		AgentID:                ctx.AgentID,
   650→		CheckpointUUID:         ctx.CheckpointUUID,
   651→		TranscriptPath:         ctx.TranscriptPath,
   652→		SubagentTranscriptPath: ctx.SubagentTranscriptPath,
   653→		IsIncremental:          ctx.IsIncremental,
   654→		IncrementalSequence:    ctx.IncrementalSequence,
   655→		IncrementalType:        ctx.IncrementalType,
   656→		IncrementalData:        ctx.IncrementalData,
   657→		CommitSubject:          messageSubject,
   658→		AuthorName:             ctx.AuthorName,
   659→		AuthorEmail:            ctx.AuthorEmail,
   660→		Agent:                  ctx.AgentType,
   661→	})
   662→	if err != nil {
   663→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write task checkpoint: %w", err)
   664→	}
   665→
   666→	if ctx.IsIncremental {
   667→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed incremental checkpoint metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   668→	} else {
   669→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed task metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
   670→	}
   671→	return plumbing.ZeroHash, nil // Commit hash not needed by callers
   672→}
   673→
   674→// GetTaskCheckpoint returns the task checkpoint for a given rewind point.
   675→// For auto-commit strategy, checkpoints are stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch in checkpoint directories.
   676→// Returns ErrNotTaskCheckpoint if the point is not a task checkpoint.
   677→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetTaskCheckpoint(point RewindPoint) (*TaskCheckpoint, error) {
   678→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   679→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
   680→	}
   681→
   682→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   683→	if err != nil {
   684→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   685→	}
   686→
   687→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   688→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   689→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   690→	if err != nil {
   691→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("metadata branch %s not found: %w", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
   692→	}
   693→
   694→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   695→	if err != nil {
   696→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   697→	}
   698→
   699→	tree, err := metadataCommit.Tree()
   700→	if err != nil {
   701→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata tree: %w", err)
   702→	}
   703→
   704→	// Find checkpoint using the metadata path from rewind point
   705→	// MetadataDir for auto-commit task checkpoints is: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>
   706→	checkpointPath := point.MetadataDir + "/checkpoint.json"
   707→	file, err := tree.File(checkpointPath)
   708→	if err != nil {
   709→		// Try finding via commit SHA lookup
   710→		taskCheckpointPath, findErr := s.findTaskCheckpointPath(repo, point.ID, point.ToolUseID)
   711→		if findErr != nil {
   712→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", checkpointPath, err)
   713→		}
   714→		file, err = tree.File(taskCheckpointPath)
   715→		if err != nil {
   716→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find checkpoint at %s: %w", taskCheckpointPath, err)
   717→		}
   718→	}
   719→
   720→	content, err := file.Contents()
   721→	if err != nil {
   722→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   723→	}
   724→
   725→	var checkpoint TaskCheckpoint
   726→	if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(content), &checkpoint); err != nil {
   727→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse checkpoint: %w", err)
   728→	}
   729→
   730→	return &checkpoint, nil
   731→}
   732→
   733→// GetTaskCheckpointTranscript returns the session transcript for a task checkpoint.
   734→// For auto-commit strategy, transcripts are stored on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch in checkpoint directories.
   735→// Returns ErrNotTaskCheckpoint if the point is not a task checkpoint.
   736→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetTaskCheckpointTranscript(point RewindPoint) ([]byte, error) {
   737→	if !point.IsTaskCheckpoint {
   738→		return nil, ErrNotTaskCheckpoint
   739→	}
   740→
   741→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   742→	if err != nil {
   743→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   744→	}
   745→
   746→	// Get the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   747→	refName := plumbing.NewBranchReferenceName(paths.MetadataBranchName)
   748→	ref, err := repo.Reference(refName, true)
   749→	if err != nil {
   750→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("metadata branch %s not found: %w", paths.MetadataBranchName, err)
   751→	}
   752→
   753→	metadataCommit, err := repo.CommitObject(ref.Hash())
   754→	if err != nil {
   755→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata branch commit: %w", err)
   756→	}
   757→
   758→	tree, err := metadataCommit.Tree()
   759→	if err != nil {
   760→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get metadata tree: %w", err)
   761→	}
   762→
   763→	// MetadataDir for auto-commit task checkpoints is: <id[:2]>/<id[2:]>/tasks/<tool-use-id>
   764→	// Extract the checkpoint path by removing "/tasks/<tool-use-id>"
   765→	metadataDir := point.MetadataDir
   766→	if idx := strings.Index(metadataDir, "/tasks/"); idx > 0 {
   767→		checkpointPath := metadataDir[:idx]
   768→
   769→		// Use the first session's transcript path from sessions array
   770→		transcriptPath := ""
   771→		summaryFile, summaryErr := tree.File(checkpointPath + "/" + paths.MetadataFileName)
   772→		if summaryErr == nil {
   773→			summaryContent, contentErr := summaryFile.Contents()
   774→			if contentErr == nil {
   775→				var summary checkpoint.CheckpointSummary
   776→				if json.Unmarshal([]byte(summaryContent), &summary) == nil && len(summary.Sessions) > 0 {
   777→					// Use first session's transcript path (task checkpoints have only one session)
   778→					// SessionFilePaths now contains absolute paths with leading "/"
   779→					// Strip the leading "/" for tree.File() which expects paths without leading slash
   780→					if summary.Sessions[0].Transcript != "" {
   781→						transcriptPath = strings.TrimPrefix(summary.Sessions[0].Transcript, "/")
   782→					}
   783→				}
   784→			}
   785→		}
   786→
   787→		// Fall back to old format if sessions map not available
   788→		if transcriptPath == "" {
   789→			transcriptPath = checkpointPath + "/" + paths.TranscriptFileName
   790→		}
   791→
   792→		file, err := tree.File(transcriptPath)
   793→		if err != nil {
   794→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to find transcript at %s: %w", transcriptPath, err)
   795→		}
   796→		content, err := file.Contents()
   797→		if err != nil {
   798→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read transcript: %w", err)
   799→		}
   800→		return []byte(content), nil
   801→	}
   802→
   803→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid metadata path format: %s", metadataDir)
   804→}
   805→
   806→// findTaskCheckpointPath finds the full path to a task checkpoint on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   807→// Searches checkpoint directories for the task checkpoint matching the commit SHA and tool use ID.
   808→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findTaskCheckpointPath(repo *git.Repository, commitSHA, toolUseID string) (string, error) {
   809→	// Use findTaskMetadataPathForCommit which searches commit history
   810→	taskPath, err := s.findTaskMetadataPathForCommit(repo, commitSHA, toolUseID)
   811→	if err != nil {
   812→		return "", err
   813→	}
   814→	if taskPath == "" {
   815→		return "", errors.New("task checkpoint not found")
   816→	}
   817→	// taskPath is like: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>/checkpoints/001-<tool-use-id>.json
   818→	// We need: cond-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS-XXXXXXXX/tasks/<tool-use-id>/checkpoint.json
   819→	if idx := strings.Index(taskPath, "/checkpoints/"); idx > 0 {
   820→		return taskPath[:idx] + "/checkpoint.json", nil
   821→	}
   822→	return taskPath + "/checkpoint.json", nil
   823→}
   824→
   825→// GetMetadataRef returns a reference to the metadata for the given checkpoint.
   826→// For auto-commit strategy, returns the checkpoint path on entire/checkpoints/v1 branch.
   827→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetMetadataRef(checkpoint Checkpoint) string {
   828→	if checkpoint.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   829→		return ""
   830→	}
   831→	return paths.MetadataBranchName + ":" + checkpoint.CheckpointID.Path()
   832→}
   833→
   834→// GetSessionMetadataRef returns a reference to the most recent metadata for a session.
   835→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionMetadataRef(sessionID string) string {
   836→	session, err := GetSession(sessionID)
   837→	if err != nil || len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   838→		return ""
   839→	}
   840→	// Checkpoints are ordered with most recent first
   841→	return s.GetMetadataRef(session.Checkpoints[0])
   842→}
   843→
   844→// GetSessionContext returns the context.md content for a session.
   845→// For auto-commit strategy, reads from the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using the checkpoint store.
   846→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetSessionContext(sessionID string) string {
   847→	session, err := GetSession(sessionID)
   848→	if err != nil || len(session.Checkpoints) == 0 {
   849→		return ""
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Get the most recent checkpoint
   853→	cp := session.Checkpoints[0]
   854→	if cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   855→		return ""
   856→	}
   857→
   858→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   859→	if err != nil {
   860→		return ""
   861→	}
   862→
   863→	content, err := store.ReadSessionContentByID(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID, sessionID)
   864→	if err != nil || content == nil {
   865→		return ""
   866→	}
   867→
   868→	return content.Context
   869→}
   870→
   871→// GetCheckpointLog returns the session transcript for a specific checkpoint.
   872→// For auto-commit strategy, looks up checkpoint by ID on the entire/checkpoints/v1 branch using the checkpoint store.
   873→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) GetCheckpointLog(cp Checkpoint) ([]byte, error) {
   874→	if cp.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   875→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   876→	}
   877→
   878→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   879→	if err != nil {
   880→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   881→	}
   882→
   883→	content, err := store.ReadLatestSessionContent(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID)
   884→	if err != nil {
   885→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   886→	}
   887→	if content == nil {
   888→		return nil, ErrNoMetadata
   889→	}
   890→
   891→	return content.Transcript, nil
   892→}
   893→
   894→// InitializeSession creates session state for a new session.
   895→// This is called during UserPromptSubmit hook to set up tracking for the session.
   896→// For auto-commit strategy, this creates a SessionState file in .git/entire-sessions/
   897→// to track CheckpointTranscriptStart (transcript offset) across checkpoints.
   898→// agentType is the human-readable name of the agent (e.g., "Claude Code").
   899→// transcriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection).
   900→// userPrompt is the user's prompt text (stored truncated as FirstPrompt for display).
   901→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) InitializeSession(sessionID string, agentType agent.AgentType, transcriptPath string, userPrompt string) error {
   902→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   903→	if err != nil {
   904→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to open git repository: %w", err)
   905→	}
   906→
   907→	// Get current HEAD commit to track as base
   908→	head, err := repo.Head()
   909→	if err != nil {
   910→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get HEAD: %w", err)
   911→	}
   912→
   913→	baseCommit := head.Hash().String()
   914→
   915→	// Check if session state already exists (e.g., session resuming)
   916→	existing, err := LoadSessionState(sessionID)
   917→	if err != nil {
   918→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to check existing session state: %w", err)
   919→	}
   920→	if existing != nil {
   921→		// Session already initialized — update last interaction time on every prompt submit
   922→		now := time.Now()
   923→		existing.LastInteractionTime = &now
   924→
   925→		// Backfill FirstPrompt if empty (for sessions
   926→		// created before the first_prompt field was added, or resumed sessions)
   927→		if existing.FirstPrompt == "" && userPrompt != "" {
   928→			existing.FirstPrompt = truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt)
   929→		}
   930→
   931→		if err := SaveSessionState(existing); err != nil {
   932→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to update session state: %w", err)
   933→		}
   934→		return nil
   935→	}
   936→
   937→	// Create new session state
   938→	now := time.Now()
   939→	state := &SessionState{
   940→		SessionID:           sessionID,
   941→		CLIVersion:          buildinfo.Version,
   942→		BaseCommit:          baseCommit,
   943→		StartedAt:           now,
   944→		LastInteractionTime: &now,
   945→		StepCount:           0,
   946→		// CheckpointTranscriptStart defaults to 0 (start from beginning of transcript)
   947→		FilesTouched:   []string{},
   948→		AgentType:      agentType,
   949→		TranscriptPath: transcriptPath,
   950→		FirstPrompt:    truncatePromptForStorage(userPrompt),
   951→	}
   952→
   953→	if err := SaveSessionState(state); err != nil {
   954→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to save session state: %w", err)
   955→	}
   956→
   957→	return nil
   958→}
   959→
   960→// ListOrphanedItems returns orphaned items created by the auto-commit strategy.
   961→// For auto-commit, checkpoints are orphaned when no commit has an Entire-Checkpoint
   962→// trailer referencing them (e.g., after rebasing or squashing).
   963→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) ListOrphanedItems() ([]CleanupItem, error) {
   964→	repo, err := OpenRepository()
   965→	if err != nil {
   966→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to open repository: %w", err)
   967→	}
   968→
   969→	// Get checkpoint store (lazily initialized)
   970→	cpStore, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   971→	if err != nil {
   972→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   973→	}
   974→
   975→	// Get all checkpoints from entire/checkpoints/v1 branch
   976→	checkpoints, err := cpStore.ListCommitted(context.Background())
   977→	if err != nil {
   978→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil //nolint:nilerr // No checkpoints is not an error for cleanup
   979→	}
   980→
   981→	if len(checkpoints) == 0 {
   982→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
   983→	}
   984→
   985→	// Filter to only auto-commit checkpoints (identified by strategy in metadata)
   986→	autoCommitCheckpoints := make(map[string]bool)
   987→	for _, cp := range checkpoints {
   988→		summary, readErr := cpStore.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), cp.CheckpointID)
   989→		if readErr != nil || summary == nil {
   990→			continue
   991→		}
   992→		// Only consider checkpoints created by this strategy
   993→		if summary.Strategy == StrategyNameAutoCommit {
   994→			autoCommitCheckpoints[cp.CheckpointID.String()] = true
   995→		}
   996→	}
   997→
   998→	if len(autoCommitCheckpoints) == 0 {
   999→		return []CleanupItem{}, nil
  1000→	}
  1001→
  1002→	// Find checkpoint IDs referenced in commits
  1003→	referencedCheckpoints := s.findReferencedCheckpoints(repo)
  1004→
  1005→	// Find orphaned checkpoints
  1006→	var items []CleanupItem
  1007→	for checkpointID := range autoCommitCheckpoints {
  1008→		if !referencedCheckpoints[checkpointID] {
  1009→			items = append(items, CleanupItem{
  1010→				Type:   CleanupTypeCheckpoint,
  1011→				ID:     checkpointID,
  1012→				Reason: "no commit references this checkpoint",
  1013→			})
  1014→		}
  1015→	}
  1016→
  1017→	return items, nil
  1018→}
  1019→
  1020→// findReferencedCheckpoints scans commits for Entire-Checkpoint trailers.
  1021→func (s *AutoCommitStrategy) findReferencedCheckpoints(repo *git.Repository) map[string]bool {
  1022→	referenced := make(map[string]bool)
  1023→
  1024→	refs, err := repo.References()
  1025→	if err != nil {
  1026→		return referenced
  1027→	}
  1028→
  1029→	visited := make(map[plumbing.Hash]bool)
  1030→
  1031→	_ = refs.ForEach(func(ref *plumbing.Reference) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
  1032→		if !ref.Name().IsBranch() {
  1033→			return nil
  1034→		}
  1035→		// Skip entire/* branches
  1036→		branchName := strings.TrimPrefix(ref.Name().String(), "refs/heads/")
  1037→		if strings.HasPrefix(branchName, "entire/") {
  1038→			return nil
  1039→		}
  1040→
  1041→		iter, iterErr := repo.Log(&git.LogOptions{From: ref.Hash()})
  1042→		if iterErr != nil {
  1043→			return nil //nolint:nilerr // Best effort
  1044→		}
  1045→
  1046→		count := 0
  1047→		_ = iter.ForEach(func(c *object.Commit) error { //nolint:errcheck // Best effort
  1048→			count++
  1049→			if count > 1000 {
  1050→				return errors.New("limit reached")
  1051→			}
  1052→			if visited[c.Hash] {
  1053→				return nil
  1054→			}
  1055→			visited[c.Hash] = true
  1056→
  1057→			if cpID, found := trailers.ParseCheckpoint(c.Message); found {
  1058→				referenced[cpID.String()] = true
  1059→			}
  1060→			return nil
  1061→		})
  1062→		return nil
  1063→	})
  1064→
  1065→	return referenced
  1066→}
  1067→
  1068→//nolint:gochecknoinits // Standard pattern for strategy registration
  1069→func init() {
  1070→	// Register auto-commit as the primary strategy name
  1071→	Register(StrategyNameAutoCommit, NewAutoCommitStrategy)
  1072→}
  1073→

     1→// Package agent provides interfaces and types for integrating with coding agents.
     2→// It abstracts agent-specific behavior (hooks, log parsing, session storage) so that
     3→// the same Strategy implementations can work with any coding agent.
     4→package agent
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"io"
     8→	"time"
     9→)
    10→
    11→// Agent defines the interface for interacting with a coding agent.
    12→// Each agent implementation (Claude Code, Cursor, Aider, etc.) converts its
    13→// native format to the normalized types defined in this package.
    14→type Agent interface {
    15→	// Name returns the agent registry key (e.g., "claude-code", "gemini")
    16→	Name() AgentName
    17→
    18→	// Type returns the agent type identifier (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI")
    19→	// This is stored in metadata and trailers.
    20→	Type() AgentType
    21→
    22→	// Description returns a human-readable description for UI
    23→	Description() string
    24→
    25→	// DetectPresence checks if this agent is configured in the repository
    26→	DetectPresence() (bool, error)
    27→
    28→	// GetHookConfigPath returns path to hook config file (empty if none)
    29→	GetHookConfigPath() string
    30→
    31→	// SupportsHooks returns true if agent supports lifecycle hooks
    32→	SupportsHooks() bool
    33→
    34→	// ParseHookInput parses hook callback input from stdin
    35→	ParseHookInput(hookType HookType, reader io.Reader) (*HookInput, error)
    36→
    37→	// GetSessionID extracts session ID from hook input
    38→	GetSessionID(input *HookInput) string
    39→
    40→	// TransformSessionID converts agent session ID to Entire session ID
    41→	TransformSessionID(agentSessionID string) string
    42→
    43→	// ExtractAgentSessionID extracts agent session ID from Entire ID
    44→	ExtractAgentSessionID(entireSessionID string) string
    45→
    46→	// GetSessionDir returns where agent stores session data for this repo.
    47→	// Examples:
    48→	//   Claude: ~/.claude/projects/<sanitized-repo-path>/
    49→	//   Aider: current working directory (returns repoPath)
    50→	//   Cursor: ~/Library/Application Support/Cursor/User/globalStorage/
    51→	GetSessionDir(repoPath string) (string, error)
    52→
    53→	// ComputeTranscriptPath returns the path where this agent stores session transcripts.
    54→	// Parameters:
    55→	//   - repoPath: absolute path to repository root
    56→	//   - sessionID: the agent's session UUID
    57→	//   - startTime: session start time (used for Gemini's date-based naming)
    58→	// Returns the absolute path to the transcript file.
    59→	ComputeTranscriptPath(repoPath, sessionID string, startTime time.Time) (string, error)
    60→
    61→	// ReadSession reads session data from agent's storage.
    62→	// Handles different formats: JSONL (Claude), SQLite (Cursor), Markdown (Aider)
    63→	ReadSession(input *HookInput) (*AgentSession, error)
    64→
    65→	// WriteSession writes session data for resumption.
    66→	// Agent handles format conversion (JSONL, SQLite, etc.)
    67→	WriteSession(session *AgentSession) error
    68→
    69→	// FormatResumeCommand returns command to resume a session
    70→	FormatResumeCommand(sessionID string) string
    71→}
    72→
    73→// HookSupport is implemented by agents with lifecycle hooks.
    74→// This optional interface allows agents like Claude Code and Cursor to
    75→// install and manage hooks that notify Entire of agent events.
    76→type HookSupport interface {
    77→	Agent
    78→
    79→	// InstallHooks installs agent-specific hooks.
    80→	// If localDev is true, hooks point to local development build.
    81→	// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    82→	// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    83→	InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error)
    84→
    85→	// UninstallHooks removes installed hooks
    86→	UninstallHooks() error
    87→
    88→	// AreHooksInstalled checks if hooks are currently installed
    89→	AreHooksInstalled() bool
    90→
    91→	// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types this agent supports
    92→	GetSupportedHooks() []HookType
    93→}
    94→
    95→// HookHandler is implemented by agents that define their own hook vocabulary.
    96→// Each agent defines its own hook names (verbs) which become subcommands
    97→// under `entire hooks <agent>`. The actual handling is done by handlers
    98→// registered in the CLI package to avoid circular dependencies.
    99→//
   100→// This allows different agents to have completely different hook vocabularies
   101→// (e.g., Claude Code has "stop", Cursor might have "completion").
   102→type HookHandler interface {
   103→	Agent
   104→
   105→	// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs this agent supports.
   106→	// These are the subcommand names that will appear under `entire hooks <agent>`.
   107→	// e.g., ["stop", "user-prompt-submit", "pre-task", "post-task", "post-todo"]
   108→	GetHookNames() []string
   109→}
   110→
   111→// FileWatcher is implemented by agents that use file-based detection.
   112→// Agents like Aider that don't support hooks can use file watching
   113→// to detect session activity.
   114→type FileWatcher interface {
   115→	Agent
   116→
   117→	// GetWatchPaths returns paths to watch for session changes
   118→	GetWatchPaths() ([]string, error)
   119→
   120→	// OnFileChange handles a detected file change and returns session info
   121→	OnFileChange(path string) (*SessionChange, error)
   122→}
   123→
   124→// TranscriptAnalyzer is implemented by agents that support transcript analysis.
   125→// This allows agent-agnostic detection of work done between checkpoints.
   126→type TranscriptAnalyzer interface {
   127→	Agent
   128→
   129→	// GetTranscriptPosition returns the current position (length) of a transcript.
   130→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), this is the line count.
   131→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), this is the message count.
   132→	// Returns 0 if the file doesn't exist or is empty.
   133→	// Use this to efficiently check if the transcript has grown since last checkpoint.
   134→	GetTranscriptPosition(path string) (int, error)
   135→
   136→	// ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset extracts files modified since a given offset.
   137→	// For JSONL formats (Claude Code), offset is the starting line number.
   138→	// For JSON formats (Gemini CLI), offset is the starting message index.
   139→	// Returns:
   140→	//   - files: list of file paths modified by the agent (from Write/Edit tools)
   141→	//   - currentPosition: the current position (line count or message count)
   142→	//   - error: any error encountered during reading
   143→	ExtractModifiedFilesFromOffset(path string, startOffset int) (files []string, currentPosition int, err error)
   144→}
   145→
   146→// TranscriptChunker is implemented by agents that support transcript chunking.
   147→// This allows agents to split large transcripts into chunks for storage (GitHub has
   148→// a 100MB blob limit) and reassemble them when reading.
   149→type TranscriptChunker interface {
   150→	Agent
   151→
   152→	// ChunkTranscript splits a transcript into chunks if it exceeds maxSize.
   153→	// Returns a slice of chunks. If the transcript fits in one chunk, returns single-element slice.
   154→	// The chunking is format-aware: JSONL splits at line boundaries, JSON splits message arrays.
   155→	ChunkTranscript(content []byte, maxSize int) ([][]byte, error)
   156→
   157→	// ReassembleTranscript combines chunks back into a single transcript.
   158→	// Handles format-specific reassembly (JSONL concatenation, JSON message merging).
   159→	ReassembleTranscript(chunks [][]byte) ([]byte, error)
   160→}
   161→
