```
    24→// State represents the state of an active session.
    25→// This is stored in .git/entire-sessions/<session-id>.json
    26→type State struct {
    27→	// SessionID is the unique session identifier
    28→	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
    29→
    30→	// CLIVersion is the version of the CLI that created this session
    31→	CLIVersion string `json:"cli_version,omitempty"`
    32→
    33→	// BaseCommit tracks the current shadow branch base. Initially set to HEAD when the
    34→	// session starts, but updated on migration (pull/rebase) and after condensation.
    35→	// Used for shadow branch naming and checkpoint storage — NOT for attribution.
    36→	BaseCommit string `json:"base_commit"`
    37→
    38→	// AttributionBaseCommit is the commit used as the reference point for attribution calculations.
    39→	// Unlike BaseCommit (which tracks the shadow branch and moves with migration), this field
    40→	// preserves the original base commit so deferred condensation can correctly calculate
    41→	// agent vs human line attribution. Updated only after successful condensation.
    42→	AttributionBaseCommit string `json:"attribution_base_commit,omitempty"`
    43→
    44→	// WorktreePath is the absolute path to the worktree root
    45→	WorktreePath string `json:"worktree_path,omitempty"`
    46→
    47→	// WorktreeID is the internal git worktree identifier (empty for main worktree)
    48→	// Derived from .git/worktrees/<name>/, stable across git worktree move
    49→	WorktreeID string `json:"worktree_id,omitempty"`
    50→
    51→	// StartedAt is when the session was started
    52→	StartedAt time.Time `json:"started_at"`
    53→
    54→	// EndedAt is when the session was explicitly closed by the user.
    55→	// nil means the session is still active or was not cleanly closed.
    56→	EndedAt *time.Time `json:"ended_at,omitempty"`
    57→
    58→	// Phase is the lifecycle stage of this session (see phase.go).
    59→	// Empty means idle (backward compat with pre-state-machine files).
    60→	Phase Phase `json:"phase,omitempty"`
    61→
    62→	// PendingCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID for the current commit cycle.
    63→	// Generated once when first needed, reused across all commits in the session.
    64→	PendingCheckpointID string `json:"pending_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    65→
    66→	// LastInteractionTime is updated on every hook invocation.
    67→	// Used for stale session detection in "entire doctor".
    68→	LastInteractionTime *time.Time `json:"last_interaction_time,omitempty"`
    69→
    70→	// StepCount is the number of checkpoints/steps created in this session.
    71→	// JSON tag kept as "checkpoint_count" for backward compatibility with existing state files.
    72→	StepCount int `json:"checkpoint_count"`
    73→
    74→	// CheckpointTranscriptStart is the transcript line offset where the current
    75→	// checkpoint cycle began. Set to 0 at session start, updated to current
    76→	// transcript length after each condensation. Used to scope the transcript
    77→	// for checkpoint condensation: "everything since last checkpoint".
    78→	CheckpointTranscriptStart int `json:"checkpoint_transcript_start,omitempty"`
    79→

    81→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.

    83→	CondensedTranscriptLines int `json:"condensed_transcript_lines,omitempty"`
    84→
    85→	// UntrackedFilesAtStart tracks files that existed at session start (to preserve during rewind)
    86→	UntrackedFilesAtStart []string `json:"untracked_files_at_start,omitempty"`
    87→
    88→	// FilesTouched tracks files modified/created/deleted during this session
    89→	FilesTouched []string `json:"files_touched,omitempty"`
    90→
    91→	// LastCheckpointID is the checkpoint ID from last condensation, reused for subsequent commits without new content
    92→	LastCheckpointID id.CheckpointID `json:"last_checkpoint_id,omitempty"`
    93→
    94→	// AgentType identifies the agent that created this session (e.g., "Claude Code", "Gemini CLI", "Cursor")
    95→	AgentType agent.AgentType `json:"agent_type,omitempty"`
    96→
    97→	// Token usage tracking (accumulated across all checkpoints in this session)
    98→	TokenUsage *agent.TokenUsage `json:"token_usage,omitempty"`
    99→
   100→	// Deprecated: TranscriptLinesAtStart is replaced by CheckpointTranscriptStart.
   101→	// Kept for backward compatibility with existing state files.
   102→	TranscriptLinesAtStart int `json:"transcript_lines_at_start,omitempty"`
   103→
   104→	// TranscriptIdentifierAtStart is the last transcript identifier when the session started.
   105→	// Used for identifier-based transcript scoping (UUID for Claude, message ID for Gemini).
   106→	TranscriptIdentifierAtStart string `json:"transcript_identifier_at_start,omitempty"`
   107→
   108→	// TranscriptPath is the path to the live transcript file (for mid-session commit detection)
   109→	TranscriptPath string `json:"transcript_path,omitempty"`
   110→
   111→	// FirstPrompt is the first user prompt that started this session (truncated for display)
   112→	FirstPrompt string `json:"first_prompt,omitempty"`
   113→
   114→	// PromptAttributions tracks user and agent line changes at each prompt start.
   115→	// This enables accurate attribution by capturing user edits between checkpoints.
   116→	PromptAttributions []PromptAttribution `json:"prompt_attributions,omitempty"`
   117→
   118→	// PendingPromptAttribution holds attribution calculated at prompt start (before agent runs).
   119→	// This is moved to PromptAttributions when SaveChanges is called.
   120→	PendingPromptAttribution *PromptAttribution `json:"pending_prompt_attribution,omitempty"`
   121→}
   122→
   123→// PromptAttribution captures line-level attribution data at the start of each prompt.
   124→// By recording what changed since the last checkpoint BEFORE the agent works,
   125→// we can accurately separate user edits from agent contributions.
   126→type PromptAttribution struct {
   127→	// CheckpointNumber is which checkpoint this was recorded before (1-indexed)
   128→	CheckpointNumber int `json:"checkpoint_number"`
   129→
   130→	// UserLinesAdded is lines added by user since the last checkpoint
   131→	UserLinesAdded int `json:"user_lines_added"`
   132→
   133→	// UserLinesRemoved is lines removed by user since the last checkpoint
   134→	UserLinesRemoved int `json:"user_lines_removed"`
   135→
   136→	// AgentLinesAdded is total agent lines added so far (base → last checkpoint).
   137→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   138→	AgentLinesAdded int `json:"agent_lines_added"`
   139→
   140→	// AgentLinesRemoved is total agent lines removed so far (base → last checkpoint).
   141→	// Always 0 for checkpoint 1 since there's no previous checkpoint to measure against.
   142→	AgentLinesRemoved int `json:"agent_lines_removed"`
   143→
   144→	// UserAddedPerFile tracks per-file user additions for accurate modification tracking.
   145→	// This enables distinguishing user self-modifications from agent modifications.
   146→	// See docs/architecture/attribution.md for details.
   147→	UserAddedPerFile map[string]int `json:"user_added_per_file,omitempty"`
   148→}
   149→
   150→// NormalizeAfterLoad applies backward-compatible migrations to state loaded from disk.
   151→// Call this after deserializing a State from JSON.
   152→func (s *State) NormalizeAfterLoad() {
   153→	// Migrate transcript fields: CheckpointTranscriptStart replaces both
   154→	// CondensedTranscriptLines and TranscriptLinesAtStart from older state files.
   155→	if s.CheckpointTranscriptStart == 0 {
   156→		if s.CondensedTranscriptLines > 0 {
   157→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.CondensedTranscriptLines
   158→		} else if s.TranscriptLinesAtStart > 0 {
   159→			s.CheckpointTranscriptStart = s.TranscriptLinesAtStart
   160→		}
   161→	}
   162→	// Clear deprecated fields so they aren't re-persisted.
   163→	// Note: this is a one-way migration. If the state is re-saved, older CLI versions
   164→	// will see 0 for these fields and fall back to scoping from the transcript start.
   165→	// This is acceptable since CLI upgrades are monotonic and the worst case is
   166→	// redundant transcript content in a condensation, not data loss.
   167→	s.CondensedTranscriptLines = 0
   168→	s.TranscriptLinesAtStart = 0
   169→
   170→	// Backfill AttributionBaseCommit for sessions created before this field existed.
   171→	// Without this, a mid-turn commit would migrate BaseCommit and the fallback in
   172→	// calculateSessionAttributions would use the migrated value, producing zero attribution.
   173→	if s.AttributionBaseCommit == "" && s.BaseCommit != "" {
   174→		s.AttributionBaseCommit = s.BaseCommit
   175→	}
   176→}
   177→
   178→// StateStore provides low-level operations for managing session state files.
   179→//
   180→// StateStore is a primitive for session state persistence. It is NOT the same as
   181→// the Sessions interface - it only handles state files in .git/entire-sessions/,
   182→// not the full session data which includes checkpoint content.
   183→//
   184→// Use StateStore directly in strategies for performance-critical state operations.
   185→// Use the Sessions interface (when implemented) for high-level session management.
   186→type StateStore struct {
   187→	// stateDir is the directory where session state files are stored
   188→	stateDir string
   189→}
   190→
   191→// NewStateStore creates a new state store.
   192→// Uses the git common dir to store session state (shared across worktrees).
   193→func NewStateStore() (*StateStore, error) {
   194→	commonDir, err := getGitCommonDir()
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→	return &StateStore{
   199→		stateDir: filepath.Join(commonDir, SessionStateDirName),
   200→	}, nil
   201→}
   202→
   203→// NewStateStoreWithDir creates a new state store with a custom directory.
   204→// This is useful for testing.
   205→func NewStateStoreWithDir(stateDir string) *StateStore {
   206→	return &StateStore{stateDir: stateDir}
   207→}
   208→
   209→// Load loads the session state for the given session ID.
   210→// Returns (nil, nil) when session file doesn't exist (not an error condition).
   211→func (s *StateStore) Load(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) (*State, error) {
   212→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   213→
   214→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   215→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   216→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   220→
   221→	data, err := os.ReadFile(stateFile) //nolint:gosec // stateFile is derived from sessionID
   222→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   223→		return nil, nil //nolint:nilnil // nil,nil indicates session not found (expected case)
   224→	}
   225→	if err != nil {
   226→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state: %w", err)
   227→	}
   228→
   229→	var state State
   230→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &state); err != nil {
   231→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to unmarshal session state: %w", err)
   232→	}
   233→	state.NormalizeAfterLoad()
   234→	return &state, nil
   235→}
   236→
   237→// Save saves the session state atomically.
   238→func (s *StateStore) Save(ctx context.Context, state *State) error {
   239→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   240→
   241→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   242→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(state.SessionID); err != nil {
   243→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   244→	}
   245→
   246→	if err := os.MkdirAll(s.stateDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create session state directory: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→
   250→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(state, "", "  ")
   251→	if err != nil {
   252→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal session state: %w", err)
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(state.SessionID)
   256→
   257→	// Atomic write: write to temp file, then rename
   258→	tmpFile := stateFile + ".tmp"
   259→	if err := os.WriteFile(tmpFile, data, 0o600); err != nil {
   260→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write session state: %w", err)
   261→	}
   262→	if err := os.Rename(tmpFile, stateFile); err != nil {
   263→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to rename session state file: %w", err)
   264→	}
   265→	return nil
   266→}
   267→
   268→// Clear removes the session state file for the given session ID.
   269→func (s *StateStore) Clear(ctx context.Context, sessionID string) error {
   270→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   271→
   272→	// Validate session ID to prevent path traversal
   273→	if err := validation.ValidateSessionID(sessionID); err != nil {
   274→		return fmt.Errorf("invalid session ID: %w", err)
   275→	}
   276→
   277→	stateFile := s.stateFilePath(sessionID)
   278→
   279→	if err := os.Remove(stateFile); err != nil {
   280→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   281→			return nil // Already gone, not an error
   282→		}
   283→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state file: %w", err)
   284→	}
   285→	return nil
   286→}
   287→
   288→// RemoveAll removes the entire session state directory.
   289→// This is used during uninstall to completely remove all session state.
   290→func (s *StateStore) RemoveAll() error {
   291→	if err := os.RemoveAll(s.stateDir); err != nil {
   292→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to remove session state directory: %w", err)
   293→	}
   294→	return nil
   295→}
   296→
   297→// List returns all session states.
   298→func (s *StateStore) List(ctx context.Context) ([]*State, error) {
   299→	_ = ctx // Reserved for future use
   300→
   301→	entries, err := os.ReadDir(s.stateDir)
   302→	if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   303→		return nil, nil
   304→	}
   305→	if err != nil {
   306→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to read session state directory: %w", err)
   307→	}
   308→
   309→	var states []*State
   310→	for _, entry := range entries {
   311→		if entry.IsDir() || !strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json") {
   312→			continue
   313→		}
   314→		if strings.HasSuffix(entry.Name(), ".tmp") {
   315→			continue // Skip temp files
   316→		}
   317→
   318→		sessionID := strings.TrimSuffix(entry.Name(), ".json")
   319→		state, err := s.Load(ctx, sessionID)
   320→		if err != nil {
   321→			continue // Skip corrupted state files
   322→		}
   323→		if state == nil {
   324→			continue
   325→		}
   326→
   327→		states = append(states, state)
   328→	}
   329→	return states, nil
   330→}
   331→
   332→// stateFilePath returns the path to a session state file.
   333→func (s *StateStore) stateFilePath(sessionID string) string {
   334→	return filepath.Join(s.stateDir, sessionID+".json")
   335→}
   336→
   337→// getGitCommonDir returns the path to the shared git directory.
   338→// In a regular checkout, this is .git/
   339→// In a worktree, this is the main repo's .git/ (not .git/worktrees/<name>/)
   340→func getGitCommonDir() (string, error) {
   341→	ctx := context.Background()
   342→	cmd := exec.CommandContext(ctx, "git", "rev-parse", "--git-common-dir")
   343→	cmd.Dir = "."
   344→	output, err := cmd.Output()
   345→	if err != nil {
   346→		return "", fmt.Errorf("failed to get git common dir: %w", err)
   347→	}
   348→
   349→	commonDir := strings.TrimSpace(string(output))
   350→
   351→	// git rev-parse --git-common-dir returns relative paths from the working directory,
   352→	// so we need to make it absolute if it isn't already
   353→	if !filepath.IsAbs(commonDir) {
   354→		commonDir = filepath.Join(".", commonDir)
   355→	}
   356→
   357→	return filepath.Clean(commonDir), nil
   358→}
   359→
```

```
   238→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   239→	if err != nil {
   240→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   241→	}
   242→
   243→	// Extract session ID from metadata dir
   244→	sessionID := filepath.Base(ctx.MetadataDir)
   245→
   246→	// Get current branch name
   247→	branchName := GetCurrentBranchName(repo)
   248→
   249→	// Combine all file changes into FilesTouched (same as manual-commit)
   250→	filesTouched := mergeFilesTouched(nil, ctx.ModifiedFiles, ctx.NewFiles, ctx.DeletedFiles)
   251→
   252→	// Write committed checkpoint using the checkpoint store
   253→	err = store.WriteCommitted(context.Background(), checkpoint.WriteCommittedOptions{
   254→		CheckpointID:                checkpointID,
   255→		SessionID:                   sessionID,
   256→		Strategy:                    StrategyNameAutoCommit, // Use new strategy name
   257→		Branch:                      branchName,
   258→		MetadataDir:                 ctx.MetadataDirAbs, // Copy all files from metadata dir
   259→		AuthorName:                  ctx.AuthorName,
   260→		AuthorEmail:                 ctx.AuthorEmail,

   263→		CheckpointTranscriptStart:   ctx.StepTranscriptStart,
   264→		TokenUsage:                  ctx.TokenUsage,
   265→		CheckpointsCount:            1,            // Each auto-commit checkpoint = 1
   266→		FilesTouched:                filesTouched, // Track modified files (same as manual-commit)
   267→	})
   268→	if err != nil {
   269→		return plumbing.ZeroHash, fmt.Errorf("failed to write committed checkpoint: %w", err)
   270→	}
   271→
   272→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Committed session metadata to %s (%s)\n", paths.MetadataBranchName, checkpointID)
```

```
   619→// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.
   620→// This fetches the transcript from entire/checkpoints/v1 and writes it to Claude's project directory.
   621→// Does not modify the working directory.
   622→// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.
   623→// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.
   624→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error {
   625→	if !point.IsLogsOnly {
   626→		return errors.New("not a logs-only rewind point")
   627→	}
   628→
   629→	if point.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {
   630→		return errors.New("missing checkpoint ID")
   631→	}
   632→
   633→	// Get checkpoint store
   634→	store, err := s.getCheckpointStore()
   635→	if err != nil {
   636→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get checkpoint store: %w", err)
   637→	}
   638→
   639→	// Read checkpoint summary to get session count
   640→	summary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)
   641→	if err != nil {
   642→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to read checkpoint: %w", err)
   643→	}
   644→	if summary == nil {
   645→		return fmt.Errorf("checkpoint not found: %s", point.CheckpointID)
   646→	}
   647→
   648→	// Get repo root for Claude project path lookup
   649→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   650→	if err != nil {
   651→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get repository root: %w", err)
   652→	}
   653→
   654→	claudeProjectDir, err := paths.GetClaudeProjectDir(repoRoot)
   655→	if err != nil {
   656→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get Claude project directory: %w", err)
   657→	}
   658→
   659→	// Ensure project directory exists
   660→	if err := os.MkdirAll(claudeProjectDir, 0o750); err != nil {
   661→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to create Claude project directory: %w", err)
   662→	}
   663→
   664→	// Check for newer local logs if not forcing
   665→	if !force {
   666→		sessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), claudeProjectDir, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)
   667→		hasConflicts := false
   668→		for _, sess := range sessions {
   669→			if sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {
   670→				hasConflicts = true
   671→				break
   672→			}
   673→		}
   674→		if hasConflicts {
   675→			shouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)
   676→			if promptErr != nil {
   677→				return promptErr
   678→			}
   679→			if !shouldOverwrite {
   680→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Resume cancelled. Local session logs preserved.\n")
   681→				return nil
   682→			}
   683→		}
   684→	}
   685→
   686→	// Count sessions to restore
   687→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   688→	if totalSessions > 1 {
   689→		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\n", totalSessions)
   690→	}
   691→
   692→	// Restore all sessions (oldest to newest, using 0-based indexing)
   693→	for i := range totalSessions {
   694→		content, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID, i)
   695→		if readErr != nil {
   696→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: failed to read session %d: %v\n", i, readErr)
   697→			continue
   698→		}
   699→		if content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   700→			continue
   701→		}
   702→
   703→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   704→		if sessionID == "" {
   705→			// Fallback: can't identify session without ID
   706→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Warning: session %d has no session ID, skipping\n", i)
   707→			continue
   708→		}
   709→
   710→		modelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)
   711→		claudeSessionFile := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+".jsonl")
   712→
   713→		// Get first prompt for display
   714→		promptPreview := ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts)
   715→
   716→		if totalSessions > 1 {
   717→			isLatest := i == totalSessions-1
   718→			if promptPreview != "" {
   719→				if isLatest {
   720→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d (latest): %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   721→				} else {
   722→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  Session %d: %s\n", i+1, promptPreview)
   723→				}
   724→			}
   725→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Writing to: %s\n", claudeSessionFile)
   726→		} else {
   727→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Writing transcript to: %s\n", claudeSessionFile)
   728→		}
   729→
   730→		if writeErr := os.WriteFile(claudeSessionFile, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {
   731→			if totalSessions > 1 {
   732→				fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "    Warning: failed to write transcript: %v\n", writeErr)
   733→				continue
   734→			}
   735→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to write transcript: %w", writeErr)
   736→		}
   737→	}
   738→
   739→	return nil
   740→}
   741→
   742→// readSessionPrompt reads the first prompt from the session's prompt.txt file stored in git.
   743→// Returns an empty string if the prompt cannot be read.
```

```
   790→// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info
   791→// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.
   792→func (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, claudeProjectDir string, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {
   793→	var sessions []SessionRestoreInfo
   794→
   795→	totalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)
   796→	// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)
   797→	for i := range totalSessions {
   798→		content, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)
   799→		if err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {
   800→			continue
   801→		}
   802→
   803→		sessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID
   804→		if sessionID == "" {
   805→			continue
   806→		}
   807→
   808→		modelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)
   809→		localPath := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+".jsonl")
   810→
   811→		localTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)
   812→		checkpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)
   813→		status := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)
   814→
   815→		sessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{
   816→			SessionID:      sessionID,
   817→			Prompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),
   818→			Status:         status,
   819→			LocalTime:      localTime,
   820→			CheckpointTime: checkpointTime,
   821→		})
   822→	}
   823→
   824→	return sessions
   825→}
```

```json
  "old_string": "// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.\n// This fetches the transcript from entire/checkpoints/v1 and writes it to Claude's project directory.\n// Does not modify the working directory.\n// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.\n// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error {\n\tif !point.IsLogsOnly {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"not a logs-only rewind point\")\n\t}\n\n\tif point.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"missing checkpoint ID\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Get checkpoint store\n\tstore, err := s.getCheckpointStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get checkpoint store: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read checkpoint summary to get session count\n\tsummary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read checkpoint: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif summary == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"checkpoint not found: %s\", point.CheckpointID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get repo root for Claude project path lookup\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repository root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tclaudeProjectDir, err := paths.GetClaudeProjectDir(repoRoot)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get Claude project directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Ensure project directory exists\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(claudeProjectDir, 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create Claude project directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check for newer local logs if not forcing\n\tif !force {\n\t\tsessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), claudeProjectDir, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)\n\t\thasConflicts := false\n\t\tfor _, sess := range sessions {\n\t\t\tif sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {\n\t\t\t\thasConflicts = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif hasConflicts {\n\t\t\tshouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)\n\t\t\tif promptErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn promptErr\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !shouldOverwrite {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Resume cancelled. Local session logs preserved.\\n\")\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Count sessions to restore\n\ttotalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)\n\tif totalSessions > 1 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\\n\", totalSessions)\n\t}\n\n\t// Restore all sessions (oldest to newest, using 0-based indexing)\n\tfor i := range totalSessions {\n\t\tcontent, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID, i)\n\t\tif readErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: failed to read session %d: %v\\n\", i, readErr)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID\n\t\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\t\t// Fallback: can't identify session without ID\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: session %d has no session ID, skipping\\n\", i)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tmodelSessionID := sessionid.ModelSessionID(sessionID)\n\t\tclaudeSessionFile := filepath.Join(claudeProjectDir, modelSessionID+\".jsonl\")\n\n\t\t// Get first prompt for display\n\t\tpromptPreview := ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts)\n\n\t\tif totalSessions > 1 {\n\t\t\tisLatest := i == totalSessions-1\n\t\t\tif promptPreview != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif isLatest {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Session %d (latest): %s\\n\", i+1, promptPreview)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Session %d: %s\\n\", i+1, promptPreview)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"    Writing to: %s\\n\", claudeSessionFile)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Writing transcript to: %s\\n\", claudeSessionFile)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif writeErr := os.WriteFile(claudeSessionFile, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {\n\t\t\tif totalSessions > 1 {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"    Warning: failed to write transcript: %v\\n\", writeErr)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write transcript: %w\", writeErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}",
  "new_string": "// RestoreLogsOnly restores session logs from a logs-only rewind point.\n// This fetches the transcript from entire/checkpoints/v1 and writes it to the\n// agent-specific session directory (computed via ComputeTranscriptPath).\n// Does not modify the working directory.\n// When multiple sessions were condensed to the same checkpoint, ALL sessions are restored.\n// If force is false, prompts for confirmation when local logs have newer timestamps.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) RestoreLogsOnly(point RewindPoint, force bool) error {\n\tif !point.IsLogsOnly {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"not a logs-only rewind point\")\n\t}\n\n\tif point.CheckpointID.IsEmpty() {\n\t\treturn errors.New(\"missing checkpoint ID\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Get checkpoint store\n\tstore, err := s.getCheckpointStore()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get checkpoint store: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Read checkpoint summary to get session count\n\tsummary, err := store.ReadCommitted(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to read checkpoint: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tif summary == nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"checkpoint not found: %s\", point.CheckpointID)\n\t}\n\n\t// Get repo root for transcript path computation\n\trepoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get repository root: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Check for newer local logs if not forcing\n\tif !force {\n\t\tsessions := s.classifySessionsForRestore(context.Background(), repoRoot, store, point.CheckpointID, summary)\n\t\thasConflicts := false\n\t\tfor _, sess := range sessions {\n\t\t\tif sess.Status == StatusLocalNewer {\n\t\t\t\thasConflicts = true\n\t\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif hasConflicts {\n\t\t\tshouldOverwrite, promptErr := PromptOverwriteNewerLogs(sessions)\n\t\t\tif promptErr != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn promptErr\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif !shouldOverwrite {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Resume cancelled. Local session logs preserved.\\n\")\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Count sessions to restore\n\ttotalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)\n\tif totalSessions > 1 {\n\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Restoring %d sessions from checkpoint:\\n\", totalSessions)\n\t}\n\n\t// Restore all sessions (oldest to newest, using 0-based indexing)\n\tfor i := range totalSessions {\n\t\tcontent, readErr := store.ReadSessionContent(context.Background(), point.CheckpointID, i)\n\t\tif readErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: failed to read session %d: %v\\n\", i, readErr)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID\n\t\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: session %d has no session ID, skipping\\n\", i)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Use ComputeTranscriptPath for agent-aware path computation\n\t\tagentType := content.Metadata.Agent\n\t\tag, agErr := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)\n\t\tif agErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: unknown agent type %q for session %d, skipping\\n\", agentType, i)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tagentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)\n\t\tstartedAt := content.Metadata.StartedAt\n\t\tif startedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\t\tstartedAt = content.Metadata.CreatedAt // backward compat\n\t\t}\n\t\ttranscriptPath, pathErr := ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, agentSessionID, startedAt)\n\t\tif pathErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: failed to compute transcript path for session %d: %v\\n\", i, pathErr)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Ensure parent directory exists\n\t\tif mkdirErr := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(transcriptPath), 0o750); mkdirErr != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Warning: failed to create directory for session %d: %v\\n\", i, mkdirErr)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Get first prompt for display\n\t\tpromptPreview := ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts)\n\n\t\tif totalSessions > 1 {\n\t\t\tisLatest := i == totalSessions-1\n\t\t\tif promptPreview != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif isLatest {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Session %d (latest): %s\\n\", i+1, promptPreview)\n\t\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"  Session %d: %s\\n\", i+1, promptPreview)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"    Writing to: %s\\n\", transcriptPath)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"Writing transcript to: %s\\n\", transcriptPath)\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tif writeErr := os.WriteFile(transcriptPath, content.Transcript, 0o600); writeErr != nil {\n\t\t\tif totalSessions > 1 {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"    Warning: failed to write transcript: %v\\n\", writeErr)\n\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write transcript: %w\", writeErr)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\treturn nil\n}"
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "// classifySessionsForRestore checks all sessions in a checkpoint and returns info\n// about each session, including whether local logs have newer timestamps.\nfunc (s *ManualCommitStrategy) classifySessionsForRestore(ctx context.Context, repoRoot string, store cpkg.Store, checkpointID id.CheckpointID, summary *cpkg.CheckpointSummary) []SessionRestoreInfo {\n\tvar sessions []SessionRestoreInfo\n\n\ttotalSessions := len(summary.Sessions)\n\t// Check all sessions (0-based indexing)\n\tfor i := range totalSessions {\n\t\tcontent, err := store.ReadSessionContent(ctx, checkpointID, i)\n\t\tif err != nil || content == nil || len(content.Transcript) == 0 {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tsessionID := content.Metadata.SessionID\n\t\tif sessionID == \"\" {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\t// Use ComputeTranscriptPath for agent-aware local path lookup\n\t\tagentType := content.Metadata.Agent\n\t\tag, agErr := agent.GetByAgentType(agentType)\n\t\tif agErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tagentSessionID := ag.ExtractAgentSessionID(sessionID)\n\t\tstartedAt := content.Metadata.StartedAt\n\t\tif startedAt.IsZero() {\n\t\t\tstartedAt = content.Metadata.CreatedAt // backward compat\n\t\t}\n\t\tlocalPath, pathErr := ag.ComputeTranscriptPath(repoRoot, agentSessionID, startedAt)\n\t\tif pathErr != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tlocalTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromFile(localPath)\n\t\tcheckpointTime := paths.GetLastTimestampFromBytes(content.Transcript)\n\t\tstatus := ClassifyTimestamps(localTime, checkpointTime)\n\n\t\tsessions = append(sessions, SessionRestoreInfo{\n\t\t\tSessionID:      sessionID,\n\t\t\tPrompt:         ExtractFirstPrompt(content.Prompts),\n\t\t\tStatus:         status,\n\t\t\tLocalTime:      localTime,\n\t\t\tCheckpointTime: checkpointTime,\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\treturn sessions\n}"
```

```
   482→// Always overwrites existing session logs to ensure consistency with checkpoint state.

   503→				slog.String("session_id", sessionID),

   505→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Session '%s' found in commit trailer but session log not available\n", sessionID)

   507→			fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "  %s\n", ag.FormatResumeCommand(agentSessionID))
   508→			return nil
   509→		}
   510→		logging.Error(ctx, "resume session failed",
   511→			slog.String("checkpoint_id", checkpointID.String()),

   513→			slog.String("error", err.Error()),
   514→		)
   515→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to get session log: %w", err)

   563→		slog.String("session_id", sessionID),
   564→	)
   565→

   568→	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "\nTo continue this session, run:\n")

   571→	return nil
```

> AGENT

All implementation work from the plan is complete. Here's a summary of what was done:

**Changes across 7 files:**

1. **`checkpoint/checkpoint.go`** — Added `StartedAt` field to `CommittedMetadata` and `WriteCommittedOptions`
2. **`checkpoint/committed.go`** — Populated `StartedAt` from opts when writing session metadata
3. **`strategy/manual_commit_condensation.go`** — Passed `state.StartedAt` to `WriteCommittedOptions`
4. **`strategy/auto_commit.go`** — Passed `StartedAt` to both `WriteCommittedOptions` call sites
5. **`strategy/manual_commit_rewind.go`** — Replaced Claude-specific `GetClaudeProjectDir` + hardcoded `.jsonl` with agent-aware `ComputeTranscriptPath` in both `RestoreLogsOnly` and `classifySessionsForRestore`
6. **`resume.go`** — Updated `resumeSingleSession` to use `ComputeTranscriptPath` instead of hardcoded `.jsonl`

**Backward compatibility:** Empty agent types fall back to `agent.Default()`, zero `StartedAt` falls back to `CreatedAt`.

**All verification passed:**
- `mise run fmt` — clean
- `mise run lint` — 0 issues
- `mise run test` — all unit tests pass
- `mise run test:integration` — all integration tests pass

The changes are ready to be committed whenever you'd like.
